1458:    // index check below, because a removed item moves every index after it and
1459:    // the value may sit one place along.
1460:    const node = recordedNodeAt(root, record.path);
1461:    const liveArray = arrayOnPath(root, record.path);
1462:    if (!node && !liveArray) continue;
1463:    const upstreamText = readUpstreamText();
1464:    if (upstreamText === null) {
1465:      throw new Error('cannot restore the dropped set at ' + where + ': the pinned copy of the file cannot be read; ' +
1466:        "name the upstream checkout in the manifest's pin.checkout, or put the file back to the pin's bytes by hand");
1467:    }
1468:    let upstreamRoot;
1469:    try {
1470:      upstreamRoot = parseJsonSource(upstreamText);
1471:    } catch (error) {
1472:      throw new Error('cannot restore the dropped set at ' + where + ': the pinned copy of the file is not valid JSON');
1473:    }
1474:    const pinnedArray = arrayOnPath(upstreamRoot, record.path);
1475:    // An array that reads the same as the pin's holds nothing this overlay
1476:    // wrote, so the record has nothing left to take back. This is also how a
1477:    // refusal below clears once the array was put right by hand.
1478:    if (liveArray && pinnedArray && deepEqual(
1479:      JSON.parse(output.slice(liveArray.node.start, liveArray.node.end)),
1480:      JSON.parse(upstreamText.slice(pinnedArray.node.start, pinnedArray.node.end)),
1481:    )) continue;
1482:    if (!indexesMatchPin({ text: output, root }, { text: upstreamText, root: upstreamRoot },
1483:      record.path, operations, records, recordedItems)) {
1484:      const arrayPath = (liveArray || pinnedArray).path;
1485:      throw new Error('cannot restore the dropped set at ' + where +
1486:        ': its path goes through an array whose items differ from the pinned copy; put the array at ' +
1487:        (arrayPath.length > 0 ? arrayPath.join('.') : 'the top level') + ' back to the pinned copy by hand and run apply');
1488:    }
1489:    // The arrays line up, so a key that is missing is gone and not one place along.
1490:    if (!node) continue;
1491:    const pinned = recordedNodeAt(upstreamRoot, record.path);
1492:    const live = JSON.parse(output.slice(node.start, node.end));
1493:    // A live value that already equals the pin's needs no write.
1494:    if (pinned && deepEqual(live, JSON.parse(upstreamText.slice(pinned.start, pinned.end)))) continue;
1495:    const { candidates, masks } = valueLeftBySets(record, records, keptPaths);
1496:    if (!candidates.some((candidate) => deepEqual(maskedValue(live, masks), maskedValue(candidate, masks)))) {
1497:      throw new Error('dropped set at ' + where +
1498:        ' was edited after it was applied; restore the value by hand or put the set back in the entry set');
1499:    }
1500:    if (pinned) {
1501:      // The pin's own text is written back, so the file returns to upstream's bytes.
1502:      output = applyEdits(output, [{ start: node.start, end: node.end, text: upstreamText.slice(pinned.start, pinned.end) }]);
1503:      continue;
1504:    }
1505:    const parent = recordedNodeAt(root, record.path.slice(0, -1));
1506:    const index = parent && parent.type === 'object'
1507:      ? parent.members.findIndex((member) => member.key === record.path[record.path.length - 1])
1508:      : -1;
1509:    // The live value equals the recorded one here, so the key carries nothing a
1510:    // person wrote. Whether this engine or an older one added it, the pin does
1511:    // not hold it and the entry set no longer asks for it.
1512:    if (index < 0) {
1513:      throw new Error('cannot restore the dropped set at ' + where + ': the pinned copy holds no value there');
1514:    }
1515:    output = applyEdits(output, [memberRemovalEdit(parent.members, index)]);
1516:  }
1517:  return output;
1518:}
1519:
1520:/**
1521: * Return the document text with every declared entry in place, and with the
1522: * items the entry set no longer lists gone.
1523: * @param {string} text Live document text.
1524: * @param {{style?: object, operations: object[]}} spec Entry set.
1525: * @param {{canReplaceExisting: boolean, recordedItems?: object[],
1526: *   onListed?: function, recordedSets?: object[], onSet?: function,
1527: *   readUpstreamText?: () => (string|null)}} options Whether a differing entry
1528: *   may be rewritten, the items this overlay added to the file last time, where
1529: *   a listed item is reported, the sets it applied last time, where an applied
1530: *   set is reported, and how the destination reads in the pinned upstream copy.
1531: * @returns {string} The edited document text.
1532: */
1533:function applyJsonOperations(text, spec, options) {
1534:  const style = spec.style || { indent: 2, primitiveArrays: 'inline' };
1535:  const operations = withLaterKeysMerged(spec.operations);
1536:  // A dropped entry is retracted before the entry set runs, so every operation
1537:  // addresses the array the set describes rather than the one it left behind.
1538:  let output = removeDroppedItems(text, operations, options.recordedItems || [], options.readUpstreamText);
1539:  // A dropped set is taken back first for the same reason.
1540:  output = restoreDroppedSets(output, operations, options.recordedSets || [], options.readUpstreamText,
1541:    options.recordedItems || []);
1542:  // Each operation sees the document the operations before it produced, so a
1543:  // later operation can address an item an earlier one in the same set added.
1544:  for (const operation of operations) {
1545:    const edits = [];
1546:    planOperation(operation, parseJsonSource(output), output, style, edits, options);
1547:    if (edits.length > 0) output = applyEdits(output, edits);
1548:  }
1549:  if (output === text) return text;
1550:  // Guard the writer: a text edit that produced unparseable JSON must never be
1551:  // written, because the failure would surface later as a routing break.
1552:  JSON.parse(output);
1553:  return output;
1554:}
1555:
1556:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
1557:// 6. PLANS
1558://
1559:// A plan is a list of file writes and deletions for one overlay, computed
1560:// against the tree on disk. Nothing is written while planning, so status and
1561:// apply share one definition of "what this overlay should look like".
1562:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
1563:
1564:function acceptedHashFor(acceptedSha256, destination) {
1565:  if (acceptedSha256 instanceof Map) return acceptedSha256.get(destination);
1566:  if (!acceptedSha256 || typeof acceptedSha256 !== 'object') return undefined;
1567:  return acceptedSha256[destination];
1568:}
1569:
1570:/**
1571: * Plan a whole-file overlay, guarded by its pin when it has one.
1572: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
1573: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
1574: * @param {string[]} problems Collects reasons the overlay cannot be applied.
1575: * @param {Record<string, string>} [acceptedSha256] Hashes the engine last applied.
1576: * @returns {{writes: object[], deletes: string[], outputs: object[]}}
1577: */
1578:function planFileOverlay(entry, ctx, problems, acceptedSha256) {
1579:  const sourcePath = path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source);
1580:  const destinationPath = path.join(ctx.root, entry.destination);
1581:  const desired = readBufferOrNull(sourcePath);
1582:  if (!desired) {
1583:    problems.push(`canonical copy is missing: ${entry.source}`);
1584:    return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1585:  }
1586:  const output = { filePath: destinationPath, buffer: desired, mode: entry.mode, sourcePath };
1587:  const current = readBufferOrNull(destinationPath);
1588:  if (!current) {
1589:    if (entry.pinnedSha256 && acceptedHashFor(acceptedSha256, entry.destination) === undefined) {
1590:      problems.push(`upstream removed ${entry.destination}; rebase the overlay`);
1591:      return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1592:    }
1593:    // Guarded paths need a recorded applied hash before restoration after removal.
1594:    return { writes: [output], deletes: [], outputs: [output] };
1595:  }
1596:  if (Buffer.compare(current, desired) === 0) return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [output] };
1597:  if (!entry.pinnedSha256) {
1598:    return { writes: [output], deletes: [], outputs: [output] };
1599:  }
1600:  const currentSha = sha256(current);
1601:  if (currentSha === entry.pinnedSha256 || currentSha === acceptedHashFor(acceptedSha256, entry.destination)) {
1602:    return { writes: [output], deletes: [], outputs: [output] };
1603:  }
1604:  problems.push(`upstream changed ${entry.destination}; rebase the overlay`);
1605:  return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [output] };
1606:}
1607:
1608:/**
1609: * Plan a folder mirrored from its overlay source.
1610: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
1611: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
1612: * @param {string[]} problems Collects reasons the overlay cannot be applied.
1613: * @param {Record<string, string>} [acceptedSha256] Hashes the engine last applied.
1614: * @returns {{writes: object[], deletes: string[], outputs: object[]}}
1615: */
1616:function planDirectoryOverlay(entry, ctx, problems) {
1617:  const sourceDir = path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source);
1618:  const destinationDir = path.join(ctx.root, entry.destination);
1619:  if (!isDirectory(sourceDir)) {
1620:    problems.push(`canonical folder is missing: ${entry.source}`);
1621:    return { writes: [], deletes: [] };
1622:  }
1623:  const desired = walkTree(sourceDir);
1624:  const current = walkTree(destinationDir);
1625:  const writes = [];
1626:  const deletes = [];
1627:  const outputs = [];
1628:  for (const [rel, wanted] of desired) {
1629:    const destinationPath = path.join(destinationDir, rel);
1630:    const existing = current.get(rel);
1631:    if (wanted.kind === 'symlink') {
1632:      if (!existing || existing.kind !== 'symlink' || existing.target !== wanted.target) {
1633:        writes.push({ filePath: destinationPath, symlinkTarget: wanted.target });
1634:      }
1635:      continue;
1636:    }
1637:    outputs.push({
1638:      filePath: destinationPath,
1639:      buffer: wanted.buffer,
1640:      mode: wanted.mode,
1641:      sourcePath: path.join(sourceDir, rel),
1642:    });
1643:    if (!existing || existing.kind !== 'file' || Buffer.compare(existing.buffer, wanted.buffer) !== 0) {
1644:      writes.push(outputs[outputs.length - 1]);
1645:    }
1646:  }
1647:  for (const rel of current.keys()) {
1648:    if (!desired.has(rel)) {
1649:      const destinationPath = path.join(destinationDir, rel);
1650:      deletes.push(destinationPath);
1651:      if (current.get(rel).kind === 'file') {
1652:        // The overlay's copy is the whole folder, so a file it lacks is its own
1653:        // delete while that file still holds upstream's bytes.
1654:        outputs.push({
1655:          filePath: destinationPath,
1656:          buffer: null,
1657:          sourcePath: path.join(sourceDir, rel),
1658:          replacedSubtree: true,
1659:        });
1660:      }
1661:    }
1662:  }
1663:  // Deepest first, so a removed folder is empty by the time its own delete runs.
1664:  deletes.sort((left, right) => right.length - left.length);
1665:  return { writes, deletes, outputs };
1666:}
1667:
1668:/**
1669: * Plan a block appended to, or rewritten inside, an upstream file.
1670: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
1671: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
1672: * @param {string[]} problems Collects reasons the overlay cannot be applied.
1673: * @param {Record<string, string>} [acceptedSha256] Hashes the engine last applied.
1674: * @returns {{writes: object[], deletes: string[], outputs: object[]}}
1675: */
1676:function planMarkedSectionOverlay(entry, ctx, problems, acceptedSha256) {
1677:  const fragmentPath = path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.fragment);
1678:  const basePath = path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.base);
1679:  const destinationPath = path.join(ctx.root, entry.destination);
1680:  const fragment = readBufferOrNull(fragmentPath);
1681:  const base = readBufferOrNull(basePath);
1682:  if (!fragment || !base) {
1683:    problems.push(`canonical fragment or base copy is missing for ${entry.destination}`);
1684:    return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1685:  }
1686:  const current = readBufferOrNull(destinationPath);
1687:  const baseText = base.toString('utf8');
1688:  const fragmentText = fragment.toString('utf8');
1689:  const currentText = current ? current.toString('utf8') : null;
1690:  let expected;
1691:  if (currentText === null) {
1692:    if (acceptedHashFor(acceptedSha256, entry.destination) === undefined) {
1693:      problems.push(`upstream removed ${entry.destination}; rebase the overlay`);
1694:      return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1695:    }
1696:    expected = `${baseText}${fragmentText}`;
1697:  } else if (currentText.includes(entry.beginMarker)) {
1698:    const begin = currentText.indexOf(entry.beginMarker);
1699:    const endMarkerIndex = currentText.indexOf(entry.endMarker);
1700:    if (endMarkerIndex < 0) {
1701:      problems.push(`${entry.destination} has the begin marker but no end marker; rebase the overlay`);
1702:      return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1703:    }
1704:    // Our own block is rewritten in place; the separator that carries it is
1705:    // part of the fragment, so the text before the marker is kept as it is.
1706:    const head = currentText.slice(0, begin);
1707:    expected = `${head}${fragmentText.slice(fragmentText.indexOf(entry.beginMarker))}`;
1708:  } else if (currentText === baseText) {
1709:    expected = `${baseText}${fragmentText}`;
1710:  } else {
1711:    problems.push(`upstream changed ${entry.destination}; rebase the overlay`);
1712:    return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1713:  }
1714:  const output = { filePath: destinationPath, buffer: Buffer.from(expected, 'utf8'), mode: entry.mode, sourcePath: fragmentPath };
1715:  if (expected === currentText) return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [output] };
1716:  return { writes: [output], deletes: [], outputs: [output] };
1717:}
1718:
1719:/**
1720: * Plan an entry set applied in place to an upstream JSON file.
1721: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
1722: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
1723: * @param {string[]} problems Collects reasons the overlay cannot be applied.
1724: * @param {Record<string, string>} [acceptedSha256] Hashes the engine last applied.
1725: * @param {{recordedItems?: object[], recordedSets?: object[],
1726: *   upstreamBytes?: ((key: string) => Buffer|null)|null}} [options] Items this
1727: *   overlay added to the destination last time, so a dropped entry leaves the live
1728: *   file, the sets it applied there, so a dropped set returns to the pin's value,
1729: *   and the reader for the pinned upstream copy both are checked against.
1730: * @returns {{writes: object[], deletes: string[], outputs: object[],
1731: *   listedItems: object[], listedSets: object[]}}
1732: *   `listedItems` holds the items the entry set still lists and this overlay
1733: *   added, which is what a later pass may retract. `listedSets` holds the sets
1734: *   the entry set still names.
1735: */
1736:function planJsonOverlay(entry, ctx, problems, acceptedSha256, { recordedItems, recordedSets, upstreamBytes } = {}) {
1737:  const specPath = path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source);
1738:  const destinationPath = path.join(ctx.root, entry.destination);
1739:  const specBuffer = readBufferOrNull(specPath);
1740:  if (!specBuffer) {
1741:    problems.push(`entry set is missing: ${entry.source}`);
1742:    return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1743:  }
1744:  const current = readBufferOrNull(destinationPath);
1745:  if (!current) {
1746:    problems.push(`upstream removed ${entry.destination}; rebase the overlay`);
1747:    return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1748:  }
1749:  const spec = JSON.parse(specBuffer.toString('utf8'));
1750:  const currentText = current.toString('utf8');
1751:  // The recorded applied hash is the only proof that the live bytes are this
1752:  // overlay's own work. Without it a differing entry may be a hand edit.
1753:  const appliedHash = acceptedHashFor(acceptedSha256, entry.destination);
1754:  const canReplaceExisting = appliedHash !== undefined && sha256(current) === appliedHash;
1755:  // The pinned upstream copy is read only once a record is actually dropped, so a
1756:  // run that retracts nothing pays no git call for it.
1757:  let upstreamText;
1758:  const readUpstreamText = () => {
1759:    if (upstreamText !== undefined) return upstreamText;
1760:    const bytes = upstreamBytes ? upstreamBytes(relativeKey(ctx.root, destinationPath)) : null;
1761:    // Live bytes that hash to the entry's pinned hash are the pinned copy itself,
1762:    // so they stand in when no checkout can be read.
1763:    if (bytes !== null) upstreamText = bytes.toString('utf8');
1764:    else upstreamText = entry.pinnedSha256 && sha256(current) === entry.pinnedSha256 ? currentText : null;
1765:    return upstreamText;
1766:  };
1767:  let expected;
1768:  const listedItems = [];
1769:  const listedSets = [];
1770:  try {
1771:    expected = applyJsonOperations(currentText, spec, {
1772:      canReplaceExisting,
1773:      recordedItems,
1774:      recordedSets,
1775:      readUpstreamText,
1776:      onListed: (record) => listedItems.push(record),
1777:      // One record per path: a later set on the same path is the value the file
1778:      // ends with, and a key stays marked as added once either set added it. The
1779:      // record moves to the end, so the records keep the order the values were written.
1780:      onSet: (record) => {
1781:        const index = listedSets.findIndex((listed) => samePath(listed.path, record.path));
1782:        if (index < 0) {
1783:          listedSets.push(record);
1784:          return;
1785:        }
1786:        const [earlier] = listedSets.splice(index, 1);
1787:        listedSets.push({ ...record, added: earlier.added === true || record.added === true });
1788:      },
1789:    });
1790:  } catch (error) {
1791:    problems.push(`cannot apply the entry set to ${entry.destination}: ${error.message}`);
1792:    // The destination is still reported, with no desired bytes, so the conflict is
1793:    // raised against the entry set that has to be reconciled instead of vanishing
1794:    // into a hard error that names only the live file.
1795:    return {
1796:      writes: [],
1797:      deletes: [],
1798:      outputs: [{ filePath: destinationPath, buffer: null, mode: entry.mode, sourcePath: specPath }],
1799:    };
1800:  }
1801:  const output = { filePath: destinationPath, buffer: Buffer.from(expected, 'utf8'), mode: entry.mode, sourcePath: specPath };
1802:  if (expected === currentText) return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [output], listedItems, listedSets };
1803:  return { writes: [output], deletes: [], outputs: [output], listedItems, listedSets };
1804:}
1805:
1806:/**
1807: * Retained paths whose live bytes the skill-root plan keeps, because they sit
1808: * inside a replaced folder. A retained path the entry replaces directly is
1809: * written from the overlay source, so it is an overlay output like any other.
1810: * @param {object} entry A skill-root manifest entry.
1811: * @param {string} sourceDir The entry's source folder.
1812: * @returns {Set<string>} Kept paths, relative to the skill root.
1813: */
1814:function keptRetainedPaths(entry, sourceDir) {
1815:  const replacedFolders = (entry.replace || []).filter((rel) => isDirectory(path.join(sourceDir, rel)));
1816:  const kept = new Set();
1817:  for (const item of entry.retained || []) {
1818:    if (replacedFolders.some((prefix) => item.path === prefix || item.path.startsWith(prefix + '/'))) kept.add(item.path);
1819:  }
1820:  return kept;
1821:}
1822:
1823:/**
1824: * A whole skill root replaced by the project's own: the source folder wins every
1825: * path it carries, the named vendor paths are removed, and the vendor files the
1826: * framework resolves by name are restored byte for byte from their pre-apply
1827: * state. Restoring from the live tree rather than from a stored copy keeps a
1828: * newer upstream template in place instead of reverting it to the pin.
1829: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
1830: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
1831: * @param {string[]} problems Collects reasons the overlay cannot be applied.
1832: * @param {Record<string, string>} [acceptedSha256] Hashes the engine last applied.
1833: * @returns {{writes: object[], deletes: string[], outputs: object[]}}
1834: */
1835:function planSkillRootOverlay(entry, ctx, problems) {
1836:  const sourceDir = path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source);
1837:  const destinationDir = path.join(ctx.root, entry.destination);
1838:  if (!isDirectory(sourceDir)) {
1839:    problems.push(`canonical folder is missing: ${entry.source}`);
1840:    return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1841:  }
1842:  const retained = entry.retained || [];
1843:  const missingRetained = retained
1844:    .map((item) => item.path)
1845:    .filter((rel) => !isFile(path.join(destinationDir, rel)));
1846:  if (missingRetained.length > 0) {
1847:    for (const rel of missingRetained) problems.push(`retained path is missing before apply: ${entry.destination}/${rel}`);
1848:    return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1849:  }
1850:
1851:  const replacedSubtrees = new Set(entry.replace.filter((rel) => isDirectory(path.join(sourceDir, rel))));
1852:  const snapshot = new Map();
1853:  for (const item of retained) {
1854:    const isUnderReplaced = [...replacedSubtrees].some((prefix) => item.path === prefix || item.path.startsWith(`${prefix}/`));
1855:    if (!isUnderReplaced) continue;
1856:    const absolute = path.join(destinationDir, item.path);
1857:    if (isFile(absolute)) snapshot.set(item.path, fs.readFileSync(absolute));
1858:  }
1859:
1860:  const writes = [];
1861:  const deletes = [];
1862:  const desired = new Map();
1863:  for (const rel of entry.replace) {
1864:    const sourcePath = path.join(sourceDir, rel);
1865:    if (isDirectory(sourcePath)) {
1866:      for (const [childRel, item] of walkTree(sourcePath)) desired.set(path.posix.join(rel, childRel), item);
1867:    } else if (isFile(sourcePath)) {
1868:      desired.set(rel, { kind: 'file', buffer: fs.readFileSync(sourcePath), mode: fs.statSync(sourcePath).mode & 0o777 });
1869:    } else {
1870:      problems.push(`canonical path is missing: ${entry.source}/${rel}`);
1871:      return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1872:    }
1873:  }
1874:  const outputs = [];
1875:  const keptPaths = keptRetainedPaths(entry, sourceDir);
1876:  for (const [rel, wanted] of desired) {
1877:    if (wanted.kind !== 'file' || keptPaths.has(rel)) continue;
1878:    outputs.push({
1879:      filePath: path.join(destinationDir, rel),
1880:      buffer: wanted.buffer,
1881:      mode: wanted.mode,
1882:      sourcePath: path.join(sourceDir, rel),
1883:    });
1884:  }
1885:
1886:  // A retained path that sits inside a replaced subtree survives the mirror,
1887:  // so it is written back from the snapshot and never deleted in the first
1888:  // place. That keeps the second apply a no-op instead of a delete-and-restore.
1889:  for (const [rel, buffer] of snapshot) {
1890:    desired.set(rel, { kind: 'file', buffer, mode: 0o644 });
1891:  }
1892:
1893:  const current = walkTree(destinationDir);
1894:  const removedPrefixes = [...entry.remove, ...replacedSubtrees];
1895:  const matchesPrefix = (prefixes, rel) => prefixes.some((prefix) => rel === prefix || rel.startsWith(`${prefix}/`));
1896:  const isRemoved = (rel) => matchesPrefix(removedPrefixes, rel);
1897:  // A replaced subtree is the overlay's to capture into; only a path the entry
1898:  // removes without also replacing is a removal the overlay performs.
1899:  const isDeclaredRemoval = (rel) => matchesPrefix(entry.remove, rel) && !matchesPrefix(entry.replace, rel);
1900:  const isReplacedSubtree = (rel) => matchesPrefix([...replacedSubtrees], rel);
1901:
1902:  for (const [rel, wanted] of desired) {
1903:    const destinationPath = path.join(destinationDir, rel);
1904:    const existing = current.get(rel);
1905:    if (wanted.kind === 'symlink') {
1906:      if (!existing || existing.kind !== 'symlink' || existing.target !== wanted.target) {
1907:        writes.push({ filePath: destinationPath, symlinkTarget: wanted.target });
1908:      }
1909:      continue;
1910:    }
1911:    if (!existing || existing.kind !== 'file' || Buffer.compare(existing.buffer, wanted.buffer) !== 0) {
1912:      writes.push({ filePath: destinationPath, buffer: wanted.buffer, mode: wanted.mode });
1913:    }
1914:  }
1915:
1916:  for (const rel of current.keys()) {
1917:    if (desired.has(rel)) continue;
1918:    if (!isRemoved(rel)) continue;
1919:    const destinationPath = path.join(destinationDir, rel);
1920:    deletes.push(destinationPath);
1921:    if (current.get(rel).kind === 'file') {
1922:      outputs.push({
1923:        filePath: destinationPath,
1924:        buffer: null,
1925:        sourcePath: path.join(sourceDir, rel),
1926:        removal: isDeclaredRemoval(rel),
1927:        replacedSubtree: isReplacedSubtree(rel),
1928:      });
1929:    }
1930:  }
1931:  // Deepest first, so a removed folder is empty by the time its own delete runs.
1932:  deletes.sort((left, right) => right.length - left.length);
1933:
1934:  return { writes, deletes, outputs };
1935:}
1936:
1937:/**
1938: * Plan the removal of named paths from the tree.
1939: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
1940: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
1941: * @param {string[]} problems Collects reasons the overlay cannot be applied.
1942: * @param {Record<string, string>} [acceptedSha256] Hashes the engine last applied.
1943: * @returns {{writes: object[], deletes: string[], outputs: object[]}}
1944: */
1945:function planRemoveRootOverlay(entry, ctx, problems) {
1946:  const root = ctx.root;
1947:  if (!Array.isArray(entry.paths) || entry.paths.length === 0) {
1948:    problems.push('paths must be a non-empty array');
1949:    return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1950:  }
1951:
1952:  const targets = [];
1953:  const seenTargets = new Set();
1954:  for (const relativePath of entry.paths) {
1955:    try {
1956:      const target = resolveRemoveRootPath(root, relativePath);
1957:      if (seenTargets.has(target)) {
1958:        problems.push(`removal path is repeated: ${relativePath}`);
1959:        continue;
1960:      }
1961:      seenTargets.add(target);
1962:      targets.push({ relativePath, target });
1963:    } catch (error) {
1964:      problems.push(error.message);
1965:    }
1966:  }
1967:  if (problems.length > 0) return { writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
1968:
1969:  const deletions = new Set();
1970:  for (const { target } of targets) {
1971:    for (const removalPath of collectRemovalPaths(target)) deletions.add(removalPath);
1972:  }
1973:  const deletes = [...deletions].sort((left, right) => {
1974:    const leftDepth = path.relative(root, left).split(path.sep).length;
1975:    const rightDepth = path.relative(root, right).split(path.sep).length;
1976:    return rightDepth - leftDepth || right.length - left.length || left.localeCompare(right);
1977:  });
1978:  const outputs = targets.map(({ target }) => ({ filePath: target, buffer: null }));
1979:  return { writes: [], deletes, outputs };
1980:}
1981:
1982:const PLANNERS = {
1983:  file: planFileOverlay,
1984:  directory: planDirectoryOverlay,
1985:  'marked-section': planMarkedSectionOverlay,
1986:  'json-entry': planJsonOverlay,
1987:  'skill-root': planSkillRootOverlay,
1988:  'remove-root': planRemoveRootOverlay,
1989:};
1990:
1991:/**
1992: * Build a read-only plan for one manifest overlay.
1993: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
1994: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
1995: * @param {{acceptedSha256?: Map<string, string>|Record<string, string>,
1996: *   recordedItems?: object[], recordedSets?: object[],
1997: *   upstreamBytes?: ((key: string) => Buffer|null)|null}}
1998: *   [options] Hashes of what this overlay wrote last, which authorize rewriting a
1999: *   destination that changed only because the canonical copy did, the items it
2000: *   added there, which a dropped entry takes back, the sets it applied there,
2001: *   which a dropped set returns to the pin's value, and the reader for the
2002: *   pinned upstream copy both are checked against.
2003: * @returns {{entry: object, problems: string[], writes: object[], deletes: string[],
2004: *   outputs: object[], listedItems: object[], listedSets: object[]}}
2005: *   `listedItems` names the added items an entry set still lists and `listedSets`
2006: *   the sets it still names, both for the state write.
2007: */
2008:function planOverlay(entry, ctx, { acceptedSha256, recordedItems, recordedSets, upstreamBytes } = {}) {
2009:  const problems = [];
2010:  const planner = PLANNERS[entry.scope];
2011:  if (!planner) {
2012:    return { entry, problems: [`unknown scope "${entry.scope}"`], writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [], listedItems: [], listedSets: [] };
2013:  }
2014:  const plan = planner(entry, ctx, problems, acceptedSha256, { recordedItems, recordedSets, upstreamBytes });
2015:  return {
2016:    entry,
2017:    problems,
2018:    writes: plan.writes,
2019:    deletes: plan.deletes,
2020:    outputs: plan.outputs || [],
2021:    listedItems: plan.listedItems || [],
2022:    listedSets: plan.listedSets || [],
2023:  };
2024:}
2025:
2026:/**
2027: * Apply one overlay plan's writes and removals.
2028: * @param {object} plan Result from planOverlay.
2029: * @param {string} root Destination tree root.
2030: * @returns {number} Number of write and delete operations applied.
2031: */
2032:function applyOnePlan(plan, root) {
2033:  for (const deletion of plan.deletes) removePath(deletion);
2034:  for (const write of plan.writes) {
2035:    if (write.symlinkTarget !== undefined) ensureSymlink(write.filePath, write.symlinkTarget);
2036:    else writeFile(write.filePath, write.buffer, write.mode);
2037:  }
2038:  if (plan.deletes.length > 0 && plan.entry.destination) {
2039:    pruneEmptyDirs(path.join(root, plan.entry.destination));
2040:  }
2041:  return plan.writes.length + plan.deletes.length;
2042:}
2043:
2044:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2045:// 7. LAYOUT LINKS
2046://
2047:// The routing engine reads each hub through relative links that sit outside
2048:// the vendored `.skilled` tree. A fresh copy of the pin does not carry them, so
2049:// regeneration fails until they exist. The manifest names each such link; a
2050:// path that already holds something else is never replaced.
2051://
2052:// The one exception is an entry that names, in `replaces`, a single upstream
2053:// link and the target the pin ships it with. Upstream sometimes ships a link
2054:// where the project needs a folder or a different target, and a re-copy brings
2055:// that link back. The named link is removed only while it still points at the
2056:// pinned target: any other target means upstream or a person changed it, and
2057:// replacing it then would hide that change, so the entry is refused instead.
2058:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2059:
2060:/**
2061: * Decide what a layout link's `replaces` entry allows right now.
2062: * @param {string} root Destination tree root.
2063: * @param {object} link Layout link entry from the manifest.
2064: * @returns {{state: string, reason?: string, replacedPath?: string}}
2065: */
2066:function replacedLinkState(root, link) {
2067:  const replaced = link.replaces;
2068:  if (replaced === undefined) return { state: 'none' };
2069:  if (!replaced || typeof replaced.path !== 'string' || typeof replaced.target !== 'string') {
2070:    return { state: 'refused', reason: `${link.path} has a replaces entry without a path and a target` };
2071:  }
2072:  const own = path.posix.normalize(link.path);
2073:  const named = path.posix.normalize(replaced.path);
2074:  if (own !== named && !own.startsWith(named + '/')) {
2075:    return { state: 'refused', reason: `${link.path} may replace only itself or a folder above it, not ${replaced.path}` };
2076:  }
2077:  const replacedPath = path.join(root, replaced.path);
2078:  let stats = null;
2079:  try {
2080:    stats = fs.lstatSync(replacedPath);
2081:  } catch (error) {
2082:    if (!error || error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
2083:    return { state: 'none' };
2084:  }
2085:  if (stats.isSymbolicLink()) {
2086:    const current = fs.readlinkSync(replacedPath);
2087:    if (own === named && current === link.target) return { state: 'none' };
2088:    if (current === replaced.target) return { state: 'pinned', replacedPath };
2089:    return { state: 'refused', reason: `${replaced.path} links to ${current}, not the pinned ${replaced.target}; refusing to replace it` };
2090:  }
2091:  if (own !== named && stats.isDirectory()) return { state: 'none' };
2092:  return { state: 'refused', reason: `${replaced.path} is not the pinned link to ${replaced.target}; refusing to replace it` };
2093:}
2094:
2095:/**
2096: * Say whether the state records this exact target as a link the engine made.
2097: * @param {object} state Loaded applied-hash state.
2098: * @param {object} link Layout link entry from the manifest.
2099: * @param {string|null} current Target the path links to right now.
2100: * @returns {boolean} Whether the record matches what stands on disk.
2101: */
2102:function isRecordedLink(state, link, current) {
2103:  const recorded = state && state.links ? state.links[link.path] : undefined;
2104:  return typeof recorded === 'string' && recorded === current;
2105:}
2106:
2107:/**
2108: * Find the manifest's layout links that are missing or refused.
2109: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
2110: * @param {string} root Destination tree root.
2111: * @param {object} state Loaded applied-hash state; its link records are what
2112: *   tells a link the engine made from one it must refuse to touch.
2113: * @returns {{missing: object[], problems: string[]}}
2114: */
2115:function planLayoutLinks(manifest, root, state) {
2116:  const missing = [];
2117:  const problems = [];
2118:  for (const link of manifest.layoutLinks || []) {
2119:    const linkPath = path.join(root, link.path);
2120:    const replacement = replacedLinkState(root, link);
2121:    if (replacement.state === 'refused') {
2122:      problems.push(replacement.reason);
2123:      continue;
2124:    }
2125:    if (replacement.state === 'pinned') {
2126:      // The pinned link still stands, so the entry's own path would be read
2127:      // through it; it is missing by definition until the link is removed.
2128:      missing.push({ link, linkPath });
2129:      continue;
2130:    }
2131:    let stats = null;
2132:    try {
2133:      stats = fs.lstatSync(linkPath);
2134:    } catch (error) {
2135:      if (!error || error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
2136:    }
2137:    let current = null;
2138:    if (stats && stats.isSymbolicLink()) current = fs.readlinkSync(linkPath);
2139:    if (!stats) {
2140:      missing.push({ link, linkPath });
2141:    } else if (current !== null && current !== link.target && isRecordedLink(state, link, current)) {
2142:      // The manifest moved this entry's target after an earlier apply, and the
2143:      // state records the link standing here as the engine's own: the link is
2144:      // retargeted rather than reported as a stranger occupying the path.
2145:      missing.push({ link, linkPath, isRetarget: true });
2146:    } else if (current !== link.target) {
2147:      problems.push(`${link.path} exists but is not the link to ${link.target}`);
2148:    }
2149:  }
2150:  return { missing, problems };
2151:}
2152:
2153:/**
2154: * Create one layout link, first removing the upstream link it replaces.
2155: * The replaced link is checked again here, because another entry may already
2156: * have removed it, and a link that changed since planning must not be deleted.
2157: * @param {string} root Destination tree root.
2158: * @param {object} link Layout link entry from the manifest.
2159: * @param {boolean} [isRetarget] Whether the state records the link standing at
2160: *   this path as the engine's own, which permits pointing it at a new target.
2161: */
2162:function applyLayoutLink(root, link, isRetarget) {
2163:  const replacement = replacedLinkState(root, link);
2164:  if (replacement.state === 'refused') throw new Error(replacement.reason);
2165:  if (replacement.state === 'pinned') fs.unlinkSync(replacement.replacedPath);
2166:  if (isRetarget === true) {
2167:    // The link is removed only while it is still a link: a path that became a
2168:    // real file or folder since planning is someone else's work, and unlinking
2169:    // it would delete that work.
2170:    const linkPath = path.join(root, link.path);
2171:    let stats = null;
2172:    try {
2173:      stats = fs.lstatSync(linkPath);
2174:    } catch (error) {
2175:      if (!error || error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
2176:    }
2177:    if (stats && !stats.isSymbolicLink()) {
2178:      throw new Error(link.path + ' is no longer the link this engine made; refusing to replace it');
2179:    }
2180:    if (stats) fs.unlinkSync(linkPath);
2181:  }
2182:  ensureSymlink(path.join(root, link.path), link.target);
2183:}
2184:
2185:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2186:// 8. REGENERATION AND VERIFICATION
2187://
2188:// Routing projections are generated from the files above, so they are never
2189:// stored as overlays: storing them would make the copy the source of truth for
2190:// data a generator owns. They are rebuilt after the overlays land, and checked
2191:// through the generators' own read-only modes.
2192:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2193:
2194:function runCommand(root, argv) {
2195:  const [bin, ...args] = argv;
2196:  const executable = bin === 'node' ? process.execPath : bin;
2197:  return spawnSync(executable, args, { cwd: root, encoding: 'utf8' });
2198:}
2199:
2200:/**
2201: * Run the manifest's read-only generator checks.
2202: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
2203: * @param {string} root Destination tree root.
2204: * @returns {string[]} One problem per failed check.
2205: */
2206:function verifyGenerated(manifest, root) {
2207:  const problems = [];
2208:  for (const check of manifest.verifyGenerated || []) {
2209:    const result = runCommand(root, check.argv);
2210:    const output = `${result.stdout || ''}${result.stderr || ''}`.trim();
2211:    if (check.expect === 'fresh-true' || check.expect === 'zero-changed') {
2212:      let parsed = null;
2213:      try {
2214:        parsed = JSON.parse(result.stdout);
2215:      } catch {
2216:        parsed = null;
2217:      }
2218:      if (check.expect === 'fresh-true' && (result.status !== 0 || !parsed || parsed.fresh !== true)) {
2219:        problems.push(`${check.id}: freshness is not fresh (${output.split('\n').slice(-1)[0] || 'no output'})`);
2220:      }
2221:      if (check.expect === 'zero-changed' && (result.status !== 0 || !parsed || parsed.changed !== 0)) {
2222:        problems.push(`${check.id}: derived content would change`);
2223:      }
2224:      continue;
2225:    }
2226:    if (result.status !== 0) {
2227:      problems.push(`${check.id}: check failed (${output.split('\n').slice(-1)[0] || 'no output'})`);
2228:    }
2229:  }
2230:  return problems;
2231:}
2232:
2233:function snapshotOutputs(manifest, root) {
2234:  const snapshot = new Map();
2235:  for (const step of manifest.regenerate || []) {
2236:    for (const output of step.outputs || []) {
2237:      const buffer = readBufferOrNull(path.join(root, output));
2238:      snapshot.set(output, buffer ? sha256(buffer) : null);
2239:    }
2240:  }
2241:  return snapshot;
2242:}
2243:
2244:/**
2245: * Run the manifest regeneration steps and report failed steps and changed outputs.
2246: * Each watched path is hashed around every step, so a file an overlay owns that a
2247: * step rewrote is traced to that step whether or not the step lists it under
2248: * `outputs`. Once the steps have run, no other record says which one wrote it.
2249: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
2250: * @param {string} root Destination tree root.
2251: * @param {string[]} [watched] Tree-relative paths to trace, such as overlay destinations.
2252: * @returns {{results: object[], failed: object[], changedOutputs: string[],
2253: *   rewrittenBy: Map<string, string[]>}} `rewrittenBy` maps each watched path a
2254: *   step changed to the ids of the steps that changed it, in run order.
2255: */
2256:function regenerate(manifest, root, watched = []) {
2257:  const before = snapshotOutputs(manifest, root);
2258:  const digestOf = (key) => {
2259:    const buffer = readBufferOrNull(path.join(root, key));
2260:    return buffer ? sha256(buffer) : null;
2261:  };
2262:  let digests = new Map(watched.map((key) => [key, digestOf(key)]));
2263:  const rewrittenBy = new Map();
2264:  const results = [];
2265:  for (const step of manifest.regenerate || []) {
2266:    results.push({ step, result: runCommand(root, step.argv) });
2267:    const next = new Map(watched.map((key) => [key, digestOf(key)]));
2268:    for (const [key, digest] of next) {
2269:      if (digests.get(key) === digest) continue;
2270:      if (!rewrittenBy.has(key)) rewrittenBy.set(key, []);
2271:      rewrittenBy.get(key).push(step.id);
2272:    }
2273:    digests = next;
2274:  }
2275:  const failed = results.filter(({ result }) => result.status !== 0);
2276:  const after = snapshotOutputs(manifest, root);
2277:  const changedOutputs = [];
2278:  for (const [output, digest] of after) {
2279:    if (before.get(output) !== digest) changedOutputs.push(output);
2280:  }
2281:  return { results, failed, changedOutputs, rewrittenBy };
2282:}
2283:
2284:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2285:// 9. PROTECTED PATHS
2286:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2287:
2288:// A `.env` file is never read, even when a manifest entry names it. A
2289:// token-named path is read only when a manifest entry names that exact path:
2290:// met while walking a directory or skill-root entry it is refused, because a
2291:// walk would otherwise read any credential file that sits beside an overlay.
2292:const EXACT_CAPTURE_SCOPES = new Set(['file', 'marked-section']);
2293:const NO_EXACT_PATHS = new Set();
2294:
2295:function isEnvName(filePath) {
2296:  return path.basename(filePath).toLowerCase().startsWith('.env');
2297:}
2298:
2299:function isTokenName(filePath) {
2300:  return path.basename(filePath).toLowerCase().includes('token');
2301:}
2302:
2303:/**
2304: * Collect every absolute path a manifest entry names exactly.
2305: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
2306: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
2307: * @returns {Set<string>} Named sources, bases, fragments, destinations and removal paths.
2308: */
2309:function exactManifestPaths(ctx, manifest) {
2310:  const exact = new Set();
2311:  for (const entry of manifest.overlays) {
2312:    for (const key of ['source', 'base', 'fragment']) {
2313:      if (typeof entry[key] === 'string') exact.add(path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry[key]));
2314:    }
2315:    if (typeof entry.destination === 'string') exact.add(path.resolve(ctx.root, entry.destination));
2316:    for (const relativePath of Array.isArray(entry.paths) ? entry.paths : []) {
2317:      if (typeof relativePath === 'string') exact.add(path.resolve(ctx.root, relativePath));
2318:    }
2319:    for (const relativePath of protectedPathsFor(entry)) {
2320:      exact.add(path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source, relativePath));
2321:      exact.add(path.resolve(ctx.root, entry.destination, relativePath));
2322:    }
2323:  }
2324:  return exact;
2325:}
2326:
2327:/**
2328: * Read the token-named paths a directory or skill-root entry lets its walk
2329: * read. Each value names one path relative to the entry, so a walk still
2330: * refuses every token-named file the entry does not list by exact path.
2331: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
2332: * @returns {string[]} The entry's validated relative paths.
2333: * @throws {Error} When a value is not a relative path inside the entry or names a .env file.
2334: */
2335:function protectedPathsFor(entry) {
2336:  if (entry.protectedPaths === undefined) return [];
2337:  if (!(entry.scope === 'directory' || entry.scope === 'skill-root') || !Array.isArray(entry.protectedPaths)) {
2338:    throw new Error(entry.id + ': protectedPaths is a list, allowed only on a directory or skill-root entry');
2339:  }
2340:  return entry.protectedPaths.map((value) => {
2341:    const normalized = typeof value === 'string' ? path.posix.normalize(value) : '';
2342:    const isInside = normalized !== '' && normalized !== '.' && !path.isAbsolute(value) &&
2343:      normalized !== '..' && !normalized.startsWith('../');
2344:    if (!isInside) {
2345:      throw new Error(entry.id + ': protectedPaths value is not a path inside the entry: ' + String(value));
2346:    }
2347:    if (isEnvName(normalized)) {
2348:      throw new Error(entry.id + ': protectedPaths value names a .env file, which is never read: ' + normalized);
2349:    }
2350:    return normalized;
2351:  });
2352:}
2353:
2354:/**
2355: * Whether the engine must refuse to read a path because its name marks a secret.
2356: * @param {string} filePath Path to test.
2357: * @param {Set<string>} [exact] Absolute paths a manifest entry names exactly.
2358: * @returns {boolean} True when the path is refused.
2359: */
2360:function isProtectedReadPath(filePath, exact = NO_EXACT_PATHS) {
2361:  if (isEnvName(filePath)) return true;
2362:  return isTokenName(filePath) && !exact.has(path.resolve(filePath));
2363:}
2364:
2365:/**
2366: * Whether a capture record's destination is one its entry lists in protectedPaths.
2367: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
2368: * @param {{key: string, entry?: object}} record Capture record.
2369: * @returns {boolean} True when the entry names this destination exactly.
2370: */
2371:function isListedProtectedPath(ctx, record) {
2372:  if (!record.entry || typeof record.entry.destination !== 'string') return false;
2373:  const destination = path.resolve(ctx.root, record.entry.destination);
2374:  const relativePath = toPosix(path.relative(destination, path.resolve(ctx.root, record.key)));
2375:  return protectedPathsFor(record.entry).includes(relativePath);
2376:}
2377:
2378:/**
2379: * Whether a capture must refuse to copy a live file into the overlay folder.
2380: * A file or marked-section entry names its destination exactly, and a
2381: * directory or skill-root entry names one through protectedPaths, so only
2382: * those may capture a token-named path.
2383: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
2384: * @param {{key: string, sourcePath: string, entry?: object}} record Capture record.
2385: * @param {string} inputPath Path as the caller named it.
2386: * @returns {boolean} True when the capture is refused.
2387: */
2388:function isCaptureRestricted(ctx, record, inputPath) {
2389:  const isExactRecord = Boolean(record.entry && EXACT_CAPTURE_SCOPES.has(record.entry.scope)) ||
2390:    isListedProtectedPath(ctx, record);
2391:  const paths = [inputPath, record.key, sourceRelative(ctx, record.sourcePath)];
2392:  return paths.some((value) => {
2393:    if (isEnvName(value)) return true;
2394:    return value.toLowerCase().includes('token') && !isExactRecord;
2395:  });
2396:}
2397:
2398:/**
2399: * Name a protected path and the one change that lets an overlay own it.
2400: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
2401: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
2402: * @param {string} filePath Absolute path that was refused.
2403: * @param {string} shownPath The path as the report prints it.
2404: * @returns {string} One problem line for the entry.
2405: */
2406:function protectedPathProblem(ctx, entry, filePath, shownPath) {
2407:  const problem = 'refusing to inspect a protected path: ' + shownPath;
2408:  if (isEnvName(filePath)) return problem + '; a .env file is never read, so move it out of the overlay';
2409:  if (entry.scope !== 'directory' && entry.scope !== 'skill-root') return problem;
2410:  // protectedPaths names a path relative to the entry, the same on both sides.
2411:  const entryRoots = [path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source), path.resolve(ctx.root, entry.destination)];
2412:  const entryRoot = entryRoots.find((candidate) => isWithin(candidate, filePath));
2413:  if (!entryRoot) return problem;
2414:  return problem + `; if the overlay owns it, add "${toPosix(path.relative(entryRoot, filePath))}" to this entry's protectedPaths`;
2415:}
2416:
2417:/**
2418: * Refuse a manifest whose paths escape their roots, and find every entry that
2419: * would read a secret. A path escape means the manifest itself is wrong, so it
2420: * stops the run. A protected path blocks only its own entry: the report still
2421: * covers every other overlay, and apply still writes nothing while one is blocked.
2422: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
2423: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
2424: * @returns {{exact: Set<string>, refused: Map<string, string>}} The exact
2425: *   manifest paths, for the per-output check, and one problem per refused entry id.
2426: */
2427:function assertSafeToPlan(ctx, manifest) {
2428:  const root = ctx.root;
2429:  const exact = exactManifestPaths(ctx, manifest);
2430:  const refused = new Map();
2431:  for (const entry of manifest.overlays) {
2432:    const candidates = [];
2433:    for (const key of ['source', 'base', 'fragment', 'destination']) {
2434:      if (!entry[key]) continue;
2435:      const base = key === 'destination' ? root : ctx.overlayRoot;
2436:      const absolute = path.resolve(base, entry[key]);
2437:      if (!isWithin(base, absolute)) {
2438:        throw new Error(entry.id + ': path escapes its root: ' + entry[key]);
2439:      }
2440:      candidates.push(absolute);
2441:    }
2442:    if (entry.scope === 'remove-root' && Array.isArray(entry.paths)) {
2443:      // A removal path the planner rejects is reported as a blocked overlay, the
2444:      // way a check reports it, rather than as an error that hides the others.
2445:      for (const relativePath of entry.paths) {
2446:        try {
2447:          candidates.push(resolveRemoveRootPath(root, relativePath));
2448:        } catch {
2449:          continue;
2450:        }
2451:      }
2452:    }
2453:    const named = candidates.find((candidate) => isProtectedReadPath(candidate, exact));
2454:    if (named) {
2455:      refused.set(entry.id, protectedPathProblem(ctx, entry, named, toPosix(path.relative(ctx.overlayRoot, named))));
2456:      continue;
2457:    }
2458:    if (entry.scope === 'directory' || entry.scope === 'skill-root') {
2459:      const sourceDirectory = path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source);
2460:      const destinationDirectory = path.resolve(root, entry.destination);
2461:      for (const filePath of [...listTreePaths(sourceDirectory), ...listTreePaths(destinationDirectory)]) {
2462:        if (isProtectedReadPath(filePath, exact)) {
2463:          refused.set(entry.id, protectedPathProblem(ctx, entry, filePath, toPosix(path.relative(root, filePath))));
2464:          break;
2465:        }
2466:      }
2467:    }
2468:  }
2469:  return { exact, refused };
2470:}
2471:
2472:/**
2473: * Stand in for an entry the engine must not read: no outputs and no writes, so
2474: * nothing classifies, applies or captures through it, and one problem the check
2475: * and apply both report.
2476: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
2477: * @param {string} problem Why the entry was refused.
2478: * @returns {object} A plan shaped like planOverlay's result.
2479: */
2480:function refusedPlan(entry, problem) {
2481:  return { entry, problems: [problem], writes: [], deletes: [], outputs: [] };
2482:}
2483:
2484:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2485:// 10. CLASSIFICATION
2486://
2487:// Each destination is classified against three byte sets: the live file, the
2488:// bytes the overlay would write now, and the hash recorded the last time the
2489:// engine applied it. That is what tells an overlay edit (apply it) from a live
2490:// edit (capture it) from a conflict (a person resolves it).
2491:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2492:
2493:function buffersEqual(left, right) {
2494:  if (left === null || right === null) return left === right;
2495:  return Buffer.compare(left, right) === 0;
2496:}
2497:
2498:function hashMatches(buffer, hash) {
2499:  return buffer !== null && hash !== undefined && sha256(buffer) === hash;
2500:}
2501:
2502:function planWouldWrite(plan, filePath) {
2503:  return plan.writes.some((write) => path.resolve(write.filePath) === path.resolve(filePath)) ||
2504:    plan.deletes.some((deletion) => path.resolve(deletion) === path.resolve(filePath));
2505:}
2506:
2507:/**
2508: * Whether an entry says its overlay was rebased onto the commit now pinned.
2509: * A folder overlay carries no hash per file, so its owner names the one
2510: * upstream commit the whole folder was read against. Pins are stored short or
2511: * full, so either may be a prefix of the other.
2512: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
2513: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
2514: * @returns {boolean}
2515: */
2516:function isRebasedOntoPin(entry, manifest) {
2517:  const rebased = typeof entry.rebasedOnto === 'string' ? entry.rebasedOnto.toLowerCase() : '';
2518:  const pinned = manifest.pin && typeof manifest.pin.commit === 'string' ? manifest.pin.commit.toLowerCase() : '';
2519:  if (rebased.length < 7 || pinned.length < 7) return false;
2520:  return rebased.startsWith(pinned) || pinned.startsWith(rebased);
2521:}
2522:
2523:/**
2524: * Name the sync state of one destination.
2525: * @param {{output: object, plan: object, key: string, live: Buffer|null, state: object,
2526: *   manifest: object, upstreamBytes: ((key: string) => Buffer|null)|null}} input
2527: * @returns {string} One of STATUS_NAMES.
2528: */
2529:function classifyDestination({ output, plan, key, live, state, manifest, upstreamBytes }) {
2530:  const desired = output.buffer;
2531:  const hasState = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.files, key);
2532:  const appliedHash = hasState ? state.files[key] : undefined;
2533:  if (buffersEqual(live, desired)) return 'in-sync';
2534:  // A path the entry declares removed is the overlay's own deletion, and apply is
2535:  // the only way to carry it out, so a live file there is pending work, never a
2536:  // person's edit to capture.
2537:  if (output.removal) return 'overlay-edited';
2538:
2539:  if (!hasState) {
2540:    if (desired === null && live !== null) {
2541:      // A replaced folder is the overlay's whole. Its delete is the overlay's
2542:      // own work when the live bytes are the upstream file's at the pin this
2543:      // run compares against. Any other bytes belong to a person and stay a
2544:      // capture.
2545:      if (output.replacedSubtree && upstreamBytes && buffersEqual(live, upstreamBytes(key))) {
2546:        return 'overlay-edited';
2547:      }
2548:      return 'live-edited';
2549:    }
2550:    return planWouldWrite(plan, output.filePath) ? 'overlay-edited' : 'both-changed';
2551:  }
2552:
2553:  const desiredHash = desired === null ? null : sha256(desired);
2554:  // An overlay names the upstream bytes it was written against. A destination
2555:  // holding exactly those bytes, as it does right after a re-copy, is waiting for
2556:  // the overlay, so a moved pin alone does not make it an upstream move.
2557:  if (plan.entry.pinnedSha256 && hashMatches(live, plan.entry.pinnedSha256)) return 'overlay-edited';
2558:  // The same holds for a folder overlay rebased onto the pinned commit: a
2559:  // destination holding upstream's bytes at that commit is what the copy left,
2560:  // so it waits for the overlay too.
2561:  if (isRebasedOntoPin(plan.entry, manifest) && upstreamBytes && live !== null && buffersEqual(live, upstreamBytes(key))) {
2562:    return 'overlay-edited';
2563:  }
2564:  if (state.exists && state.pin !== manifest.pin.commit &&
2565:      !hashMatches(live, appliedHash) && !buffersEqual(live, desired)) {
2566:    return 'upstream-moved';
2567:  }
2568:  if (live === null || hashMatches(live, appliedHash) ||
2569:      (plan.entry.pinnedSha256 && hashMatches(live, plan.entry.pinnedSha256))) {
2570:    return 'overlay-edited';
2571:  }
2572:  if (desiredHash === appliedHash && !hashMatches(live, appliedHash)) return 'live-edited';
2573:  return 'both-changed';
2574:}
2575:
2576:// A layout link the manifest names is part of the delivered tree, not of the pin,
2577:// so a tree that lost one cannot be repaired by copying anything: the report has
2578:// to carry it as outstanding work or the link stays missing silently.
2579:function layoutLinkRecords(layout, root) {
2580:  return layout.missing.map(({ link, linkPath, isRetarget }) => ({
2581:    state: 'overlay-edited',
2582:    key: relativeKey(root, linkPath),
2583:    target: link.target,
2584:    link,
2585:    isRetarget: isRetarget === true,
2586:    layout: true,
2587:  }));
2588:}
2589:
2590:/**
2591: * Plan every overlay against the accepted hashes and classify each destination.
2592: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
2593: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
2594: * @param {object} state Loaded applied-hash state.
2595: * @param {((key: string) => Buffer|null)|null} [upstreamBytes] Reads a path's
2596: *   bytes at the pin this run compares against; defaults to the manifest pin.
2597: * @returns {{plans: object[], records: object[], blocked: object[], layout: object}}
2598: *   `blocked` holds plans whose problems left no destination to classify.
2599: */
2600:function analyze(ctx, manifest, state, upstreamBytes = pinnedUpstreamBytes(manifest)) {
2601:  const root = ctx.root;
2602:  const { exact, refused } = assertSafeToPlan(ctx, manifest);
2603:  const plans = manifest.overlays.map((entry) => (refused.has(entry.id)
2604:    ? refusedPlan(entry, refused.get(entry.id))
2605:    : planOverlay(entry, ctx, {
2606:      acceptedSha256: state.files,
2607:      recordedItems: (state.items || {})[entry.destination] || [],
2608:      recordedSets: (state.sets || {})[entry.destination] || [],
2609:      upstreamBytes,
2610:    })));
2611:  const records = [];
2612:  const blocked = [];
2613:  const seen = new Set();
2614:
2615:  for (let index = 0; index < plans.length; index += 1) {
2616:    const plan = plans[index];
2617:    // A planner can produce an output the pre-plan walk never saw, such as a
2618:    // generated name. That output is never classified: the whole entry is
2619:    // swapped for a refused plan, so apply has nothing of it to write.
2620:    const protectedPath = plan.outputs
2621:      .flatMap((output) => [output.filePath, output.sourcePath])
2622:      .find((filePath) => filePath && isProtectedReadPath(filePath, exact));
2623:    if (protectedPath) {
2624:      plans[index] = refusedPlan(plan.entry, protectedPathProblem(ctx, plan.entry, protectedPath,
2625:        toPosix(path.relative(root, protectedPath))));
2626:      blocked.push(plans[index]);
2627:      continue;
2628:    }
2629:    if (plan.problems.length > 0 && plan.outputs.length === 0) {
2630:      blocked.push(plan);
2631:      continue;
2632:    }
2633:    for (const output of plan.outputs) {
2634:      const key = relativeKey(root, output.filePath);
2635:      if (seen.has(key)) throw new Error('multiple overlays own the same destination: ' + key);
2636:      seen.add(key);
2637:      let stateName = plan.entry.scope === 'remove-root'
2638:        ? (pathExists(output.filePath) ? 'overlay-edited' : 'in-sync')
2639:        : classifyDestination({
2640:          output,
2641:          plan,
2642:          key,
2643:          live: readBufferOrNull(output.filePath),
2644:          state,
2645:          manifest,
2646:          upstreamBytes,
2647:        });
2648:      if (plan.problems.length > 0 && stateName === 'overlay-edited') stateName = 'both-changed';
2649:      // An entry set the engine cannot apply to a file nobody edited since the
2650:      // last apply, or since the pin, is a problem in the entry set alone. Only
2651:      // the live copy moving as well makes it both-changed.
2652:      if (stateName === 'both-changed' && plan.entry.scope === 'json-entry' && output.buffer === null &&
2653:          plan.problems.length > 0) {
2654:        const live = readBufferOrNull(output.filePath);
2655:        if (hashMatches(live, acceptedHashFor(state.files, key)) || hashMatches(live, plan.entry.pinnedSha256)) {
2656:          stateName = 'cannot-apply';
2657:        }
2658:      }
2659:      records.push({
2660:        state: stateName,
2661:        key,
2662:        output,
2663:        plan,
2664:        source: plan.entry.scope === 'remove-root' ? null : sourceRelative(ctx, output.sourcePath),
2665:        removal: plan.entry.scope === 'remove-root',
2666:      });
2667:    }
2668:  }
2669:  const layout = planLayoutLinks(manifest, root, state);
2670:  for (const record of layoutLinkRecords(layout, root)) records.push(record);
2671:  return { plans, records, blocked, layout };
2672:}
2673:
2674:function fixFor(ctx, record) {
2675:  if (record.state === 'overlay-edited') return ENGINE_COMMAND + ' apply';
2676:  if (record.state === 'live-edited') return ENGINE_COMMAND + ' apply --capture ' + record.key;
2677:  if (record.state === 'cannot-apply') {
2678:    const reasons = record.plan.problems.map((problem) => problem.replace(/^cannot apply the entry set to [^:]+: /, ''));
2679:    return reasons.join('; ') + '; change the entry set or the live file, then run ' + ENGINE_COMMAND + ' status';
2680:  }
2681:  return 'resolve ' + ctx.overlayLabel + '/' + record.source + ' and ' + record.key +
2682:    ', then run ' + ENGINE_COMMAND + ' status';
2683:}
2684:
2685:function stateLine(ctx, record) {
2686:  if (record.layout) return record.state + ' ' + record.key + '  (layout link)';
2687:  if (record.removal) return record.state + ' ' + record.key + '  (remove-root row: ' + record.plan.entry.id + ')';
2688:  return record.state + ' ' + record.key + '  (overlay: ' + ctx.overlayLabel + '/' + record.source + ')';
2689:}
2690:
2691:function problemLine(ctx, record) {
2692:  return stateLine(ctx, record) + '  fix: ' + fixFor(ctx, record);
2693:}
2694:
2695:// A file a regenerate step rewrote after the overlay was applied would read as an
2696:// upstream move or a live edit, which sends the reader to a diff that does not
2697:// hold the change. The line names the step instead.
2698:function regeneratedLine(ctx, record, stepIds) {
2699:  const source = ctx.overlayLabel + '/' + record.source;
2700:  return 'regenerated ' + record.key + '  (overlay: ' + source + ')  step: ' + stepIds.join(', ') +
2701:    '  fix: carry the step\'s change into ' + source + ', or change the step so it leaves ' + record.key +
2702:    ' alone, then run ' + ENGINE_COMMAND + ' apply';
2703:}
2704:
2705:function printProblems(ctx, records) {
2706:  for (const record of records) {
2707:    if (record.state !== 'in-sync') console.log(problemLine(ctx, record));
2708:  }
2709:}
2710:
2711:function stateCounts(records) {
2712:  const counts = Object.fromEntries(STATUS_NAMES.map((name) => [name, 0]));
2713:  for (const record of records) counts[record.state] += 1;
2714:  return counts;
2715:}
2716:
2717:function stateSummary(records) {
2718:  const counts = stateCounts(records);
2719:  return STATUS_NAMES.map((name) => name + '=' + counts[name]).join(' ');
2720:}
2721:
2722:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2723:// 11. LOCK
2724://
2725:// Every command that writes the tree or the overlay folder takes the lock
2726:// itself, so no wrapper is needed to keep two runs apart. A lock whose writer
2727:// has ended on this host is taken over, because it is only leftover state; a
2728:// lock that may still have a live owner is refused, and a person removes it
2729:// once they know that run has ended.
2730:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2731:
2732:/**
2733: * Tell whether the run that wrote an owner line has ended.
2734: * A lock left behind by a killed run blocks every later run until someone
2735: * deletes the file by hand, so the recorded process id is probed with signal 0,
2736: * which asks the kernel whether that process exists without touching it. Only a
2737: * missing process on this host counts: another host's ids say nothing here, and
2738: * any other error (a process owned by another user answers EPERM) says the
2739: * process is there.
2740: * A line that carries a process id and no `host=` token was written before the
2741: * engine recorded the host, and that engine only ever ran on the machine it was
2742: * installed on, so its id is probed here too. On a folder shared between
2743: * machines that can take the lock from a live run elsewhere, but refusing the
2744: * line forever would block every upgrade behind a hand delete.
2745: * @param {string} line Owner line read from the lock file.
2746: * @returns {boolean} True only when the writer is known to be gone.
2747: */
2748:function isLockOwnerGone(line) {
2749:  const pid = / pid=(\d+) /.exec(line);
2750:  const host = / host=(\S+) /.exec(line);
2751:  if (!pid) return false;
2752:  if (host && host[1] !== os.hostname()) return false;
2753:  try {
2754:    process.kill(Number(pid[1]), 0);
2755:  } catch (error) {
2756:    return Boolean(error) && error.code === 'ESRCH';
2757:  }
2758:  return false;
2759:}
2760:
2761:/**
2762: * Delete a leftover file only when it is still the file that was judged stale.
2763: * Deleting by the shared path would also delete whatever another run wrote
2764: * there after the judgement. The file is renamed aside first: a rename is one
2765: * step, so only one run can take a given file. A file that turns out to be
2766: * another run's is put back, unless a newer one already stands in its place.
2767: * @param {string} filePath The shared path of the leftover.
2768: * @param {function(string): boolean} isJudgedStale Given the set-aside path,
2769: *   says whether that file is the one judged stale.
2770: * @returns {boolean} True when this run deleted the leftover.
2771: */
2772:function removeLeftover(filePath, isJudgedStale) {
2773:  const claimPath = `${filePath}.${process.pid}.${crypto.randomBytes(4).toString('hex')}.claim`;
2774:  try {
2775:    fs.renameSync(filePath, claimPath);
2776:  } catch (error) {
2777:    if (error && error.code === 'ENOENT') return false;
2778:    throw error;
2779:  }
2780:  const isStale = isJudgedStale(claimPath);
2781:  if (!isStale) {
2782:    try {
2783:      fs.linkSync(claimPath, filePath);
2784:    } catch (linkError) {
2785:      if (!linkError || linkError.code !== 'EEXIST') throw linkError;
2786:    }
2787:  }
2788:  fs.unlinkSync(claimPath);
2789:  return isStale;
2790:}
2791:
2792:/**
2793: * Try to take the overlay lock.
2794: * A lock whose writer has ended is removed and taken over, with one note saying
2795: * whose lock it was; every other held lock is left alone and reported.
2796: * Two runs can find the same leftover, and if each removed it the later removal
2797: * would delete the lock the other run has just written, leaving both holding it.
2798: * The removal therefore happens behind a `<lock>.takeover` marker: the run that
2799: * creates it re-reads the lock and creates the new lock before the marker goes.
2800: * A marker older than the timeout is what a run killed mid-takeover leaves, so
2801: * it is cleared rather than blocking every later run for good. Neither leftover
2802: * is deleted by its shared path: each is set aside first and deleted only when
2803: * it is the file that was judged stale (see removeLeftover).
2804: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
2805: * @param {string} owner Who takes it, written at the start of the owner line.
2806: * @returns {{isHeld: boolean, lockPath: string, ownerLine: string, remedy?: string, release?: function}}
2807: *   A refusal carries `remedy`: the sentence telling the operator what to do
2808: *   about the thing that actually blocked the run.
2809: */
2810:function acquireLock(ctx, owner) {
2811:  const ownerLine = `${owner} pid=${process.pid} host=${os.hostname()} ${new Date().toISOString()}`;
2812:  const takeoverPath = ctx.lockPath + LOCK_TAKEOVER_SUFFIX;
2813:
2814:  /**
2815:   * Report the lock as held by someone else.
2816:   * @param {string} otherLine The line the lock file holds, or a sentence saying
2817:   *   why no takeover could be made.
2818:   * @param {string} [remedy] What the operator has to do next; defaults to
2819:   *   removing the lock file by hand, which is wrong when the lock is not what
2820:   *   blocked the run.
2821:   * @returns {{isHeld: boolean, lockPath: string, ownerLine: string, remedy: string}}
2822:   */
2823:  function refusal(otherLine, remedy) {
2824:    return {
2825:      isHeld: false,
2826:      lockPath: ctx.lockPath,
2827:      ownerLine: otherLine,
2828:      remedy: remedy === undefined ? 'remove ' + ctx.lockPath + ' by hand once that run has ended' : remedy,
2829:    };
2830:  }
2831:
2832:  /**
2833:   * Create the takeover marker, clearing one a killed run left behind.
2834:   * @returns {number|null} The marker's descriptor, or null while another live
2835:   *   run is taking the lock over.
2836:   */
2837:  function reserveTakeover() {
2838:    try {
2839:      return fs.openSync(takeoverPath, 'wx');
2840:    } catch (error) {
2841:      if (!error || error.code !== 'EEXIST') throw error;
2842:    }
2843:    let stats;
2844:    try {
2845:      stats = fs.statSync(takeoverPath);
2846:    } catch (error) {
2847:      if (error && error.code === 'ENOENT') return null;
2848:      throw error;
2849:    }
2850:    // No live run can still be behind a marker this old, so it is a leftover
2851:    // that would otherwise block every takeover from here on.
2852:    if (Date.now() - stats.mtimeMs <= LOCK_TAKEOVER_STALE_MS) return null;
2853:    // Two runs can judge the same marker stale at once. The one that comes
2854:    // second must not delete the fresh marker the first has just written, so
2855:    // only the file with the inode and timestamp judged above is removed.
2856:    const isCleared = removeLeftover(takeoverPath, (claimPath) => {
2857:      const claimed = fs.statSync(claimPath);
2858:      return claimed.ino === stats.ino && claimed.mtimeMs === stats.mtimeMs;
2859:    });
2860:    if (!isCleared) return null;
2861:    try {
2862:      return fs.openSync(takeoverPath, 'wx');
2863:    } catch (retryError) {
2864:      if (!retryError || retryError.code !== 'EEXIST') throw retryError;
2865:      return null;
2866:    }
2867:  }
2868:
2869:  let fd;
2870:  try {
2871:    fd = fs.openSync(ctx.lockPath, 'wx');
2872:  } catch (error) {
2873:    if (!error || error.code !== 'EEXIST') throw error;
2874:    const current = readBufferOrNull(ctx.lockPath);
2875:    const currentLine = current ? current.toString('utf8').trim() : '(empty lock file)';
2876:    if (!current || !isLockOwnerGone(currentLine)) return refusal(currentLine);
2877:    const takeoverFd = reserveTakeover();
2878:    if (takeoverFd === null) {
2879:      // The lock is not what blocked this run, so the take-over marker is not the
2880:      // operator's to delete: it is cleared by itself once it is old enough.
2881:      return refusal(
2882:        'another run is taking the overlay lock over: ' + takeoverPath,
2883:        'run it again in a minute: a takeover marker that old is cleared'
2884:      );
2885:    }
2886:    try {
2887:      // The line was read before this run held the marker, so another run may
2888:      // have finished its own takeover since; only the bytes judged stale above
2889:      // are this run's to remove.
2890:      const recheck = readBufferOrNull(ctx.lockPath);
2891:      const recheckLine = recheck ? recheck.toString('utf8').trim() : '(empty lock file)';
2892:      if (!recheck || recheckLine !== currentLine) return refusal(recheckLine);
2893:      // A lock another run wrote after the read above is put back, and the
2894:      // create below then finds it and reports that run as the holder.
2895:      const isRemoved = removeLeftover(ctx.lockPath, (claimPath) => {
2896:        const claimed = readBufferOrNull(claimPath);
2897:        return Boolean(claimed) && claimed.equals(recheck);
2898:      });
2899:      if (isRemoved) console.log('note: took over the overlay lock of a run that has ended: ' + currentLine);
2900:      try {
2901:        fd = fs.openSync(ctx.lockPath, 'wx');
2902:      } catch (retryError) {
2903:        if (!retryError || retryError.code !== 'EEXIST') throw retryError;
2904:        const taken = readBufferOrNull(ctx.lockPath);
2905:        return refusal(taken ? taken.toString('utf8').trim() : '(empty lock file)');
2906:      }
2907:    } finally {
2908:      fs.closeSync(takeoverFd);
2909:      try {
2910:        fs.unlinkSync(takeoverPath);
2911:      } catch (unlinkError) {
2912:        if (!unlinkError || unlinkError.code !== 'ENOENT') throw unlinkError;
2913:      }
2914:    }
2915:  }
2916:  try {
2917:    fs.writeSync(fd, ownerLine + '\n');
2918:  } finally {
2919:    fs.closeSync(fd);
2920:  }
2921:  const lock = { isHeld: true, lockPath: ctx.lockPath, ownerLine };
2922:  lock.release = () => {
2923:    // Remove only our own lock: a file someone replaced by hand is theirs.
2924:    const current = readBufferOrNull(ctx.lockPath);
2925:    if (current && current.toString('utf8').trim() === ownerLine) fs.unlinkSync(ctx.lockPath);
2926:  };
2927:  return lock;
2928:}
2929:
2930:function lockStillOurs(ctx, lock) {
2931:  if (!lock || !lock.isHeld || lock.lockPath !== ctx.lockPath) return false;
2932:  const current = readBufferOrNull(ctx.lockPath);
2933:  return current !== null && current.toString('utf8').trim() === lock.ownerLine;
2934:}
2935:
2936:/**
2937: * Run a step while holding the overlay lock, refusing when another run holds it.
2938: * A caller that already holds the lock across several steps passes it in
2939: * `options.lock`, and the step then runs without taking it a second time.
2940: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
2941: * @param {string} owner Owner label for a newly taken lock.
2942: * @param {function} run Step to run; receives the lock and returns an exit code.
2943: * @param {{lock?: object}} [options]
2944: * @returns {number} The step's exit code, or 2 when the lock is held elsewhere.
2945: */
2946:function withLock(ctx, owner, run, options = {}) {
2947:  if (lockStillOurs(ctx, options.lock)) return run(options.lock);
2948:  const lock = acquireLock(ctx, owner);
2949:  if (!lock.isHeld) {
2950:    console.log('refuse: the overlay lock is held: ' + lock.ownerLine);
2951:    console.log('  ' + lock.remedy);
2952:    return EXIT_ERROR;
2953:  }
2954:  try {
2955:    return run(lock);
2956:  } finally {
2957:    lock.release();
2958:  }
2959:}
2960:
2961:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2962:// 12. STATUS AND THE FULL CHECK
2963:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2964:
2965:/**
2966: * Run the full read-only check: every overlay plan, the layout links and the
2967: * generators' own check modes.
2968: * The plans come from the same analysis apply uses, so the check reads the same
2969: * accepted hashes: a destination that holds the last applied copy of a changed
2970: * overlay is pending work, not a rebase.
2971: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
2972: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
2973: * @param {object} state Loaded applied-hash state.
2974: * @param {object[]} plans Plans from analyze(), made with the accepted hashes.
2975: * @returns {{checked: number, ok: number, miss: number, problems: string[]}}
2976: */
2977:function runCheck(ctx, manifest, state, plans) {
2978:  const root = ctx.root;
2979:  const problems = [];
2980:  let misses = 0;
2981:  const layout = planLayoutLinks(manifest, root, state);
2982:  const layoutCount = (manifest.layoutLinks || []).length > 0 ? 1 : 0;
2983:  for (const problem of layout.problems) problems.push(`layout: ${problem}`);
2984:  for (const { link } of layout.missing) problems.push(`layout: ${link.path} is missing (link to ${link.target})`);
2985:  if (layout.problems.length + layout.missing.length > 0) misses += 1;
2986:  const rowsFor = (plan) => (plan.entry.scope === 'remove-root'
2987:    ? Math.max(1, Array.isArray(plan.entry.paths) ? plan.entry.paths.length : 0)
2988:    : 1);
2989:  for (const plan of plans) {
2990:    if (plan.problems.length > 0) {
2991:      misses += rowsFor(plan);
2992:      for (const problem of plan.problems) problems.push(`${plan.entry.id}: ${problem}`);
2993:      continue;
2994:    }
2995:    if (plan.entry.scope === 'remove-root') {
2996:      for (let index = 0; index < plan.outputs.length; index += 1) {
2997:        if (!pathExists(plan.outputs[index].filePath)) continue;
2998:        misses += 1;
2999:        problems.push(`${plan.entry.id}: ${plan.entry.paths[index]} is still present`);
3000:      }
3001:      continue;
3002:    }
3003:    const changed = plan.writes.length + plan.deletes.length;
3004:    if (changed > 0) {
3005:      misses += 1;
3006:      const sample = [...plan.writes.map((write) => path.relative(root, write.filePath)), ...plan.deletes.map((target) => path.relative(root, target))];
3007:      problems.push(`${plan.entry.id}: ${changed} path(s) out of sync (${sample.slice(0, 3).join(', ')}${sample.length > 3 ? ', …' : ''})`);
3008:    }
3009:  }
3010:
3011:  const generatedProblems = verifyGenerated(manifest, root);
3012:  for (const problem of generatedProblems) problems.push(`generated: ${problem}`);
3013:
3014:  const overlayChecks = plans.reduce((count, plan) => count + rowsFor(plan), 0);
3015:  const checked = overlayChecks + 1 + layoutCount;
3016:  const miss = misses + (generatedProblems.length > 0 ? 1 : 0);
3017:  return { checked, ok: checked - miss, miss, problems };
3018:}
3019:
3020:/**
3021: * Every source field an overlay keeps off the layout, where a copy sits inside
3022: * the overlay folder at its destination path with the leading dot dropped. A
3023: * copy kept elsewhere still applies, so this names where it belongs instead of
3024: * refusing the run.
3025: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
3026: * @returns {{overlay: string, field: string, from: string, to: string}[]} One
3027: *   record per field off the layout, in manifest order.
3028: */
3029:function layoutMoves(manifest) {
3030:  const moves = [];
3031:  const collect = (entry, field, current, layout) => {
3032:    if (typeof current === 'string' && current !== layout) {
