3033:      moves.push({ overlay: entry.id, field, from: current, to: layout });
3034:    }
3035:  };
3036:  for (const entry of manifest.overlays || []) {
3037:    if (entry.scope === 'remove-root' || typeof entry.destination !== 'string') continue;
3038:    const layout = adoptSource(entry.destination);
3039:    if (entry.scope !== 'marked-section') {
3040:      collect(entry, 'source', entry.source, layout);
3041:      continue;
3042:    }
3043:    // A marked-section entry names the same copy twice when `source` matches
3044:    // `fragment`, so both fields move together; a source that differs is some
3045:    // other copy and stays where the entry put it.
3046:    if (entry.source === entry.fragment) collect(entry, 'source', entry.source, layout);
3047:    collect(entry, 'fragment', entry.fragment, layout);
3048:    if (typeof entry.base === 'string') {
3049:      const extension = path.posix.extname(layout);
3050:      const base = extension === '' ? layout + '.base' : layout.slice(0, -extension.length) + '.base' + extension;
3051:      collect(entry, 'base', entry.base, base);
3052:    }
3053:  }
3054:  return moves;
3055:}
3056:
3057:/**
3058: * Report every destination not in sync, then run the full check.
3059: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
3060: * @param {{isJson?: boolean, isAudit?: boolean}} [options] `isAudit` also
3061: *   compares the vendored tree with the pin, for the paths no overlay owns.
3062: * @returns {number} 0 when everything is in sync, 1 otherwise.
3063: */
3064:function commandStatus(ctx, options = {}) {
3065:  const manifest = loadManifest(ctx);
3066:  const state = loadState(ctx, manifest);
3067:  const analysis = analyze(ctx, manifest, state);
3068:  const check = runCheck(ctx, manifest, state, analysis.plans);
3069:  const audit = options.isAudit ? auditVendoredTree(ctx, manifest) : null;
3070:  const outOfSync = analysis.records.filter((record) => record.state !== 'in-sync');
3071:  const stale = staleRootFiles(manifest);
3072:  const offLayout = layoutMoves(manifest);
3073:  const isInSync = outOfSync.length === 0 && analysis.blocked.length === 0 && check.miss === 0 &&
3074:    stale.length === 0 && (audit === null || audit.findings.length === 0);
3075:
3076:  if (options.isJson) {
3077:    const report = {
3078:      result: isInSync ? 'PASSED' : 'FAILED',
3079:      root: ctx.root,
3080:      overlayFolder: ctx.overlayLabel,
3081:      counts: stateCounts(analysis.records),
3082:      destinations: outOfSync.map((record) => ({
3083:        state: record.state,
3084:        key: record.key,
3085:        kind: record.layout ? 'layout-link' : (record.removal ? 'remove-root' : 'overlay'),
3086:        overlay: record.layout ? null : record.plan.entry.id,
3087:        source: record.source ? ctx.overlayLabel + '/' + record.source : null,
3088:        fix: fixFor(ctx, record),
3089:      })),
3090:      staleRootFiles: stale,
3091:      offLayout,
3092:      check,
3093:    };
3094:    if (audit) report.audit = audit;
3095:    console.log(JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));
3096:    return isInSync ? EXIT_OK : EXIT_CONFLICT;
3097:  }
3098:
3099:  for (const record of outOfSync) console.log(problemLine(ctx, record));
3100:  console.log(stateSummary(analysis.records));
3101:  for (const problem of check.problems) console.log(`MISS     ${problem}`);
3102:  // A re-pin never copies a root file, so an upstream move to an owned one is
3103:  // outstanding work no destination line can report, and this comparison is the
3104:  // only place status sees it between re-pins.
3105:  for (const entry of stale) {
3106:    console.log(`STALE    root-file ${entry.path}: upstream changed it since overlay ${entry.overlay} was pinned; ` +
3107:      'rebase the overlay, then update its pinnedSha256');
3108:  }
3109:  for (const move of offLayout) {
3110:    console.log(`LAYOUT   ${move.overlay}: ${move.field} ${move.from} belongs at ${move.to}; run ${ENGINE_COMMAND} apply --relayout`);
3111:  }
3112:  if (audit) {
3113:    for (const finding of audit.findings) console.log(`AUDIT    ${finding.kind} ${finding.path}`);
3114:    if (!audit.isAvailable) console.log('AUDIT    skipped: no upstream checkout at the pin');
3115:    console.log(`audit=${audit.findings.length}`);
3116:  }
3117:  console.log(`checked=${check.checked} ok=${check.ok} miss=${check.miss}`);
3118:  console.log(isInSync ? 'RESULT: PASSED' : 'RESULT: FAILED');
3119:  return isInSync ? EXIT_OK : EXIT_CONFLICT;
3120:}
3121:
3122:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
3123:// 13. APPLY
3124:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
3125:
3126:/**
3127: * Apply every overlay edit; the caller holds the lock.
3128: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
3129: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
3130: * @param {{isRegenerateForced?: boolean, outcome?: object,
3131: *   upstreamBytes?: ((key: string) => Buffer|null)|null}} [options] A re-pin
3132: *   regenerates even when no overlay wrote, because the copy changed the inputs;
3133: *   `outcome.changed` receives the count printed on the last line;
3134: *   `upstreamBytes` reads the pin this run compares against.
3135: * @returns {number} 0 when applied, 1 on conflict or a failed step.
3136: */
3137:function applyUnderLock(ctx, manifest, options = {}) {
3138:  const root = ctx.root;
3139:  const state = loadState(ctx, manifest);
3140:  const analysis = analyze(ctx, manifest, state, options.upstreamBytes);
3141:  const blockers = analysis.records.filter((record) => BLOCKING_STATES.has(record.state));
3142:  const layoutProblems = analysis.layout.problems;
3143:  if (blockers.length > 0 || analysis.blocked.length > 0 || layoutProblems.length > 0) {
3144:    // Nothing is written when one overlay cannot be applied on its own: a
3145:    // half-applied set is worse than an untouched tree.
3146:    if (blockers.length > 0) {
3147:      printProblems(ctx, analysis.records);
3148:      // A destination whose desired bytes could not be computed has no line of
3149:      // its own saying why, so the plan's own reason is printed beside it.
3150:      for (const record of blockers) {
3151:        for (const problem of (record.plan && record.plan.problems) || []) console.log(`BLOCKED  ${record.plan.entry.id}: ${problem}`);
3152:      }
3153:    }
3154:    for (const plan of analysis.blocked) {
3155:      for (const problem of plan.problems) console.log(`BLOCKED  ${plan.entry.id}: ${problem}`);
3156:    }
3157:    for (const problem of layoutProblems) console.log(`BLOCKED  layout: ${problem}`);
3158:    if (analysis.blocked.length > 0 || layoutProblems.length > 0) {
3159:      console.log(`blocked=${analysis.blocked.length + (layoutProblems.length > 0 ? 1 : 0)} changed=0`);
3160:    }
3161:    return EXIT_CONFLICT;
3162:  }
3163:
3164:  const changedRecords = analysis.records.filter((record) => record.state === 'overlay-edited' && !record.layout);
3165:  const layoutRecords = analysis.records.filter((record) => record.layout);
3166:  const plansToApply = new Set(changedRecords.map((record) => record.plan));
3167:  let written = 0;
3168:  for (const plan of plansToApply) written += applyOnePlan(plan, root);
3169:
3170:  // The links are restored before the regeneration steps run: a route resolving
3171:  // skills through one of them reads nothing until the link exists.
3172:  for (const record of layoutRecords) {
3173:    applyLayoutLink(root, record.link, record.isRetarget);
3174:    console.log('linked ' + record.key + ' -> ' + record.target);
3175:  }
3176:
3177:  const isRegenerated = written > 0 || options.isRegenerateForced === true;
3178:  let rewrittenBy = new Map();
3179:  if (isRegenerated) {
3180:    const owned = analysis.records.filter((record) => !record.layout && !record.removal).map((record) => record.key);
3181:    const regeneration = regenerate(manifest, root, owned);
3182:    rewrittenBy = regeneration.rewrittenBy;
3183:    for (const { step, result } of regeneration.failed) {
3184:      const output = ((result.stdout || '') + (result.stderr || '')).trim().split('\n').slice(-3).join(' | ');
3185:      console.log('regen ' + step.id + ': FAILED exit=' + result.status + ' ' + output);
3186:    }
3187:    if (regeneration.failed.length > 0) {
3188:      console.log('regeneration failed for ' + regeneration.failed.length + ' step(s); the tree is only partly rebuilt');
3189:      return EXIT_CONFLICT;
3190:    }
3191:  }
3192:
3193:  // Only a set the entry set still names is kept. The check after the write
3194:  // reads these records and not the ones the run started with: a set this run
3195:  // took back has left the file, and reading its old record against the
3196:  // written file would judge the run's own work as a stranger's edit.
3197:  const sets = {};
3198:  for (const plan of analysis.plans) {
3199:    if (!plan.listedSets || plan.listedSets.length === 0) continue;
3200:    sets[plan.entry.destination] = plan.listedSets;
3201:  }
3202:  const updated = analyze(ctx, manifest, { ...state, sets }, options.upstreamBytes);
3203:  const unresolved = updated.records.filter((record) => record.state !== 'in-sync');
3204:  if (unresolved.length > 0) {
3205:    for (const record of unresolved) {
3206:      const stepIds = record.layout ? undefined : rewrittenBy.get(record.key);
3207:      console.log(stepIds ? regeneratedLine(ctx, record, stepIds) : problemLine(ctx, record));
3208:    }
3209:    return EXIT_CONFLICT;
3210:  }
3211:  const files = {};
3212:  for (const record of updated.records) {
3213:    if (record.output.buffer !== null) files[record.key] = sha256(record.output.buffer);
3214:  }
3215:  // Only the items an entry set still lists are recorded: one it dropped has just
3216:  // left the live file, and a destination with none listed keeps no key at all.
3217:  // The plans are the ones whose writes this run applied, because the analysis
3218:  // that follows sees those items as already live and no longer as its own work.
3219:  const items = {};
3220:  for (const plan of analysis.plans) {
3221:    if (!plan.listedItems || plan.listedItems.length === 0) continue;
3222:    items[plan.entry.destination] = plan.listedItems;
3223:  }
3224:  const links = {};
3225:  for (const link of manifest.layoutLinks || []) {
3226:    const linkPath = path.join(root, link.path);
3227:    let stats = null;
3228:    try {
3229:      stats = fs.lstatSync(linkPath);
3230:    } catch (error) {
3231:      if (!error || error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
3232:    }
3233:    // Only a link standing at the manifest's target is recorded as the
3234:    // engine's own: that record is what lets a later apply retarget it.
3235:    if (stats && stats.isSymbolicLink() && fs.readlinkSync(linkPath) === link.target) {
3236:      links[link.path] = link.target;
3237:    }
3238:  }
3239:  writeStateIfChanged(ctx, { pin: manifest.pin.commit, files, links, items, sets });
3240:
3241:  // The applied hashes are recorded first: they describe the destinations, which
3242:  // are in sync, whatever the generators' own checks say next.
3243:  const generatedProblems = isRegenerated ? verifyGenerated(manifest, root) : [];
3244:  for (const problem of generatedProblems) console.log(`MISS     generated: ${problem}`);
3245:  const changed = changedRecords.length + layoutRecords.length;
3246:  if (options.outcome) options.outcome.changed = changed;
3247:  console.log(changed + ' changed');
3248:  return generatedProblems.length > 0 ? EXIT_CONFLICT : EXIT_OK;
3249:}
3250:
3251:/**
3252: * Apply every overlay edit under the lock, then regenerate and verify.
3253: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
3254: * @param {{lock?: object}} [options] A lock the caller already holds.
3255: * @returns {number} 0 when applied, 1 on conflict, 2 when the lock is held.
3256: */
3257:function commandApply(ctx, options = {}) {
3258:  const manifest = loadManifest(ctx);
3259:  return withLock(ctx, 'overlay-engine apply', () => applyUnderLock(ctx, manifest), options);
3260:}
3261:
3262:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
3263:// 14. CAPTURE
3264:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
3265:
3266:function directoryRelative(rootDirectory, filePath) {
3267:  const relative = path.relative(rootDirectory, filePath);
3268:  if (relative === '' || !isWithin(rootDirectory, filePath)) return null;
3269:  return toPosix(relative);
3270:}
3271:
3272:function captureCandidates(root, inputPath) {
3273:  if (path.isAbsolute(inputPath)) return [path.resolve(inputPath)];
3274:  return [path.resolve(root, inputPath), path.resolve(process.cwd(), inputPath)];
3275:}
3276:
3277:/**
3278: * Find the overlay that owns a capture candidate.
3279: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
3280: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
3281: * @param {string} candidate Absolute path inside the tree.
3282: * @returns {object|null} The capture record, or null when no overlay owns it.
3283: */
3284:function captureRecordForPath(ctx, manifest, candidate) {
3285:  const root = ctx.root;
3286:  if (!isWithin(root, candidate) || candidate === root) return null;
3287:  const key = toPosix(path.relative(root, candidate));
3288:  for (const entry of manifest.overlays) {
3289:    if (entry.scope === 'remove-root') {
3290:      for (const relativePath of entry.paths || []) {
3291:        const removalPath = resolveRemoveRootPath(root, relativePath);
3292:        if (!isWithin(removalPath, candidate)) continue;
3293:        return { entry, key, destinationPath: candidate, removal: true, removalOwner: 'remove-root row ' + entry.id };
3294:      }
3295:      continue;
3296:    }
3297:    const destinationPath = path.resolve(root, entry.destination);
3298:    if (entry.scope === 'file' && candidate === destinationPath) {
3299:      return { entry, key, destinationPath, sourcePath: path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source) };
3300:    }
3301:    if (entry.scope === 'marked-section' && candidate === destinationPath) {
3302:      return { entry, key, destinationPath, sourcePath: path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.fragment) };
3303:    }
3304:    if (entry.scope === 'json-entry' && candidate === destinationPath) {
3305:      return { entry, key, destinationPath, sourcePath: path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source) };
3306:    }
3307:    if (entry.scope === 'directory') {
3308:      const relative = directoryRelative(destinationPath, candidate);
3309:      if (relative === null) continue;
3310:      const sourcePath = path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source, relative);
3311:      return { entry, key, destinationPath: candidate, sourcePath };
3312:    }
3313:    if (entry.scope === 'skill-root') {
3314:      const relative = directoryRelative(destinationPath, candidate);
3315:      if (relative === null) continue;
3316:      const isReplacement = (entry.replace || []).some((replacement) =>
3317:        relative === replacement || relative.startsWith(replacement + '/'));
3318:      const sourcePath = path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source, relative);
3319:      const isKept = [...keptRetainedPaths(entry, path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source))].some((kept) =>
3320:        relative === kept || relative.startsWith(kept + '/'));
3321:      if (isReplacement && !isKept) {
3322:        return { entry, key, destinationPath: candidate, sourcePath };
3323:      }
3324:      const isRemoved = (entry.remove || []).some((removed) =>
3325:        relative === removed || relative.startsWith(removed + '/'));
3326:      if (isRemoved) {
3327:        return { entry, key, destinationPath: candidate, removal: true, removalOwner: 'overlay ' + entry.id };
3328:      }
3329:    }
3330:  }
3331:  return null;
3332:}
3333:
3334:function extractMarkedSection(entry, live) {
3335:  const text = live.toString('utf8');
3336:  const begin = text.indexOf(entry.beginMarker);
3337:  if (begin < 0) throw new Error('live file is missing the begin marker');
3338:  const endMarker = text.indexOf(entry.endMarker, begin);
3339:  if (endMarker < 0) throw new Error('live file is missing the end marker');
3340:  let end = endMarker + entry.endMarker.length;
3341:  if (text.slice(end, end + 2) === '\r\n') end += 2;
3342:  else if (text[end] === '\n') end += 1;
3343:  return Buffer.from(text.slice(begin, end), 'utf8');
3344:}
3345:
3346:/**
3347: * Capture one live edit; the caller holds the lock.
3348: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
3349: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
3350: * @param {string} inputPath Destination path as the caller named it.
3351: * @returns {number} 0 when captured, 1 when not live-edited, 2 on refusal.
3352: */
3353:function captureUnderLock(ctx, manifest, inputPath) {
3354:  const root = ctx.root;
3355:  let record = null;
3356:  for (const candidate of captureCandidates(root, inputPath)) {
3357:    record = captureRecordForPath(ctx, manifest, candidate);
3358:    if (record) break;
3359:  }
3360:  if (!record) {
3361:    console.log('refuse: path is not an overlay destination: ' + inputPath);
3362:    return EXIT_ERROR;
3363:  }
3364:  if (record.removal) {
3365:    console.log('refuse: ' + record.key + ' is scheduled for removal by ' + record.removalOwner + '; capture is not supported');
3366:    return EXIT_ERROR;
3367:  }
3368:  if (isCaptureRestricted(ctx, record, inputPath)) {
3369:    console.log('refuse: protected capture path: ' + record.key);
3370:    return EXIT_ERROR;
3371:  }
3372:  if (record.entry.scope === 'json-entry') {
3373:    console.log('refuse: edit ' + ctx.overlayLabel + '/' + sourceRelative(ctx, record.sourcePath) + ' instead');
3374:    return EXIT_ERROR;
3375:  }
3376:  if (!['directory', 'skill-root'].includes(record.entry.scope) && !isFile(record.sourcePath)) {
3377:    console.log(record.entry.id + ': canonical source is missing: ' + sourceRelative(ctx, record.sourcePath));
3378:    return EXIT_ERROR;
3379:  }
3380:  if (!isFile(record.destinationPath)) {
3381:    console.log('refuse: live destination is not a regular file: ' + record.key);
3382:    return EXIT_ERROR;
3383:  }
3384:  const livePath = canonicalPath(record.destinationPath);
3385:  if (isCaptureRestricted(ctx, record, livePath)) {
3386:    console.log('refuse: protected capture path: ' + record.key);
3387:    return EXIT_ERROR;
3388:  }
3389:  const state = loadState(ctx, manifest);
3390:  const analysis = analyze(ctx, manifest, state);
3391:  const classified = analysis.records.find((item) => item.key === record.key);
3392:  if (!classified) {
3393:    console.log('refuse: path is not an overlay destination: ' + inputPath);
3394:    return EXIT_ERROR;
3395:  }
3396:  if (classified.state === 'in-sync') {
3397:    console.log('nothing to capture');
3398:    return EXIT_OK;
3399:  }
3400:  if (classified.state !== 'live-edited') {
3401:    printProblems(ctx, [classified]);
3402:    // The same reason apply prints for this destination: a capture and an apply
3403:    // that both refuse it have to read the same way.
3404:    for (const problem of (classified.plan && classified.plan.problems) || []) {
3405:      console.log(`BLOCKED  ${classified.plan.entry.id}: ${problem}`);
3406:    }
3407:    return EXIT_CONFLICT;
3408:  }
3409:  const live = fs.readFileSync(record.destinationPath);
3410:  const source = record.entry.scope === 'marked-section'
3411:    ? extractMarkedSection(record.entry, live)
3412:    : live;
3413:  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(record.sourcePath), { recursive: true });
3414:  fs.writeFileSync(record.sourcePath, source);
3415:
3416:  const files = { ...state.files, [record.key]: sha256(live) };
3417:  writeStateIfChanged(ctx, {
3418:    pin: state.exists ? state.pin : manifest.pin.commit,
3419:    files,
3420:    links: state.links,
3421:    items: state.items,
3422:    sets: state.sets,
3423:  });
3424:  console.log('captured ' + record.key);
3425:  return EXIT_OK;
3426:}
3427:
3428:/**
3429: * Copy a live edit to an overlay destination back into its overlay source.
3430: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
3431: * @param {string} inputPath Destination path, relative to the root or the cwd.
3432: * @param {{lock?: object}} [options] A lock the caller already holds.
3433: * @returns {number} 0 when captured, 1 when not live-edited, 2 on refusal.
3434: */
3435:function commandCapture(ctx, inputPath, options = {}) {
3436:  const manifest = loadManifest(ctx);
3437:  return withLock(ctx, 'overlay-engine capture', () => captureUnderLock(ctx, manifest, inputPath), options);
3438:}
3439:
3440:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
3441:// 15. GUARD
3442://
3443:// Answers one question, whether a path is owned by an overlay, and writes
3444:// nothing: a live edit to an owned path is lost at the next re-copy, so the
3445:// caller is told to edit the overlay instead.
3446:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
3447:
3448:/**
3449: * List every live path an overlay owns, for the guard.
3450: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
3451: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
3452: * @returns {object[]} Records with the entry, destination and source path.
3453: */
3454:function destinationCandidates(ctx, manifest) {
3455:  const root = ctx.root;
3456:  const records = [];
3457:  for (const entry of manifest.overlays) {
3458:    if (entry.scope === 'remove-root') {
3459:      for (const relativePath of entry.paths || []) {
3460:        records.push({ entry, destinationPath: resolveRemoveRootPath(root, relativePath), removal: true, removalOwner: 'remove-root row ' + entry.id });
3461:      }
3462:      continue;
3463:    }
3464:    const destinationPath = path.resolve(root, entry.destination);
3465:    if (!isWithin(root, destinationPath)) throw new Error(entry.id + ': destination escapes the sync root');
3466:    if (entry.scope === 'file' || entry.scope === 'json-entry') {
3467:      records.push({ entry, destinationPath, sourcePath: path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source) });
3468:      continue;
3469:    }
3470:    if (entry.scope === 'marked-section') {
3471:      records.push({ entry, destinationPath, sourcePath: path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.fragment) });
3472:      continue;
3473:    }
3474:    if (entry.scope === 'directory') {
3475:      const sourceDirectory = path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source);
3476:      for (const sourcePath of listTreePaths(sourceDirectory)) {
3477:        if (!isFile(sourcePath)) continue;
3478:        const relative = path.relative(sourceDirectory, sourcePath);
3479:        records.push({ entry, destinationPath: path.join(destinationPath, relative), sourcePath });
3480:      }
3481:      for (const livePath of listTreePaths(destinationPath)) {
3482:        const relative = path.relative(destinationPath, livePath);
3483:        if (records.some((record) => record.entry === entry && path.resolve(record.destinationPath) === path.resolve(livePath))) continue;
3484:        records.push({ entry, destinationPath: livePath, sourcePath: path.join(sourceDirectory, relative) });
3485:      }
3486:      continue;
3487:    }
3488:    if (entry.scope === 'skill-root') {
3489:      const sourceDirectory = path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source);
3490:      const retained = new Set((entry.retained || []).map((item) => item.path));
3491:      const kept = keptRetainedPaths(entry, sourceDirectory);
3492:      const owned = new Set();
3493:      for (const replacement of entry.replace || []) {
3494:        const sourcePath = path.join(sourceDirectory, replacement);
3495:        const destinationReplacement = path.join(destinationPath, replacement);
3496:        const relativePaths = isDirectory(sourcePath)
3497:          ? listTreePaths(sourcePath).filter(isFile).map((item) => path.relative(sourceDirectory, item))
3498:          : (isFile(sourcePath) ? [replacement] : []);
3499:        for (const relative of relativePaths) {
3500:          if (kept.has(relative)) continue;
3501:          owned.add(relative);
3502:          records.push({ entry, destinationPath: path.join(destinationPath, relative), sourcePath: path.join(sourceDirectory, relative) });
3503:        }
3504:        const livePaths = isDirectory(destinationReplacement)
3505:          ? listTreePaths(destinationReplacement)
3506:          : (pathExists(destinationReplacement) ? [destinationReplacement] : []);
3507:        for (const livePath of livePaths) {
3508:          if (!isFile(livePath)) continue;
3509:          const relative = path.relative(destinationPath, livePath);
3510:          if (kept.has(relative) || owned.has(relative)) continue;
3511:          owned.add(relative);
3512:          records.push({ entry, destinationPath: livePath, sourcePath: path.join(sourceDirectory, relative) });
3513:        }
3514:      }
3515:      for (const removed of entry.remove || []) {
3516:        const removedPath = path.join(destinationPath, removed);
3517:        const livePaths = isDirectory(removedPath)
3518:          ? listTreePaths(removedPath)
3519:          : (pathExists(removedPath) ? [removedPath] : []);
3520:        for (const livePath of livePaths) {
3521:          if (!isFile(livePath)) continue;
3522:          const relative = path.relative(destinationPath, livePath);
3523:          if (retained.has(relative) || owned.has(relative)) continue;
3524:          owned.add(relative);
3525:          records.push({ entry, destinationPath: livePath, sourcePath: path.join(sourceDirectory, relative), removal: true, removalOwner: 'overlay ' + entry.id });
3526:        }
3527:      }
3528:    }
3529:  }
3530:  return records;
3531:}
3532:
3533:/**
3534: * Answer whether a path is owned by an overlay. Never writes.
3535: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
3536: * @param {string} inputPath Path to test, relative to the cwd or absolute.
3537: * @returns {number} 0 to allow, 1 to refuse.
3538: */
3539:function commandGuard(ctx, inputPath) {
3540:  const root = ctx.root;
3541:  const manifest = loadManifest(ctx);
3542:  const candidate = canonicalPath(path.resolve(process.cwd(), inputPath));
3543:  const removalCandidate = canonicalPathWithoutLeaf(path.resolve(process.cwd(), inputPath));
3544:  const lexicalCandidate = path.resolve(process.cwd(), inputPath);
3545:  for (const record of destinationCandidates(ctx, manifest)) {
3546:    if (record.removal) {
3547:      const removalPath = canonicalPathWithoutLeaf(record.destinationPath);
3548:      const isLexicalMatch = isWithin(record.destinationPath, lexicalCandidate);
3549:      const isCanonicalMatch = isWithin(removalPath, removalCandidate);
3550:      if (!isLexicalMatch && !isCanonicalMatch) continue;
3551:      const destination = isWithin(root, lexicalCandidate)
3552:        ? relativeKey(root, lexicalCandidate)
3553:        : relativeKey(canonicalPath(root), removalCandidate);
3554:      console.log('refuse: ' + destination + ' is scheduled for removal by ' + record.removalOwner);
3555:      return EXIT_CONFLICT;
3556:    }
3557:    if (!pathExists(record.destinationPath)) continue;
3558:    if (canonicalPath(record.destinationPath) !== candidate) continue;
3559:    const destination = relativeKey(root, record.destinationPath);
3560:    const source = sourceRelative(ctx, record.sourcePath);
3561:    console.log('refuse: ' + destination + ' is owned by ' + ctx.overlayLabel + '/' + source +
3562:      '; edit the overlay, then run ' + ENGINE_COMMAND + ' apply');
3563:    return EXIT_CONFLICT;
3564:  }
3565:  const refusal = strictWriteRefusal(ctx, manifest, inputPath);
3566:  if (refusal) {
3567:    console.log(refusal);
3568:    return EXIT_CONFLICT;
3569:  }
3570:  console.log('allow');
3571:  return EXIT_OK;
3572:}
3573:
3574:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
3575:// 16. REPIN
3576://
3577:// A re-pin replaces the tree's upstream files with a newer commit and puts the
3578:// overlays back on top. It copies the `.skilled` paths upstream changed, so the
3579:// files the project regenerates stay put unless upstream changed them, and the
3580:// runtime folders whole, because no generator restores a drifted runtime file.
3581:// Three rules keep it reversible:
3582://   1. The paths it touches are computed once, and that one list drives both
3583://      the backup and the copy, so nothing is written that was not backed up.
3584://   2. An unowned file that differs from the old pin stops the run before any
3585://      write: the copy would silently revert an edit someone made in place.
3586://   3. Every failure after the backup names the run that --rollback restores.
3587:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
3588:
3589:function runGit(upstream, args) {
3590:  const result = spawnSync('git', ['-C', upstream, ...args], { encoding: 'buffer', maxBuffer: GIT_MAX_BUFFER });
3591:  if (result.error) throw result.error;
3592:  return result;
3593:}
3594:
3595:/**
3596: * Read a path's bytes at one commit of an upstream checkout.
3597: * @param {string} upstream Upstream checkout.
3598: * @param {string} commit Commit id.
3599: * @returns {(key: string) => Buffer|null}
3600: */
3601:function commitBytesReader(upstream, commit) {
3602:  return (key) => {
3603:    const result = runGit(upstream, ['cat-file', 'blob', `${commit}:${key}`]);
3604:    return result.status === 0 ? result.stdout : null;
3605:  };
3606:}
3607:
3608:/**
3609: * Read a path's bytes from an extracted commit tree.
3610: * @param {string} tree Folder holding the commit's roots.
3611: * @returns {(key: string) => Buffer|null}
3612: */
3613:function treeBytesReader(tree) {
3614:  return (key) => {
3615:    const filePath = path.join(tree, key);
3616:    return isFile(filePath) ? fs.readFileSync(filePath) : null;
3617:  };
3618:}
3619:
3620:/**
3621: * Read a path's bytes at the commit the manifest records, when the manifest
3622: * names the checkout that holds it. Without that checkout there is nothing to
3623: * compare, and the caller keeps its no-state classification.
3624: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
3625: * @returns {((key: string) => Buffer|null)|null}
3626: */
3627:function pinnedUpstreamBytes(manifest) {
3628:  const pin = manifest.pin || {};
3629:  if (typeof pin.checkout !== 'string' || typeof pin.commit !== 'string' ||
3630:      !isDirectory(pin.checkout)) return null;
3631:  return commitBytesReader(pin.checkout, pin.commit);
3632:}
3633:
3634:function gitText(upstream, args) {
3635:  const result = runGit(upstream, args);
3636:  if (result.status !== 0) {
3637:    throw new Error('git ' + args[0] + ' failed in ' + upstream + ': ' + result.stderr.toString('utf8').trim());
3638:  }
3639:  return result.stdout.toString('utf8');
3640:}
3641:
3642:function resolveCommit(upstream, commit) {
3643:  const result = runGit(upstream, ['rev-parse', '--verify', '--quiet', '--end-of-options', commit + '^{commit}']);
3644:  return result.status === 0 ? result.stdout.toString('utf8').trim() : null;
3645:}
3646:
3647:function objectFormat(upstream) {
3648:  const result = runGit(upstream, ['rev-parse', '--show-object-format']);
3649:  return result.status === 0 && result.stdout.toString('utf8').trim() === 'sha256' ? 'sha256' : 'sha1';
3650:}
3651:
3652:// The object id git gives these bytes, so a live file can be compared with a
3653:// pinned one without reading the pinned bytes out of the repository.
3654:function gitBlobId(format, buffer) {
3655:  return crypto.createHash(format).update(`blob ${buffer.length}\0`).update(buffer).digest('hex');
3656:}
3657:
3658:/**
3659: * List the blobs below the given roots at one commit.
3660: * Submodule entries are left out: an archive carries no bytes for them.
3661: * @param {string} upstream Upstream checkout.
3662: * @param {string} commit Full commit id.
3663: * @param {string[]} roots Root folders, relative to the repository root.
3664: * @returns {Map<string, {mode: string, object: string}>} Blobs keyed by path.
3665: */
3666:function listBlobs(upstream, commit, roots) {
3667:  const entries = new Map();
3668:  const output = gitText(upstream, ['ls-tree', '-r', '-z', '--full-tree', commit, '--', ...roots]);
3669:  for (const record of output.split('\0')) {
3670:    if (!record) continue;
3671:    const tab = record.indexOf('\t');
3672:    const [mode, type, object] = record.slice(0, tab).split(' ');
3673:    if (type === 'blob') entries.set(record.slice(tab + 1), { mode, object });
3674:  }
3675:  return entries;
3676:}
3677:
3678:function diffNames(upstream, oldCommit, newCommit, filter, roots) {
3679:  const output = gitText(upstream, ['diff', '--no-ext-diff', '--name-only', '-z', '--no-renames',
3680:    '--diff-filter=' + filter, oldCommit, newCommit, '--', ...roots]);
3681:  return output.split('\0').filter(Boolean);
3682:}
3683:
3684:function normalizedRelative(value, label) {
3685:  const normalized = typeof value === 'string' ? path.posix.normalize(value).replace(/\/+$/, '') : '';
3686:  if (normalized === '' || normalized === '.' || normalized === '..' || normalized.startsWith('../') ||
3687:      path.isAbsolute(value) || value.includes('\\') || value.includes('\0')) {
3688:    throw new Error(`${label} must name a relative path inside the tree: ${String(value)}`);
3689:  }
3690:  return normalized;
3691:}
3692:
3693:function isUnderAny(relativePath, prefixes) {
3694:  return prefixes.some((prefix) => relativePath === prefix || relativePath.startsWith(prefix + '/'));
3695:}
3696:
3697:/**
3698: * Read the manifest's optional `repin` block.
3699: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
3700: * @returns {{roots: string[], runtimeFolders: string[], exclude: string[], rootFiles: string[]}}
3701: */
3702:function repinSettings(manifest) {
3703:  const settings = manifest.repin === undefined ? {} : manifest.repin;
3704:  if (!settings || typeof settings !== 'object' || Array.isArray(settings)) {
3705:    throw new Error('the manifest repin block must be an object');
3706:  }
3707:  const listOf = (key, fallback) => {
3708:    const value = settings[key] === undefined ? fallback : settings[key];
3709:    if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error(`repin.${key} must be a list of paths`);
3710:    return value.map((item) => normalizedRelative(item, 'repin.' + key));
3711:  };
3712:  const runtimeFolders = listOf('runtimeFolders', DEFAULT_RUNTIME_FOLDERS);
3713:  return {
3714:    roots: [...new Set([VENDOR_ROOT, ...runtimeFolders])],
3715:    runtimeFolders,
3716:    exclude: listOf('exclude', []),
3717:    // Upstream root files the project carries. A re-pin never copies them, so
3718:    // naming them is what lets the run report an upstream change to one.
3719:    rootFiles: listOf('rootFiles', []),
3720:  };
3721:}
3722:
3723:// Both scopes record upstream's copy of the destination in `pinnedSha256`: a
3724:// file overlay copies that file whole, and a marked-section overlay keeps it as
3725:// its `base`.
3726:const PINNED_UPSTREAM_SCOPES = new Set(['file', 'marked-section']);
3727:
3728:/**
3729: * List the owned root files whose upstream copy has moved since the overlay was
3730: * pinned. A re-pin never copies a root file, so only this comparison tells an
3731: * overlay whose upstream base changed from one that still matches it. A
3732: * marked-section overlay pins the upstream copy it writes its block into, so it
3733: * is judged here as well.
3734: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
3735: * @returns {{path: string, overlay: string}[]} Sorted by path; empty when the
3736: *   manifest names no readable upstream checkout or no root files.
3737: */
3738:function staleRootFiles(manifest) {
3739:  const { rootFiles } = repinSettings(manifest);
3740:  const readUpstream = rootFiles.length === 0 ? null : pinnedUpstreamBytes(manifest);
3741:  if (readUpstream === null) return [];
3742:  const stale = [];
3743:  for (const entry of manifest.overlays) {
3744:    if (!PINNED_UPSTREAM_SCOPES.has(entry.scope) || !entry.pinnedSha256) continue;
3745:    if (!rootFiles.includes(entry.destination)) continue;
3746:    const upstreamBytes = readUpstream(entry.destination);
3747:    if (upstreamBytes === null || sha256(upstreamBytes) === entry.pinnedSha256) continue;
3748:    stale.push({ path: entry.destination, overlay: entry.id });
3749:  }
3750:  return stale.sort((left, right) => left.path.localeCompare(right.path));
3751:}
3752:/**
3753: * Compute the one set of paths the copy will write and delete.
3754: * @param {object} job Re-pin job with upstream, commits and settings.
3755: * @returns {{copy: string[], delete: string[]}} Sorted paths, relative to the tree.
3756: */
3757:function touchedPaths(job) {
3758:  const { upstream, oldCommit, newCommit, settings } = job;
3759:  const isKept = (relativePath) => !isUnderAny(relativePath, settings.exclude);
3760:  const copy = new Set();
3761:  for (const relativePath of diffNames(upstream, oldCommit, newCommit, 'AMT', [VENDOR_ROOT])) {
3762:    if (job.newBlobs.has(relativePath)) copy.add(relativePath);
3763:  }
3764:  for (const relativePath of job.newBlobs.keys()) {
3765:    if (isUnderAny(relativePath, settings.runtimeFolders)) copy.add(relativePath);
3766:  }
3767:  const deleted = diffNames(upstream, oldCommit, newCommit, 'D', settings.roots)
3768:    .filter((relativePath) => job.oldBlobs.has(relativePath) && !job.newBlobs.has(relativePath));
3769:  return {
3770:    copy: [...copy].filter(isKept).sort(),
3771:    delete: deleted.filter(isKept).sort(),
3772:  };
3773:}
3774:
3775:/**
3776: * Build a test for whether a path is managed by the overlay folder: an overlay
3777: * destination, a layout link, or a regenerated output. The copy may overwrite
3778: * those, because apply and the regenerate steps write them back.
3779: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
3780: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
3781: * @returns {(relativePath: string) => boolean}
3782: */
3783:function managedPathTest(ctx, manifest) {
3784:  const exact = new Set();
3785:  const prefixes = [];
3786:  const vendorKept = new Set();
3787:  for (const entry of manifest.overlays) {
3788:    if (entry.scope === 'remove-root') {
3789:      for (const relativePath of entry.paths || []) prefixes.push(path.posix.normalize(relativePath));
3790:      continue;
3791:    }
3792:    const destination = path.posix.normalize(entry.destination);
3793:    if (entry.scope === 'directory') {
3794:      prefixes.push(destination);
3795:    } else if (entry.scope === 'skill-root') {
3796:      for (const relativePath of [...(entry.replace || []), ...(entry.remove || [])]) {
3797:        prefixes.push(path.posix.join(destination, relativePath));
3798:      }
3799:      // A retained file inside a replaced folder is the vendor's own, kept as found.
3800:      for (const relativePath of keptRetainedPaths(entry, path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, entry.source))) {
3801:        vendorKept.add(path.posix.join(destination, relativePath));
3802:      }
3803:    } else {
3804:      exact.add(destination);
3805:    }
3806:  }
3807:  for (const link of manifest.layoutLinks || []) {
3808:    exact.add(path.posix.normalize(link.path));
3809:    if (link.replaces && typeof link.replaces.path === 'string') exact.add(path.posix.normalize(link.replaces.path));
3810:  }
3811:  for (const step of manifest.regenerate || []) {
3812:    for (const output of step.outputs || []) exact.add(path.posix.normalize(output));
3813:  }
3814:  return (relativePath) => !vendorKept.has(relativePath) &&
3815:    (exact.has(relativePath) || isUnderAny(relativePath, prefixes));
3816:}
3817:
3818:// Why a live path is not the given blob, or null when it is.
3819:function blobDifference(filePath, blob, format) {
3820:  let stats;
3821:  try {
3822:    stats = fs.lstatSync(filePath);
3823:  } catch (error) {
3824:    if (error && (error.code === 'ENOENT' || error.code === 'ENOTDIR')) return 'missing from the tree';
3825:    throw error;
3826:  }
3827:  if (stats.isDirectory()) return 'a folder in the tree';
3828:  const isLink = blob.mode === '120000';
3829:  if (isLink !== stats.isSymbolicLink()) return isLink ? 'not a link in the tree' : 'a link in the tree';
3830:  const bytes = isLink ? Buffer.from(fs.readlinkSync(filePath)) : fs.readFileSync(filePath);
3831:  return gitBlobId(format, bytes) === blob.object ? null : (isLink ? 'a different link target' : 'different bytes');
3832:}
3833:
3834:/**
3835: * Find unowned paths the copy would overwrite or delete that differ from the old
3836: * pin, and project files at paths upstream ships for the first time.
3837: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
3838: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
3839: * @param {object} job Re-pin job.
3840: * @param {{copy: string[], delete: string[]}} touched The one set of touched paths.
3841: * @returns {string[]} One refusal line per path.
3842: */
3843:function unownedDrift(ctx, manifest, job, touched) {
3844:  const isManaged = managedPathTest(ctx, manifest);
3845:  const format = objectFormat(job.upstream);
3846:  const lines = [];
3847:  for (const relativePath of [...touched.copy, ...touched.delete].sort()) {
3848:    if (isManaged(relativePath)) continue;
3849:    const filePath = path.join(ctx.root, relativePath);
3850:    const oldBlob = job.oldBlobs.get(relativePath);
3851:    if (oldBlob) {
3852:      const reason = blobDifference(filePath, oldBlob, format);
3853:      if (reason) lines.push(`refuse: unowned ${relativePath} differs from the old pin (${reason})`);
3854:      continue;
3855:    }
3856:    // The copy deletes a link before it writes below it, so what the tree shows
3857:    // through that link is the link target's file, never a copy at this path.
3858:    if (isUnderAny(relativePath, touched.delete)) continue;
3859:    if (!pathExists(filePath)) continue;
3860:    if (blobDifference(filePath, job.newBlobs.get(relativePath), format)) {
3861:      lines.push(`refuse: unowned ${relativePath} is not in the old pin, and the tree holds its own copy there`);
3862:    }
3863:  }
3864:  return lines;
3865:}
3866:
3867:/**
3868: * Find touched paths the copy cannot write safely: a folder where upstream ships
3869: * a file, or a path below a link or file that is not deleted first. Writing
3870: * through a link would land outside the path the backup recorded.
3871: * @param {string} root Destination tree root.
3872: * @param {{copy: string[], delete: string[]}} touched The one set of touched paths.
3873: * @returns {string[]} One refusal line per path.
3874: */
3875:function treeShapeProblems(root, touched) {
3876:  const deletedFirst = new Set(touched.delete);
3877:  const lines = [];
3878:  for (const relativePath of [...touched.copy, ...touched.delete]) {
3879:    if (isEnvName(relativePath)) {
3880:      lines.push(`refuse: ${relativePath} is a .env path, which is never read; list it in repin.exclude`);
3881:      continue;
3882:    }
3883:    const parts = relativePath.split('/');
3884:    let blockedBy = null;
3885:    for (let depth = 1; depth < parts.length && !blockedBy; depth += 1) {
3886:      const ancestor = parts.slice(0, depth).join('/');
3887:      let stats;
3888:      try {
3889:        stats = fs.lstatSync(path.join(root, ancestor));
3890:      } catch (error) {
3891:        if (error && (error.code === 'ENOENT' || error.code === 'ENOTDIR')) break;
3892:        throw error;
3893:      }
3894:      if (stats.isDirectory()) continue;
3895:      if (deletedFirst.has(ancestor)) break;
3896:      blockedBy = `${ancestor}, which the tree holds as a ${stats.isSymbolicLink() ? 'link' : 'file'}`;
3897:    }
3898:    if (blockedBy) {
3899:      lines.push(`refuse: ${relativePath} sits below ${blockedBy}`);
3900:    } else if (isDirectory(path.join(root, relativePath))) {
3901:      lines.push(`refuse: ${relativePath} is a folder in the tree where upstream ships a file; list it in repin.exclude to keep it`);
3902:    }
3903:  }
3904:  return lines;
3905:}
3906:
3907:/**
3908: * Extract the copied roots at the new commit with git archive.
3909: * @param {object} job Re-pin job.
3910: * @param {string} scratch Temporary folder for this run.
3911: * @param {{copy: string[]}} touched The one set of touched paths.
3912: * @returns {string} Folder holding the new commit's roots.
3913: */
3914:function extractNewCommit(job, scratch, touched) {
3915:  const target = path.join(scratch, 'new');
3916:  fs.mkdirSync(target);
3917:  const presentRoots = job.settings.roots.filter((root) => {
3918:    for (const relativePath of job.newBlobs.keys()) if (isUnderAny(relativePath, [root])) return true;
3919:    return false;
3920:  });
3921:  if (presentRoots.length > 0) {
3922:    const archive = path.join(scratch, 'new.tar');
3923:    gitText(job.upstream, ['archive', '--format=tar', '-o', archive, job.newCommit, '--', ...presentRoots]);
3924:    const result = spawnSync('tar', ['-xf', archive, '-C', target], { encoding: 'utf8' });
3925:    if (result.error) throw result.error;
3926:    if (result.status !== 0) throw new Error('tar could not extract the archive: ' + (result.stderr || '').trim());
3927:    fs.rmSync(archive, { force: true });
3928:  }
3929:  const missing = touched.copy.filter((relativePath) => !pathExists(path.join(target, relativePath)));
3930:  if (missing.length > 0) {
3931:    throw new Error(`the archive of ${job.newCommit} lacks ${missing.length} path(s) the copy needs, first ${missing[0]}; ` +
3932:      'an export-ignore attribute upstream would explain it');
3933:  }
3934:  return target;
3935:}
3936:
3937:function makeRunId() {
3938:  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g, '').replace(/\.\d+Z$/, 'Z');
3939:  return stamp + '-' + crypto.randomBytes(3).toString('hex');
3940:}
3941:
3942:function rollbackCommand(ctx, runId) {
3943:  const root = ctx.root === DEFAULT_ROOT ? '' : ' --root ' + (/\s/.test(ctx.root) ? `'${ctx.root}'` : ctx.root);
3944:  return `rollback: ${ENGINE_COMMAND} repin --rollback ${runId}${root}`;
3945:}
3946:
3947:// Whether a path is reached through a link. Such a path is never read or deleted
3948:// by the backup or the rollback: its bytes live at the link's target, and the
3949:// link itself is the entry that gets restored.
3950:function hasLinkedAncestor(root, relativePath) {
3951:  const parts = relativePath.split('/');
3952:  for (let depth = 1; depth < parts.length; depth += 1) {
3953:    const ancestor = path.join(root, ...parts.slice(0, depth));
3954:    let stats;
3955:    try {
3956:      stats = fs.lstatSync(ancestor);
3957:    } catch (error) {
3958:      if (error && (error.code === 'ENOENT' || error.code === 'ENOTDIR')) return false;
3959:      throw error;
3960:    }
3961:    if (!stats.isDirectory()) return true;
3962:  }
3963:  return false;
3964:}
3965:
3966:// Every file and link below a path, for a path apply or a regenerate step writes
3967:// as a whole folder.
3968:function backupLeaves(root, relativePath) {
3969:  const filePath = path.join(root, relativePath);
3970:  if (!isDirectory(filePath)) return [relativePath];
3971:  return listTreePaths(filePath).map((leaf) => relativeKey(root, leaf));
3972:}
3973:
3974:/**
3975: * Back up every path the run may write, before its first write.
3976: * The touched set is the copy's own list. The engine group adds what apply and
3977: * the regenerate steps write afterwards, so a rollback also restores those.
3978: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
3979: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
3980: * @param {object} analysis Analysis of the tree before the copy.
3981: * @param {{copy: string[], delete: string[]}} touched The one set of touched paths.
3982: * @param {string} runDir The run folder.
3983: * @param {object} meta Run facts recorded beside the entries.
3984: * @returns {object} The backup record written to backup.json.
3985: */
3986:function writeBackup(ctx, manifest, analysis, touched, runDir, meta) {
3987:  const root = ctx.root;
3988:  const groups = new Map();
3989:  for (const relativePath of touched.copy) groups.set(relativePath, 'copy');
3990:  for (const relativePath of touched.delete) if (!groups.has(relativePath)) groups.set(relativePath, 'delete');
3991:  const enginePaths = [
3992:    relativeKey(root, ctx.manifestPath),
3993:    relativeKey(root, ctx.statePath),
3994:    ...analysis.records.map((record) => record.key),
3995:    ...(manifest.layoutLinks || []).flatMap((link) => [link.path, link.replaces && link.replaces.path]),
3996:    ...(manifest.regenerate || []).flatMap((step) => step.outputs || []),
3997:  ].filter((value) => typeof value === 'string').map((value) => path.posix.normalize(value));
3998:  for (const relativePath of enginePaths) {
3999:    for (const leaf of backupLeaves(root, relativePath)) if (!groups.has(leaf)) groups.set(leaf, 'engine');
4000:  }
4001:
4002:  const backupDir = path.join(runDir, 'backup');
4003:  const entries = [];
4004:  const createdDirs = new Set();
4005:  for (const relativePath of [...groups.keys()].sort()) {
4006:    const filePath = path.join(root, relativePath);
4007:    relativeKey(root, filePath);
4008:    let stats = null;
4009:    if (!hasLinkedAncestor(root, relativePath)) {
4010:      try {
4011:        stats = fs.lstatSync(filePath);
4012:      } catch (error) {
4013:        if (!error || (error.code !== 'ENOENT' && error.code !== 'ENOTDIR')) throw error;
4014:      }
4015:    }
4016:    const group = groups.get(relativePath);
4017:    if (!stats) {
4018:      entries.push({ path: relativePath, group, existed: false });
4019:      for (let dir = path.dirname(filePath); dir !== root && isWithin(root, dir); dir = path.dirname(dir)) {
4020:        if (pathExists(dir)) break;
4021:        createdDirs.add(relativeKey(root, dir));
4022:      }
4023:      continue;
4024:    }
4025:    if (stats.isSymbolicLink()) {
4026:      const target = fs.readlinkSync(filePath);
4027:      entries.push({ path: relativePath, group, existed: true, kind: 'symlink', target, sha256: sha256(Buffer.from(target)) });
4028:      continue;
4029:    }
4030:    if (isEnvName(relativePath) || !stats.isFile()) {
4031:      throw new Error('refusing to back up ' + relativePath + (stats.isFile() ? ', a .env path' : ', which is not a file or a link'));
4032:    }
4033:    const buffer = fs.readFileSync(filePath);
4034:    writeFile(path.join(backupDir, relativePath), buffer, 0o600);
4035:    entries.push({ path: relativePath, group, existed: true, kind: 'file', mode: stats.mode & 0o777, sha256: sha256(buffer) });
4036:  }
4037:  const backup = {
4038:    schemaVersion: 1,
4039:    ...meta,
4040:    entries,
4041:    createdDirs: [...createdDirs].sort((left, right) => right.length - left.length),
4042:  };
4043:  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(runDir, 'backup.json'), JSON.stringify(backup, null, 2) + '\n');
4044:  return backup;
4045:}
4046:
4047:function pruneEmptyParents(root, filePath) {
4048:  for (let dir = path.dirname(filePath); dir !== root && isWithin(root, dir); dir = path.dirname(dir)) {
4049:    if (!isDirectory(dir) || fs.readdirSync(dir).length > 0) return;
4050:    fs.rmdirSync(dir);
4051:  }
4052:}
4053:
4054:function copyUpstreamEntry(fromPath, toPath) {
4055:  const stats = fs.lstatSync(fromPath);
4056:  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(toPath), { recursive: true });
4057:  if (stats.isSymbolicLink()) {
4058:    ensureSymlink(toPath, fs.readlinkSync(fromPath));
4059:    return;
4060:  }
4061:  // Unlink first, so a link standing at the path is replaced, never written through.
4062:  try {
4063:    fs.unlinkSync(toPath);
4064:  } catch (error) {
4065:    if (!error || error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
4066:  }
4067:  fs.writeFileSync(toPath, fs.readFileSync(fromPath));
4068:  fs.chmodSync(toPath, stats.mode & 0o777);
4069:}
4070:
4071:/**
4072: * Point the manifest's pin at the new commit, changing only that value's text.
4073: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
4074: * @param {string} commit The new pin.
4075: */
4076:function writePin(ctx, commit) {
4077:  const text = fs.readFileSync(ctx.manifestPath, 'utf8');
4078:  const node = nodeAtPath(parseJsonSource(text), ['pin', 'commit']);
4079:  if (node.type !== 'string') throw new Error('the manifest pin.commit is not a string');
4080:  fs.writeFileSync(ctx.manifestPath, text.slice(0, node.start) + JSON.stringify(commit) + text.slice(node.end));
4081:}
4082:
4083:/**
4084: * Name the overlays whose every output now equals the new commit's bytes, which
4085: * makes them dead weight a person can drop.
4086: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
4087: * @param {object} analysis Analysis of the tree after the copy.
4088: * @param {string} newTree Folder holding the new commit's roots.
4089: * @param {string[]} roots Copied roots.
4090: * @returns {string[]} Overlay ids.
4091: */
4092:function overlaysEqualToUpstream(ctx, analysis, newTree, roots) {
4093:  const ids = [];
4094:  for (const plan of analysis.plans) {
4095:    if (plan.problems.length > 0 || plan.outputs.length === 0) continue;
4096:    const isEqual = plan.outputs.every((output) => {
4097:      const key = relativeKey(ctx.root, output.filePath);
4098:      if (!isUnderAny(key, roots)) return false;
4099:      const upstreamPath = path.join(newTree, key);
4100:      if (output.buffer === null) return !pathExists(upstreamPath);
4101:      return isFile(upstreamPath) && buffersEqual(fs.readFileSync(upstreamPath), output.buffer);
4102:    });
4103:    if (isEqual) ids.push(plan.entry.id);
4104:  }
4105:  return ids;
4106:}
4107:
4108:// The most not-copied paths printed one per line; the report file keeps them all.
4109:const NOT_COPIED_SHOWN = 20;
4110:
4111:/**
4112: * List the upstream changes between the two commits that this tree holds but a
4113: * re-pin never copies: files under a top-level file or real folder outside the
4114: * copied roots, such as AGENTS.md, and paths in repin.exclude. Leaving them out
4115: * is deliberate, so a project's own copy is never overwritten, but without this
4116: * list an upstream change there is dropped without anyone seeing it. A
4117: * top-level link is the project's own wiring, such as a specs link, and is skipped.
4118: * @param {object} ctx Engine context of the tree being re-pinned.
4119: * @param {object} job Re-pin job.
4120: * @returns {string[]} Sorted paths, relative to the tree.
4121: */
4122:function notCopiedChanges(ctx, job) {
4123:  const { roots, exclude } = job.settings;
4124:  const holdsRealTopLevel = (relativePath) => {
4125:    const top = relativePath.split('/')[0];
4126:    try {
4127:      return !fs.lstatSync(path.join(ctx.root, top)).isSymbolicLink();
4128:    } catch {
4129:      return false;
4130:    }
4131:  };
4132:  return diffNames(job.upstream, job.oldCommit, job.newCommit, 'AMTD', [])
4133:    .filter((relativePath) => !isUnderAny(relativePath, [ctx.overlayLabel]) &&
4134:      (isUnderAny(relativePath, exclude) ||
4135:        (!isUnderAny(relativePath, roots) && holdsRealTopLevel(relativePath))))
4136:    .sort();
4137:}
4138:
4139:function reportNotCopied(paths, settings) {
4140:  if (paths.length === 0) return;
4141:  console.log(`not-copied ${paths.length} upstream change(s) outside ${settings.roots.join(', ')} or in repin.exclude; carry them over by hand if they apply (AGENTS.md sections: /doctor:agents-md)`);
4142:  for (const relativePath of paths.slice(0, NOT_COPIED_SHOWN)) console.log('not-copied ' + relativePath);
4143:  if (paths.length > NOT_COPIED_SHOWN) console.log(`not-copied … ${paths.length - NOT_COPIED_SHOWN} more in report.json`);
4144:}
4145:
4146:/**
4147: * List the named root files upstream changed between the two commits. A re-pin
4148: * never copies a root file, so each one is named on its own line, uncapped: an
4149: * unowned one has to be carried over by hand, and an owned one needs its
4150: * overlay rebased.
4151: * @param {object} ctx Engine context of the tree being re-pinned.
4152: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
4153: * @param {object} job Re-pin job.
4154: * @returns {{path: string, owned: boolean}[]} Sorted by path.
4155: */
4156:function rootFileChanges(ctx, manifest, job) {
4157:  const { rootFiles } = job.settings;
4158:  if (rootFiles.length === 0) return [];
4159:  const isManaged = managedPathTest(ctx, manifest);
4160:  return diffNames(job.upstream, job.oldCommit, job.newCommit, 'AMTD', rootFiles)
4161:    .filter((relativePath) => rootFiles.includes(relativePath))
4162:    .sort()
4163:    .map((relativePath) => ({ path: relativePath, owned: isManaged(relativePath) }));
4164:}
4165:
4166:function reportRootFiles(changes) {
4167:  for (const change of changes) {
4168:    console.log(change.owned
4169:      ? `root-file ${change.path} changed upstream; rebase the overlay that owns it`
4170:      : `root-file ${change.path} changed upstream and no overlay owns it; carry the change over by hand, ` +
4171:        `or run ${ENGINE_COMMAND} apply --adopt ${shellPath(change.path)} so a later re-pin reports it as an overlay`);
4172:  }
4173:}
4174:
4175:function reportBlocked(analysis, report) {
4176:  for (const record of analysis.records) {
4177:    if (!BLOCKING_STATES.has(record.state)) continue;
4178:    const overlay = record.layout ? 'layout' : record.plan.entry.id;
4179:    report.blocked.push({ overlay, state: record.state, path: record.key });
4180:    console.log(`blocked ${overlay}: ${record.state} ${record.key}`);
4181:  }
4182:  for (const plan of analysis.blocked) {
4183:    for (const problem of plan.problems) {
4184:      report.blocked.push({ overlay: plan.entry.id, state: 'refused', path: plan.entry.destination || null, problem });
4185:      console.log(`blocked ${plan.entry.id}: refused ${problem}`);
4186:    }
4187:  }
4188:  for (const problem of analysis.layout.problems) {
4189:    report.blocked.push({ overlay: 'layout', state: 'refused', path: null, problem });
4190:    console.log(`blocked layout: refused ${problem}`);
4191:  }
4192:}
4193:
4194:/**
4195: * Copy, set the pin, apply twice and check; the caller holds the lock and has
4196: * taken the backup.
4197: * @returns {boolean} Whether every step finished clean.
4198: */
4199:function repinWrites(ctx, job, touched, newTree, report) {
4200:  const root = ctx.root;
4201:  for (const relativePath of [...touched.delete].reverse()) {
4202:    const filePath = path.join(root, relativePath);
4203:    if (removePath(filePath)) pruneEmptyParents(root, filePath);
4204:  }
4205:  for (const relativePath of touched.copy) {
4206:    copyUpstreamEntry(path.join(newTree, relativePath), path.join(root, relativePath));
4207:  }
4208:  writePin(ctx, job.newPin);
4209:
4210:  const loaded = loadManifest(ctx);
4211:  // A stage has no project install behind it, so it skips the regenerate and
4212:  // verify steps and reports only what the overlays do.
4213:  const manifest = job.isStage ? { ...loaded, regenerate: [], verifyGenerated: [] } : loaded;
4214:  if (job.isStage) {
4215:    report.stageSkipped = {
4216:      regenerate: (loaded.regenerate || []).length,
4217:      verify: (loaded.verifyGenerated || []).length,
4218:    };
4219:    // A clean stage proves the copy and the overlays, never the generators, so
4220:    // the report says so rather than reading as a full rehearsal.
4221:    if (report.stageSkipped.regenerate + report.stageSkipped.verify > 0) {
4222:      console.log(`stage skipped ${report.stageSkipped.regenerate} regenerate and ${report.stageSkipped.verify} verify step(s); the live run runs them`);
4223:    }
4224:  }
4225:  // The extracted tree holds only the copied roots. A destination outside them is
4226:  // read from the new commit itself, which is the pin it is restored to.
4227:  const fromTree = treeBytesReader(newTree);
4228:  const fromCommit = commitBytesReader(job.upstream, job.newCommit);
4229:  const readUpstream = (key) => fromTree(key) || fromCommit(key);
4230:  const afterCopy = analyze(ctx, manifest, loadState(ctx, manifest), readUpstream);
4231:  reportBlocked(afterCopy, report);
4232:  report.equalsUpstream = overlaysEqualToUpstream(ctx, afterCopy, newTree, job.settings.roots);
4233:  for (const id of report.equalsUpstream) console.log('equals-upstream ' + id);
4234:
4235:  const first = {};
4236:  if (applyUnderLock(ctx, manifest, { isRegenerateForced: true, outcome: first, upstreamBytes: readUpstream }) !== EXIT_OK) return false;
4237:  const second = {};
4238:  if (applyUnderLock(ctx, manifest, { outcome: second, upstreamBytes: readUpstream }) !== EXIT_OK) return false;
4239:  if (second.changed !== 0) {
4240:    console.log(`MISS     a second apply changed ${second.changed} destination(s)`);
4241:    return false;
4242:  }
4243:  const finalState = loadState(ctx, manifest);
4244:  const finalAnalysis = analyze(ctx, manifest, finalState, readUpstream);
4245:  const check = runCheck(ctx, manifest, finalState, finalAnalysis.plans);
4246:  for (const problem of check.problems) console.log(`MISS     ${problem}`);
4247:  const outOfSync = finalAnalysis.records.filter((record) => record.state !== 'in-sync');
4248:  printProblems(ctx, outOfSync);
4249:  console.log(`checked=${check.checked} ok=${check.ok} miss=${check.miss}`);
4250:  return outOfSync.length === 0 && finalAnalysis.blocked.length === 0 && check.miss === 0;
4251:}
4252:
4253:function refuseRepin(lines) {
4254:  for (const line of lines) console.log(line);
4255:  console.log('RESULT: FAILED');
4256:  return EXIT_ERROR;
4257:}
4258:
4259:/**
4260: * Run one re-pin while holding the tree's lock.
4261: * @param {object} ctx Engine context of the tree being written.
4262: * @param {object} job Re-pin job.
4263: * @param {object} report Report collected for --json and report.json.
4264: * @returns {number} Exit code.
4265: */
4266:function repinUnderLock(ctx, job, report) {
4267:  const manifest = loadManifest(ctx);
4268:  const analysis = analyze(ctx, manifest, loadState(ctx, manifest), commitBytesReader(job.upstream, job.oldCommit));
4269:  const blockers = analysis.records.filter((record) => BLOCKING_STATES.has(record.state));
4270:  if (blockers.length > 0) {
4271:    for (const record of blockers) console.log(problemLine(ctx, record));
4272:    return refuseRepin([`refuse: status shows ${blockers.length} live or conflicting destination(s); resolve them before a re-pin`]);
4273:  }
4274:  // A refused overlay would block the apply that follows the copy, after the copy
4275:  // had already written, so it stops the run before the backup instead.
4276:  if (analysis.blocked.length > 0) {
4277:    const lines = analysis.blocked.flatMap((plan) => plan.problems.map((problem) => `blocked ${plan.entry.id}: refused ${problem}`));
4278:    lines.push(`refuse: status shows ${analysis.blocked.length} refused overlay(s); fix them before a re-pin`);
4279:    return refuseRepin(lines);
4280:  }
4281:  if (isUnderAny(ctx.overlayLabel, job.settings.roots)) {
4282:    return refuseRepin([`refuse: the overlay folder ${ctx.overlayLabel} sits inside a folder the re-pin copies`]);
4283:  }
4284:
4285:  job.oldBlobs = listBlobs(job.upstream, job.oldCommit, job.settings.roots);
4286:  job.newBlobs = listBlobs(job.upstream, job.newCommit, job.settings.roots);
4287:  const touched = touchedPaths(job);
4288:  const drift = unownedDrift(ctx, manifest, job, touched);
4289:  if (drift.length > 0) {
4290:    drift.push(`  move each edit into an overlay, list the path in repin.exclude, or restore it from ${job.oldPin}`);
4291:    return refuseRepin(drift);
4292:  }
4293:  const shape = treeShapeProblems(ctx.root, touched);
4294:  if (shape.length > 0) return refuseRepin(shape);
4295:  // A json-entry destination the copy leaves in place keeps its live bytes, so
4296:  // its entry set meets the new pin with the file as it is now. A refusal there
4297:  // would stop the apply after the copy had written, so it is planned here first.
4298:  const state = loadState(ctx, manifest);
4299:  const leftInPlace = manifest.overlays
4300:    .filter((entry) => entry.scope === 'json-entry' &&
4301:      !touched.copy.includes(entry.destination) && !touched.delete.includes(entry.destination))
4302:    .map((entry) => planOverlay(entry, ctx, {
4303:      acceptedSha256: state.files,
4304:      recordedItems: (state.items || {})[entry.destination] || [],
4305:      recordedSets: (state.sets || {})[entry.destination] || [],
4306:      upstreamBytes: commitBytesReader(job.upstream, job.newCommit),
4307:    }))
4308:    .filter((plan) => plan.problems.length > 0);
4309:  if (leftInPlace.length > 0) {
4310:    const lines = leftInPlace.flatMap((plan) => plan.problems.map((problem) => `blocked ${plan.entry.id}: refused ${problem}`));
4311:    lines.push(`refuse: ${leftInPlace.length} json-entry overlay(s) the copy leaves in place cannot be applied at the new pin; for a refused array, put it back to the copy at the current pin by hand, run apply, then re-pin`);
4312:    return refuseRepin(lines);
4313:  }
4314:  report.notCopied = notCopiedChanges(ctx, job);
4315:  reportNotCopied(report.notCopied, job.settings);
4316:  report.rootFiles = rootFileChanges(ctx, manifest, job);
4317:  reportRootFiles(report.rootFiles);
4318:
4319:  const scratch = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'overlay-repin-'));
4320:  try {
4321:    const newTree = extractNewCommit(job, scratch, touched);
4322:    let runId = makeRunId();
4323:    while (pathExists(path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, RUNS_FOLDER, runId))) runId = makeRunId();
4324:    const runDir = path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, RUNS_FOLDER, runId);
4325:    fs.mkdirSync(runDir, { recursive: true });
4326:    report.runId = runId;
4327:    report.copy = touched.copy.length;
4328:    report.delete = touched.delete.length;
4329:    let isClean = false;
4330:    let failure = null;
4331:    try {
4332:      const backup = writeBackup(ctx, manifest, analysis, touched, runDir, {
4333:        runId,
4334:        upstream: job.upstream,
4335:        from: job.oldCommit,
4336:        to: job.newCommit,
4337:      });
4338:      console.log(`backup of ${backup.entries.filter((entry) => entry.existed).length} paths in ${ctx.overlayLabel}/${RUNS_FOLDER}/${runId}`);
4339:      console.log(`copy ${touched.copy.length} paths, delete ${touched.delete.length} paths`);
4340:      isClean = repinWrites(ctx, job, touched, newTree, report);
4341:    } catch (error) {
4342:      failure = error;
4343:      console.log('error: ' + error.message);
4344:    }
4345:    if (!job.isStage && !isClean) console.log(rollbackCommand(ctx, runId));
4346:    // A stage that printed a blocked line or a miss is not a rehearsal that
4347:    // passed, so it gets its own word instead of the clean stage's.
4348:    report.result = job.isStage
4349:      ? (isClean ? 'STAGED' : 'BLOCKED')
4350:      : (isClean ? 'PASSED' : 'FAILED');
4351:    console.log('RESULT: ' + report.result);
4352:    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(runDir, 'report.json'), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
4353:    if (failure) return EXIT_ERROR;
4354:    return isClean ? EXIT_OK : EXIT_CONFLICT;
4355:  } finally {
4356:    fs.rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
4357:  }
4358:}
4359:
4360:/**
4361: * Why a stage folder cannot be used, or null when it can.
4362: * @param {string} root Live tree root.
4363: * @param {string} stageDir Requested stage folder.
4364: * @returns {string|null}
4365: */
4366:function stageFolderProblem(root, stageDir) {
4367:  if (isWithin(root, stageDir) || isWithin(canonicalPath(root), canonicalPath(stageDir))) {
4368:    return 'refuse: the stage folder sits inside the tree: ' + stageDir;
4369:  }
4370:  if (!pathExists(stageDir)) return null;
4371:  if (!isDirectory(stageDir)) return 'refuse: the stage folder is not a folder: ' + stageDir;
4372:  if (fs.readdirSync(stageDir).length > 0) return 'refuse: the stage folder is not empty: ' + stageDir;
4373:  return null;
4374:}
4375:
4376:/**
4377: * Copy a tree byte for byte, links kept as links. The lock file and .env files
4378: * are left out: the copy takes its own lock, and a .env file is never read.
4379: * @param {string} source Live tree root.
4380: * @param {string} destination Empty stage folder.
4381: * @param {string} lockPath The live lock file.
4382: * @returns {number} Number of .env files left out.
4383: */
4384:function copyTreeForStage(source, destination, lockPath) {
4385:  let skipped = 0;
4386:  fs.mkdirSync(destination, { recursive: true });
4387:  const pending = [''];
4388:  while (pending.length > 0) {
4389:    const relativePath = pending.pop();
4390:    for (const dirent of fs.readdirSync(path.join(source, relativePath), { withFileTypes: true })) {
4391:      const childPath = path.join(relativePath, dirent.name);
4392:      const fromPath = path.join(source, childPath);
4393:      const toPath = path.join(destination, childPath);
4394:      if (path.resolve(fromPath) === path.resolve(lockPath)) continue;
4395:      const stats = fs.lstatSync(fromPath);
4396:      if (stats.isSymbolicLink()) {
4397:        fs.symlinkSync(fs.readlinkSync(fromPath), toPath);
4398:      } else if (stats.isDirectory()) {
4399:        fs.mkdirSync(toPath);
4400:        pending.push(childPath);
4401:      } else if (stats.isFile()) {
4402:        if (isEnvName(fromPath)) {
4403:          skipped += 1;
4404:          continue;
4405:        }
4406:        fs.copyFileSync(fromPath, toPath);
4407:        fs.chmodSync(toPath, stats.mode & 0o7777);
4408:      }
4409:    }
4410:  }
4411:  return skipped;
4412:}
4413:
4414:/**
4415: * Re-pin the tree, or a staged copy of it, to a new upstream commit.
4416: * @param {object} liveCtx Engine context of the live tree.
4417: * @param {{upstream: string, to: string, stage?: string|null}} options
4418: * @param {object} report Report collected for --json and report.json.
4419: * @returns {number} Exit code.
4420: */
4421:function repinRun(liveCtx, options, report) {
4422:  const upstream = path.resolve(options.upstream);
4423:  if (!isDirectory(upstream)) return refuseRepin(['refuse: the upstream is not a folder: ' + upstream]);
4424:  const manifest = loadManifest(liveCtx);
4425:  const oldPin = manifest.pin && typeof manifest.pin.commit === 'string' ? manifest.pin.commit : '';
4426:  if (!oldPin) return refuseRepin(['refuse: the overlay manifest has no pin.commit']);
4427:  const settings = repinSettings(manifest);
4428:  const oldCommit = resolveCommit(upstream, oldPin);
4429:  if (!oldCommit) return refuseRepin([`refuse: the pinned ${oldPin} is not a commit in ${upstream}`]);
4430:  const newCommit = resolveCommit(upstream, options.to);
4431:  if (!newCommit) return refuseRepin([`refuse: ${options.to} is not a commit in ${upstream}`]);
4432:  report.from = oldCommit;
4433:  report.to = newCommit;
4434:  if (newCommit === oldCommit) {
4435:    report.result = 'PASSED';
4436:    console.log('nothing to copy');
4437:    console.log('RESULT: PASSED');
4438:    return EXIT_OK;
4439:  }
4440:  // Keep the short form the operator typed, the way the manifest records pins.
4441:  const typed = options.to.toLowerCase();
4442:  const newPin = /^[0-9a-f]{7,}$/.test(typed) && newCommit.startsWith(typed) ? typed : newCommit;
4443:  const job = { upstream, oldPin, oldCommit, newCommit, newPin, settings, isStage: Boolean(options.stage) };
4444:
4445:  let ctx = liveCtx;
4446:  if (job.isStage) {
4447:    const stageDir = path.resolve(options.stage);
4448:    const problem = stageFolderProblem(liveCtx.root, stageDir);
4449:    if (problem) return refuseRepin([problem]);
4450:    // Reading the tree while another run writes it would stage a torn copy, so a
4451:    // lock that may still have a live owner is refused here as every other mode
4452:    // refuses it. A lock whose writer has ended on this host is only leftover
4453:    // state, and a stage writes nothing in the live tree, so the line is read and
4454:    // the file left where it is for the next writing run to take over.
4455:    if (pathExists(liveCtx.lockPath)) {
4456:      const current = readBufferOrNull(liveCtx.lockPath);
4457:      const owner = (current || Buffer.from('(empty lock file)')).toString('utf8').trim();
4458:      if (!current || !isLockOwnerGone(owner)) {
4459:        return refuseRepin(['refuse: the overlay lock is held: ' + owner, '  remove ' + liveCtx.lockPath + ' by hand once that run has ended']);
4460:      }
4461:      console.log('note: the overlay lock belongs to a run that has ended: ' + owner);
4462:    }
4463:    const skipped = copyTreeForStage(liveCtx.root, stageDir, liveCtx.lockPath);
4464:    console.log('staged a copy of the tree in ' + stageDir + (skipped > 0 ? ` (${skipped} .env file(s) left out)` : ''));
4465:    ctx = createContext(stageDir);
4466:    report.stage = stageDir;
4467:  }
4468:  return withLock(ctx, 'overlay-engine repin', () => repinUnderLock(ctx, job, report));
4469:}
4470:
4471:/**
4472: * Restore every path a run backed up and remove what the run created.
4473: * Every backed-up file is checked against its recorded hash before anything is
4474: * written, so a damaged backup restores nothing.
4475: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
4476: * @param {string} runDir The run folder.
4477: * @param {object} backup Parsed backup.json.
4478: * @returns {number} Exit code.
4479: */
4480:function rollbackUnderLock(ctx, runDir, backup) {
4481:  const root = ctx.root;
4482:  const restored = [];
4483:  const problems = [];
4484:  for (const entry of backup.entries) {
4485:    const relativePath = normalizedRelative(entry.path, 'backup entry');
4486:    relativeKey(root, path.join(root, relativePath));
4487:    if (!entry.existed) continue;
4488:    if (entry.kind === 'symlink') {
4489:      if (typeof entry.target !== 'string' || sha256(Buffer.from(entry.target)) !== entry.sha256) {
4490:        problems.push(`refuse: the backed-up link ${relativePath} does not match its recorded sha256`);
4491:      }
4492:      restored.push({ entry, relativePath });
4493:      continue;
4494:    }
4495:    const buffer = readBufferOrNull(path.join(runDir, 'backup', relativePath));
4496:    if (!buffer || sha256(buffer) !== entry.sha256) {
4497:      problems.push(`refuse: the backup of ${relativePath} does not match its recorded sha256`);
4498:      continue;
4499:    }
4500:    restored.push({ entry, relativePath, buffer });
4501:  }
4502:  if (problems.length > 0) return refuseRepin(problems);
4503:
4504:  let removed = 0;
4505:  const created = backup.entries.filter((entry) => !entry.existed)
4506:    .map((entry) => normalizedRelative(entry.path, 'backup entry'))
4507:    .sort((left, right) => right.split('/').length - left.split('/').length || right.localeCompare(left));
4508:  for (const relativePath of created) {
4509:    if (hasLinkedAncestor(root, relativePath)) continue;
4510:    if (removePath(path.join(root, relativePath))) removed += 1;
4511:  }
4512:  for (const relativePath of backup.createdDirs || []) {
4513:    const dir = path.join(root, normalizedRelative(relativePath, 'backup folder'));
4514:    if (isDirectory(dir) && fs.readdirSync(dir).length === 0) fs.rmdirSync(dir);
4515:  }
4516:
4517:  restored.sort((left, right) => left.relativePath.split('/').length - right.relativePath.split('/').length);
4518:  for (const { entry, relativePath, buffer } of restored) {
4519:    const filePath = path.join(root, relativePath);
4520:    // A parent the run turned into a file or link is cleared, never written through.
4521:    const parts = relativePath.split('/');
4522:    for (let depth = 1; depth < parts.length; depth += 1) {
4523:      const ancestor = path.join(root, ...parts.slice(0, depth));
4524:      if (pathExists(ancestor) && !isDirectory(ancestor)) fs.unlinkSync(ancestor);
4525:    }
4526:    if (isDirectory(filePath)) fs.rmSync(filePath, { recursive: true, force: true });
4527:    if (entry.kind === 'symlink') {
4528:      ensureSymlink(filePath, entry.target);
4529:      if (fs.readlinkSync(filePath) !== entry.target) problems.push(`restored link ${relativePath} has the wrong target`);
4530:      continue;
4531:    }
4532:    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
4533:    removePath(filePath);
4534:    fs.writeFileSync(filePath, buffer);
4535:    fs.chmodSync(filePath, entry.mode);
4536:    const written = readBufferOrNull(filePath);
4537:    if (!written || sha256(written) !== entry.sha256) problems.push(`restored ${relativePath} does not match its recorded sha256`);
4538:  }
4539:  for (const problem of problems) console.log('MISS     ' + problem);
4540:  console.log(`restored ${restored.length} paths, removed ${removed} paths`);
4541:  console.log(problems.length > 0 ? 'RESULT: FAILED' : 'RESULT: PASSED');
4542:  return problems.length > 0 ? EXIT_ERROR : EXIT_OK;
4543:}
4544:
4545:function rollbackRun(ctx, runId) {
4546:  if (!RUN_ID_PATTERN.test(runId)) return refuseRepin(['refuse: not a run id: ' + runId]);
4547:  const runDir = path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, RUNS_FOLDER, runId);
4548:  const buffer = readBufferOrNull(path.join(runDir, 'backup.json'));
4549:  if (!buffer) return refuseRepin([`refuse: no backup for run ${runId} in ${ctx.overlayLabel}/${RUNS_FOLDER}`]);
4550:  const backup = JSON.parse(buffer.toString('utf8'));
4551:  if (backup.schemaVersion !== 1 || !Array.isArray(backup.entries)) {
4552:    return refuseRepin([`refuse: the backup for run ${runId} has an unsupported shape`]);
4553:  }
4554:  return withLock(ctx, 'overlay-engine repin rollback', () => rollbackUnderLock(ctx, runDir, backup));
4555:}
4556:
4557:/**
4558: * Run a re-pin or a rollback, collecting every printed line for the report.
4559: * With --json the lines go into the report instead of the terminal.
4560: * @param {object} ctx Engine context of the live tree.
4561: * @param {{upstream?: string, to?: string, stage?: string|null, rollback?: string|null, isJson?: boolean}} options
4562: * @returns {number} Exit code.
4563: */
4564:function commandRepin(ctx, options) {
4565:  const report = {
4566:    result: null,
4567:    mode: options.rollback ? 'rollback' : (options.stage ? 'stage' : 'live'),
4568:    runId: options.rollback || null,
4569:    copy: 0,
4570:    delete: 0,
4571:    blocked: [],
4572:    equalsUpstream: [],
4573:    notCopied: [],
4574:    rootFiles: [],
4575:    stageSkipped: null,
4576:    lines: [],
4577:  };
4578:  const original = console.log;
4579:  console.log = (...args) => {
4580:    const line = args.join(' ');
4581:    report.lines.push(line);
4582:    if (!options.isJson) original(line);
4583:  };
4584:  let code;
4585:  try {
4586:    code = options.rollback ? rollbackRun(ctx, options.rollback) : repinRun(ctx, options, report);
4587:    // A lock refusal comes from withLock, which prints no result line of its own.
4588:    const last = report.lines[report.lines.length - 1];
4589:    if (!last || !last.startsWith('RESULT: ')) console.log('RESULT: ' + (code === EXIT_OK ? 'PASSED' : 'FAILED'));
4590:  } finally {
4591:    console.log = original;
4592:  }
4593:  if (report.result === null) {
4594:    const last = report.lines.filter((line) => line.startsWith('RESULT: ')).pop();
4595:    report.result = last ? last.slice('RESULT: '.length) : (code === EXIT_OK ? 'PASSED' : 'FAILED');
4596:  }
4597:  if (options.isJson) console.log(JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));
4598:  return code;
4599:}
4600:
4601:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
4602:// 17. STRICT WRITES
4603://
4604:// Off unless the manifest sets "strictWrites": true, so a repository that
4605:// vendors nothing is never checked. A vendoring project turns it on because the
4606:// guard above only knows the paths an overlay already owns. A new file in the
4607:// vendored tree has no upstream copy for a re-pin to carry, and an edit to an
