4608:// upstream file no overlay owns is overwritten by the next re-pin. Both are
4609:// found weeks later, at the re-pin, unless the write is refused when it is made.
4610:// The same test runs three times: at the edit (--guard), at the commit
4611:// (pre-commit) and when a path is brought under an overlay (apply --adopt).
4612:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
4613:
4614:function isStrictWrites(manifest) {
4615:  if (manifest.strictWrites !== undefined && typeof manifest.strictWrites !== 'boolean') {
4616:    throw new Error('the manifest strictWrites value must be true or false');
4617:  }
4618:  return manifest.strictWrites === true;
4619:}
4620:
4621:// `*` matches inside one path segment. `**` on its own matches across segments,
4622:// while `**/` matches zero or more whole segments, so a glob naming a file in
4623:// any folder never also matches a longer file name beside it.
4624:function globToRegExp(glob) {
4625:  let pattern = '';
4626:  for (let index = 0; index < glob.length; index += 1) {
4627:    const char = glob[index];
4628:    if (char === '*' && glob[index + 1] === '*') {
4629:      const isWholeSegments = glob[index + 2] === '/';
4630:      pattern += isWholeSegments ? '(?:.*/)?' : '.*';
4631:      index += 1;
4632:      if (isWholeSegments) index += 1;
4633:    } else if (char === '*') {
4634:      pattern += '[^/]*';
4635:    } else {
4636:      pattern += char.replace(/[.+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
4637:    }
4638:  }
4639:  return new RegExp('^' + pattern + '$');
4640:}
4641:
4642:/**
4643: * Read what the write checks need from the manifest: the vendored roots, the
4644: * named root files, the `repin.exclude` paths, and the `generated` globs. A
4645: * generated path is written by one of the project's generators, so no overlay
4646: * can own it and no check refuses it. An excluded path is one a re-pin never
4647: * copies, so a write there cannot be lost and no check refuses it either.
4648: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
4649: * @returns {{roots: string[], rootFiles: string[], exclude: string[], isGenerated: (key: string) => boolean}}
4650: */
4651:function writeCheckSettings(manifest) {
4652:  const { roots, rootFiles, exclude } = repinSettings(manifest);
4653:  const generated = manifest.generated === undefined ? [] : manifest.generated;
4654:  if (!Array.isArray(generated) || generated.some((glob) => typeof glob !== 'string' || glob === '')) {
4655:    throw new Error('the manifest generated list must be a list of glob strings');
4656:  }
4657:  const patterns = generated.map(globToRegExp);
4658:  return { roots, rootFiles, exclude, isGenerated: (key) => patterns.some((pattern) => pattern.test(key)) };
4659:}
4660:
4661:/**
4662: * Say whether a re-pin would copy over a key, which is the only reason a write
4663: * to it goes through an overlay. An excluded path is the project's own, because
4664: * the copy never reaches it, so a refusal there would name a lost edit that
4665: * cannot happen.
4666: * @param {string} key Path relative to the tree.
4667: * @param {object} settings Result of the write checks' manifest read.
4668: * @returns {boolean}
4669: */
4670:function isVendoredKey(key, settings) {
4671:  if (isUnderAny(key, settings.exclude)) return false;
4672:  return settings.rootFiles.includes(key) || settings.roots.some((root) => key.startsWith(root + '/'));
4673:}
4674:
4675:function isIgnoredByGit(root, filePath) {
4676:  return runGit(root, ['check-ignore', '-q', '--', filePath]).status === 0;
4677:}
4678:
4679:// A path as a command line takes it: quoted when it holds a space or another
4680:// character a shell would split or expand, so a printed command can be pasted.
4681:function shellPath(key) {
4682:  return /^[A-Za-z0-9._\/-]+$/.test(key) ? key : '"' + key.replace(/(["\\$`])/g, '\\$1') + '"';
4683:}
4684:
4685:// Where adopt keeps a destination inside the overlay folder: the same path
4686:// with the leading dot dropped, so the copy is not a hidden folder.
4687:function adoptSource(key) {
4688:  return key.replace(/^\.+/, '');
4689:}
4690:
4691:// The highest missing folder above a new file, at least two segments deep, so
4692:// a new skill is adopted as one directory entry rather than one entry per file.
4693:function adoptionUnit(root, filePath) {
4694:  let unit = filePath;
4695:  let parent = path.dirname(filePath);
4696:  while (isWithin(root, parent) && !pathExists(parent) &&
4697:      path.relative(root, parent).split(path.sep).length >= 2) {
4698:    unit = parent;
4699:    parent = path.dirname(parent);
4700:  }
4701:  return unit;
4702:}
4703:
4704:// The directory or skill-root entry whose destination folder holds a key.
4705:function folderEntryHolding(manifest, key) {
4706:  for (const entry of manifest.overlays) {
4707:    if (entry.scope !== 'directory' && entry.scope !== 'skill-root') continue;
4708:    const destination = path.posix.normalize(entry.destination);
4709:    if (key.startsWith(destination + '/')) return { entry, relative: key.slice(destination.length + 1) };
4710:  }
4711:  return null;
4712:}
4713:
4714:/**
4715: * Say why a write under a vendored root must go through an overlay first, or
4716: * return null to allow it. The caller has already refused every existing path
4717: * an overlay owns, so what reaches here is unowned, new, generated or managed.
4718: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
4719: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
4720: * @param {string} inputPath Path to test, relative to the cwd or absolute.
4721: * @returns {string|null} One refusal line, or null.
4722: */
4723:function strictWriteRefusal(ctx, manifest, inputPath) {
4724:  if (!isStrictWrites(manifest)) return null;
4725:  const root = canonicalPath(ctx.root);
4726:  const filePath = canonicalPath(path.resolve(process.cwd(), inputPath));
4727:  if (!isWithin(root, filePath) || filePath === root) return null;
4728:  const key = relativeKey(root, filePath);
4729:  const settings = writeCheckSettings(manifest);
4730:  if (!isVendoredKey(key, settings) || isDirectory(filePath)) return null;
4731:  if (settings.isGenerated(key) || isIgnoredByGit(root, filePath)) return null;
4732:
4733:  const overlay = ctx.overlayLabel + '/';
4734:  const isLive = pathExists(filePath);
4735:  const holder = folderEntryHolding(manifest, key);
4736:  if (managedPathTest(ctx, manifest)(key)) {
4737:    // A link or a regenerated output is written by apply, and an existing owned
4738:    // file was refused before this ran. Only a new file in an owned folder is left.
4739:    if (isLive || !holder) return null;
4740:    return `refuse: ${key} is a new file inside overlay ${holder.entry.id}; create it as ` +
4741:      `${overlay}${holder.entry.source}/${holder.relative}, then run ${ENGINE_COMMAND} apply`;
4742:  }
4743:  if (holder) {
4744:    // Inside a skill-root folder but not on its replace list: the entry keeps
4745:    // the upstream file as found, and only that list can hand it to the overlay.
4746:    return `refuse: ${key} is ${isLive ? 'an upstream file overlay ' + holder.entry.id + ' keeps as it is' :
4747:      'a new file overlay ' + holder.entry.id + ' does not replace'}; add ${holder.relative} to that entry's ` +
4748:      `replace list${isLive ? ', and take it off the retained list if it is there' : ''}, keep its copy at ` +
4749:      `${overlay}${holder.entry.source}/${holder.relative}, then run ${ENGINE_COMMAND} apply`;
4750:  }
4751:  if (isLive) {
4752:    return `refuse: ${key} is a vendored file no overlay owns, so a re-pin would overwrite this edit; run ` +
4753:      `${ENGINE_COMMAND} apply --adopt ${shellPath(key)}, then edit ${overlay}${adoptSource(key)}`;
4754:  }
4755:  const unitKey = relativeKey(root, adoptionUnit(root, filePath));
4756:  return `refuse: ${key} is a new file in the vendored tree, which a re-pin would not carry; create it as ` +
4757:    `${overlay}${adoptSource(key)}, then run ${ENGINE_COMMAND} apply --adopt ${shellPath(unitKey)}`;
4758:}
4759:
4760:function pathsOverlap(left, right) {
4761:  return left === right || left.startsWith(right + '/') || right.startsWith(left + '/');
4762:}
4763:
4764:// Why a destination or its overlay source collides with an existing entry.
4765:function adoptOverlap(ctx, manifest, key, source) {
4766:  for (const entry of manifest.overlays) {
4767:    const destinations = entry.scope === 'remove-root' ? (entry.paths || []) : [entry.destination];
4768:    if (destinations.some((destination) => typeof destination === 'string' &&
4769:        pathsOverlap(path.posix.normalize(destination), key))) {
4770:      return `${key} overlaps overlay ${entry.id}; change that entry or its source instead`;
4771:    }
4772:    const sources = ['source', 'base', 'fragment'].map((field) => entry[field])
4773:      .filter((value) => typeof value === 'string');
4774:    if (sources.some((value) => pathsOverlap(path.posix.normalize(value), source))) {
4775:      return `${ctx.overlayLabel}/${source} overlaps the source of overlay ${entry.id}`;
4776:    }
4777:  }
4778:  return null;
4779:}
4780:
4781:function isRefusedAdoptName(filePath) {
4782:  if (isEnvName(filePath)) return true;
4783:  return isTokenName(filePath) && !TOKEN_NAME_ADOPT_EXTENSIONS.has(path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase());
4784:}
4785:
4786:function adoptionId(manifest, key) {
4787:  const slug = key.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '').toLowerCase();
4788:  const ids = new Set(manifest.overlays.map((entry) => entry.id));
4789:  let id = 'adopted-' + slug;
4790:  for (let suffix = 2; ids.has(id); suffix += 1) id = `adopted-${slug}-${suffix}`;
4791:  return id;
4792:}
4793:
4794:/**
4795: * Append one entry to the manifest's overlays list, changing no other text, so
4796: * the file keeps its formatting and every key the engine does not know.
4797: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
4798: * @param {object} entry Manifest overlay entry.
4799: */
4800:function appendOverlayEntry(ctx, entry) {
4801:  const text = fs.readFileSync(ctx.manifestPath, 'utf8');
4802:  const list = nodeAtPath(parseJsonSource(text), ['overlays']);
4803:  if (list.type !== 'array') throw new Error('the manifest overlays value is not a list');
4804:  const last = list.items[list.items.length - 1];
4805:  const indent = (last && indentBefore(text, last.start)) || '    ';
4806:  const body = JSON.stringify(entry, null, 2).split('\n').join('\n' + indent);
4807:  const updated = last
4808:    ? text.slice(0, last.end) + ',\n' + indent + body + text.slice(last.end)
4809:    : text.slice(0, list.start) + '[\n' + indent + body + '\n  ]' + text.slice(list.end);
4810:  fs.writeFileSync(ctx.manifestPath, updated);
4811:}
4812:
4813:/**
4814: * Bring one vendored path under an overlay; the caller holds the lock.
4815: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
4816: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
4817: * @param {string} inputPath Path to adopt, relative to the tree, the cwd or absolute.
4818: * @returns {number} 0 when adopted and applied, 1 when the apply is blocked, 2 on refusal.
4819: */
4820:function adoptUnderLock(ctx, manifest, inputPath) {
4821:  const refuse = (message) => {
4822:    console.log('refuse: ' + message);
4823:    return EXIT_ERROR;
4824:  };
4825:  const root = canonicalPath(ctx.root);
4826:  const settings = writeCheckSettings(manifest);
4827:  // A path under repin.exclude would be vendored but for that list. A re-pin never
4828:  // copies it, so it needs no overlay, yet a project may keep its copy in the
4829:  // overlay folder to carry it. Adopt takes such a path only from that copy.
4830:  const isExcludedVendoredKey = (key) => isUnderAny(key, settings.exclude) &&
4831:    isVendoredKey(key, { ...settings, exclude: [] });
4832:  const hasOverlayCopy = (key) => pathExists(path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, ...adoptSource(key).split('/')));
4833:  const candidates = captureCandidates(root, inputPath)
4834:    .map((candidate) => canonicalPathWithoutLeaf(candidate))
4835:    .filter((candidate) => isWithin(root, candidate) && candidate !== root);
4836:  const destinationPath = candidates.find((candidate) => {
4837:    const key = relativeKey(root, candidate);
4838:    return isVendoredKey(key, settings) || (isExcludedVendoredKey(key) && hasOverlayCopy(key));
4839:  });
4840:  const excludedPath = candidates.find((candidate) => isExcludedVendoredKey(relativeKey(root, candidate)));
4841:  if (!destinationPath && excludedPath) {
4842:    const key = relativeKey(root, excludedPath);
4843:    return refuse(`${key} is under repin.exclude, so adopt takes it only from its overlay copy at ` +
4844:      `${ctx.overlayLabel}/${adoptSource(key)}, which does not exist; move or create the file there, then adopt again`);
4845:  }
4846:  if (!destinationPath) {
4847:    return refuse(`${inputPath} is not under a vendored root (${settings.roots.join(', ')}) and is not a file ` +
4848:      'named in repin.rootFiles');
4849:  }
4850:  const key = relativeKey(root, destinationPath);
4851:  const source = adoptSource(key);
4852:  const sourcePath = path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, ...source.split('/'));
4853:  const shownSource = ctx.overlayLabel + '/' + source;
4854:  // A link inside the overlay folder would take the copy somewhere the manifest
4855:  // entry does not name, so the source has to resolve inside the folder itself.
4856:  if (!isWithin(canonicalPath(ctx.overlayRoot), canonicalPath(sourcePath))) {
4857:    return refuse(`${shownSource} resolves outside the overlay folder through a link; adopt writes only inside ${ctx.overlayLabel}`);
4858:  }
4859:  if (settings.isGenerated(key)) {
4860:    return refuse(`${key} is on the manifest's generated list; change what it is generated from instead`);
4861:  }
4862:  const overlap = adoptOverlap(ctx, manifest, key, source);
4863:  if (overlap) return refuse(overlap);
4864:
4865:  const isLive = pathExists(destinationPath);
4866:  const hasSource = pathExists(sourcePath);
4867:  if (isLive && hasSource) return refuse(`both ${key} and ${shownSource} exist; keep one, then adopt again`);
4868:  if (!isLive && !hasSource) return refuse(`nothing to adopt; create ${shownSource} first`);
4869:
4870:  const fromPath = hasSource ? sourcePath : destinationPath;
4871:  const files = isDirectory(fromPath) ? listTreePaths(fromPath) : [fromPath];
4872:  for (const filePath of files) {
4873:    if (isRefusedAdoptName(filePath)) {
4874:      return refuse(`${path.basename(filePath)} is a protected path and cannot be an overlay`);
4875:    }
4876:    if (!isFile(filePath)) {
4877:      return refuse(`${toPosix(path.relative(fromPath, filePath)) || path.basename(filePath)} is not a regular file; ` +
4878:        'adopt takes files and folders only');
4879:    }
4880:  }
4881:
4882:  // An upstream file becomes a file entry pinned to upstream's bytes, so the
4883:  // next re-pin reports upstream-moved instead of keeping or losing the copy
4884:  // without a word.
4885:  let pinnedSha256 = null;
4886:  if (isLive) {
4887:    const pin = manifest.pin || {};
4888:    const readUpstream = pinnedUpstreamBytes(manifest);
4889:    if (!readUpstream || resolveCommit(pin.checkout, pin.commit) === null) {
4890:      return refuse(`cannot read pin.commit in the upstream checkout pin.checkout names, to tell whether ${key} ` +
4891:        'is upstream\'s; adopt it where that checkout exists');
4892:    }
4893:    const shipped = files.map((filePath) => relativeKey(root, filePath))
4894:      .filter((fileKey) => readUpstream(fileKey) !== null);
4895:    if (shipped.length > 0 && isDirectory(destinationPath)) {
4896:      return refuse(`upstream ships ${shipped.length} file(s) under ${key}, such as ${shipped.sort()[0]}; ` +
4897:        'adopt each upstream file on its own');
4898:    }
4899:    if (shipped.length > 0) pinnedSha256 = sha256(readUpstream(key));
4900:    for (const filePath of files) {
4901:      copyUpstreamEntry(filePath, path.join(sourcePath, path.relative(destinationPath, filePath)));
4902:    }
4903:  }
4904:
4905:  const isFolder = isDirectory(sourcePath);
4906:  const entry = { id: adoptionId(manifest, key), scope: isFolder ? 'directory' : 'file', source, destination: key };
4907:  // A directory walk refuses a token-named file the entry does not list, so
4908:  // the names adopt accepted as source code are recorded with the entry.
4909:  const tokenNamed = isFolder
4910:    ? listTreePaths(sourcePath).filter(isTokenName).map((filePath) => toPosix(path.relative(sourcePath, filePath)))
4911:    : [];
4912:  if (tokenNamed.length > 0) entry.protectedPaths = tokenNamed.sort();
4913:  if (pinnedSha256) entry.pinnedSha256 = pinnedSha256;
4914:  entry.description = pinnedSha256
4915:    ? 'Overlay of an upstream file. pinnedSha256 is the upstream copy it was taken from.'
4916:    : 'Project addition with no upstream copy. This entry is what carries it across a re-pin.';
4917:  appendOverlayEntry(ctx, entry);
4918:  console.log(`adopted ${key} as ${shownSource} (${entry.scope} entry ${entry.id})`);
4919:  return applyUnderLock(ctx, loadManifest(ctx));
4920:}
4921:
4922:/**
4923: * Bring one vendored path under an overlay, then apply.
4924: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
4925: * @param {string} inputPath Path to adopt.
4926: * @param {{lock?: object}} [options] A lock the caller already holds.
4927: * @returns {number} Exit code.
4928: */
4929:function commandAdopt(ctx, inputPath, options = {}) {
4930:  const manifest = loadManifest(ctx);
4931:  return withLock(ctx, 'overlay-engine adopt', () => adoptUnderLock(ctx, manifest, inputPath), options);
4932:}
4933:
4934:function gitTextOrNull(cwd, args) {
4935:  const result = runGit(cwd, args);
4936:  return result.status === 0 ? result.stdout.toString('utf8') : null;
4937:}
4938:
4939:function gitBytesOrNull(cwd, args) {
4940:  const result = runGit(cwd, args);
4941:  return result.status === 0 ? result.stdout : null;
4942:}
4943:
4944:/**
4945: * Compare two file contents as text, ignoring a CRLF versus LF line-ending
4946: * difference. A NUL byte marks a binary file, which has no line endings to
4947: * ignore, so such a pair never matches here.
4948: * @param {Buffer} left One file's bytes.
4949: * @param {Buffer} right The other file's bytes.
4950: * @returns {boolean} Whether both hold the same lines.
4951: */
4952:function isSameText(left, right) {
4953:  if (left.includes(0) || right.includes(0)) return false;
4954:  // Latin-1 maps one byte to one character, so nothing is lost or reshaped.
4955:  return left.toString('latin1').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n') === right.toString('latin1').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
4956:}
4957:
4958:function pinCommitOf(manifestText) {
4959:  try {
4960:    return JSON.parse(manifestText).pin.commit;
4961:  } catch {
4962:    return undefined;
4963:  }
4964:}
4965:
4966:/**
4967: * List the staged changes under a vendored root that no overlay owns. A write
4968: * hook sees only an agent's write tools, and a script, a shell edit or a
4969: * person reaches the tree without one, so the commit is checked too. A staged
4970: * copy equal to upstream's is upstream's own file, and a commit that moves the
4971: * pin is a re-pin, which the re-pin's own drift scan already judged.
4972: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
4973: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
4974: * @returns {{status: string, key: string}[]} One record per unowned staged path.
4975: */
4976:function stagedUnownedChanges(ctx, manifest) {
4977:  const root = canonicalPath(ctx.root);
4978:  const top = gitTextOrNull(root, ['rev-parse', '--show-toplevel']);
4979:  if (top === null) return [];
4980:  const topLevel = canonicalPath(top.trim());
4981:  const prefix = toPosix(path.relative(topLevel, root));
4982:  const listing = gitTextOrNull(topLevel, ['diff', '--cached', '--name-status', '--no-renames', '-z']);
4983:  if (!listing) return [];
4984:  const settings = writeCheckSettings(manifest);
4985:  const parts = listing.split('\0').filter(Boolean);
4986:  const changes = [];
4987:  for (let index = 0; index + 1 < parts.length; index += 2) {
4988:    const file = parts[index + 1];
4989:    if (prefix && !file.startsWith(prefix + '/')) continue;
4990:    const key = prefix ? file.slice(prefix.length + 1) : file;
4991:    if (isVendoredKey(key, settings)) changes.push({ status: parts[index][0], file, key });
4992:  }
4993:  if (changes.length === 0) return [];
4994:
4995:  const manifestFile = path.posix.join(prefix, toPosix(path.relative(ctx.root, ctx.manifestPath)));
4996:  const stagedPin = pinCommitOf(gitTextOrNull(topLevel, ['show', ':' + manifestFile]));
4997:  const headPin = pinCommitOf(gitTextOrNull(topLevel, ['show', 'HEAD:' + manifestFile]));
4998:  if (stagedPin !== undefined && headPin !== undefined && stagedPin !== headPin) return [];
4999:
5000:  const pin = manifest.pin || {};
5001:  const hasCheckout = typeof pin.checkout === 'string' && typeof pin.commit === 'string' && isDirectory(pin.checkout);
5002:  const isManaged = managedPathTest(ctx, manifest);
5003:  return changes.filter((change) => {
5004:    if (isManaged(change.key) || settings.isGenerated(change.key)) return false;
5005:    if (!hasCheckout) return true;
5006:    const upstreamBlob = gitTextOrNull(pin.checkout, ['rev-parse', '--verify', '--quiet', `${pin.commit}:${change.key}`]);
5007:    // Deleting a file upstream never shipped removes the project's own stray file.
5008:    if (change.status === 'D') return upstreamBlob !== null;
5009:    const stagedBlob = gitTextOrNull(topLevel, ['rev-parse', '--verify', '--quiet', ':' + change.file]);
5010:    if (upstreamBlob !== null && stagedBlob !== null && upstreamBlob.trim() === stagedBlob.trim()) return false;
5011:    if (upstreamBlob === null || stagedBlob === null) return true;
5012:    // A project may normalise line endings on commit, so the same text can reach
5013:    // the index as different bytes than upstream stored. Compare the contents
5014:    // before calling a staged copy a change.
5015:    const upstreamBytes = gitBytesOrNull(pin.checkout, ['cat-file', 'blob', `${pin.commit}:${change.key}`]);
5016:    const stagedBytes = gitBytesOrNull(topLevel, ['cat-file', 'blob', ':' + change.file]);
5017:    return !(upstreamBytes !== null && stagedBytes !== null && isSameText(upstreamBytes, stagedBytes));
5018:  }).map(({ status, key }) => ({ status, key }));
5019:}
5020:
5021:/**
5022: * List the blobs upstream holds at one commit, under the given roots and root
5023: * files.
5024: * @param {string} upstream Upstream checkout.
5025: * @param {string} commit Commit id.
5026: * @param {string[]} pathspecs Vendored roots and root files to list.
5027: * @returns {Map<string, {mode: string, object: string}>} One record per path,
5028: *   keyed by the path relative to the tree. A submodule is left out: the tree
5029: *   carries no bytes for it.
5030: */
5031:function lsTreeBlobs(upstream, commit, pathspecs) {
5032:  const blobs = new Map();
5033:  for (const record of gitText(upstream, ['ls-tree', '-r', '-z', commit, '--', ...pathspecs]).split('\0')) {
5034:    const tab = record.indexOf('\t');
5035:    if (tab < 0) continue;
5036:    const [mode, type, object] = record.slice(0, tab).split(' ');
5037:    if (type !== 'blob') continue;
5038:    blobs.set(record.slice(tab + 1), { mode, object });
5039:  }
5040:  return blobs;
5041:}
5042:
5043:/**
5044: * Hash a batch of tree files in one git run, so an audit of a whole vendored
5045: * tree costs one process rather than one per file. Every filter is skipped, so
5046: * each id names the bytes on disk, which is what a re-pin compares.
5047: * @param {string} root Tree root.
5048: * @param {string} format The checkout's object format, used for a file git will
5049: *   not hash on its own.
5050: * @param {string[]} keys Paths relative to the tree root.
5051: * @returns {Map<string, string>} One object id per key.
5052: */
5053:function blobIdsInTree(root, format, keys) {
5054:  const ids = new Map();
5055:  if (keys.length === 0) return ids;
5056:  const result = spawnSync('git', ['-C', root, 'hash-object', '--no-filters', '--stdin-paths'], {
5057:    input: Buffer.from(keys.join('\n') + '\n'), maxBuffer: GIT_MAX_BUFFER,
5058:  });
5059:  if (result.error) throw result.error;
5060:  const lines = result.status === 0 ? result.stdout.toString('utf8').split('\n') : [];
5061:  for (let index = 0; index < keys.length; index += 1) {
5062:    // A batch git refuses, and any file that moved under it, is hashed from the
5063:    // bytes here rather than reported as drift.
5064:    ids.set(keys[index], lines[index] || gitBlobId(format, fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, keys[index]))));
5065:  }
5066:  return ids;
5067:}
5068:
5069:/**
5070: * List the paths git tracks in a tree, relative to that tree's own root. The
5071: * repository may hold the tree in a folder of its own, so the listing is mapped
5072: * back onto the tree.
5073: * @param {string} root Tree root.
5074: * @returns {string[]} Paths relative to the tree root.
5075: */
5076:function trackedTreeKeys(root) {
5077:  // A temporary folder sits behind a link on some systems, so the tree's own
5078:  // path is resolved before it is compared with the repository's top level.
5079:  const projectRoot = canonicalPath(root);
5080:  const top = gitTextOrNull(projectRoot, ['rev-parse', '--show-toplevel']);
5081:  if (top === null) return [];
5082:  const topLevel = canonicalPath(top.trim());
5083:  const prefix = toPosix(path.relative(topLevel, projectRoot));
5084:  const listing = gitTextOrNull(topLevel, ['ls-files', '-z']);
5085:  if (!listing) return [];
5086:  const keys = [];
5087:  for (const file of listing.split('\0').filter(Boolean)) {
5088:    if (!prefix) {
5089:      keys.push(file);
5090:    } else if (file.startsWith(prefix + '/')) {
5091:      keys.push(file.slice(prefix.length + 1));
5092:    }
5093:  }
5094:  return keys;
5095:}
5096:
5097:/**
5098: * Compare the vendored tree with upstream at the pin, for the paths no overlay
5099: * owns. The strict write checks judge a write as it is made, so a vendored file
5100: * that already differs from upstream, or an upstream file the tree lacks, waits
5101: * for a re-pin to refuse the run unless something reports it first.
5102: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
5103: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
5104: * @returns {{isAvailable: boolean, findings: {kind: string, path: string}[]}}
5105: *   One finding per unowned path that drifted, sorted by path. `isAvailable` is
5106: *   false when the manifest names no checkout that holds the pin.
5107: */
5108:function auditVendoredTree(ctx, manifest) {
5109:  const pin = manifest.pin || {};
5110:  if (typeof pin.checkout !== 'string' || typeof pin.commit !== 'string' || !isDirectory(pin.checkout)) {
5111:    return { isAvailable: false, findings: [] };
5112:  }
5113:  // A folder that is not a repository, or one that never fetched the pinned
5114:  // commit, holds no copy to compare against. The audit reports itself skipped
5115:  // instead of letting the unreadable pin fail the whole status run.
5116:  if (resolveCommit(pin.checkout, pin.commit) === null) return { isAvailable: false, findings: [] };
5117:  const settings = writeCheckSettings(manifest);
5118:  const isManaged = managedPathTest(ctx, manifest);
5119:  // A generated path is one of the project's own outputs and a managed one is
5120:  // written back by apply, so neither can hold drift a re-pin would lose.
5121:  const isJudged = (key) => isVendoredKey(key, settings) && !settings.isGenerated(key) &&
5122:    !isManaged(key) && !isEnvName(key);
5123:  const format = objectFormat(pin.checkout);
5124:  const upstream = lsTreeBlobs(pin.checkout, pin.commit, [...settings.roots, ...settings.rootFiles]);
5125:  const findings = [];
5126:  const regular = [];
5127:
5128:  for (const [key, blob] of upstream) {
5129:    if (!isJudged(key)) continue;
5130:    const filePath = path.join(ctx.root, key);
5131:    let stats;
5132:    try {
5133:      stats = fs.lstatSync(filePath);
5134:    } catch (error) {
5135:      if (!error || (error.code !== 'ENOENT' && error.code !== 'ENOTDIR')) throw error;
5136:      findings.push({ kind: 'missing', path: key });
5137:      continue;
5138:    }
5139:    const isUpstreamLink = blob.mode === '120000';
5140:    if (stats.isDirectory() || isUpstreamLink !== stats.isSymbolicLink()) {
5141:      findings.push({ kind: 'differs', path: key });
5142:      continue;
5143:    }
5144:    if (isUpstreamLink) {
5145:      const target = gitBytesOrNull(pin.checkout, ['cat-file', 'blob', blob.object]);
5146:      if (target === null || !target.equals(Buffer.from(fs.readlinkSync(filePath)))) {
5147:        findings.push({ kind: 'differs', path: key });
5148:      }
5149:      continue;
5150:    }
5151:    regular.push({ key, blob, filePath });
5152:  }
5153:
5154:  const treeIds = blobIdsInTree(ctx.root, format, regular.map((entry) => entry.key));
5155:  for (const entry of regular) {
5156:    if (treeIds.get(entry.key) === entry.blob.object) continue;
5157:    // A re-pin refuses a path whose bytes are not upstream's bytes, so the same
5158:    // comparison runs here. An audit that read CRLF as LF would pass a tree the
5159:    // next re-pin refuses.
5160:    const upstreamBytes = gitBytesOrNull(pin.checkout, ['cat-file', 'blob', entry.blob.object]);
5161:    if (upstreamBytes !== null && upstreamBytes.equals(fs.readFileSync(entry.filePath))) continue;
5162:    findings.push({ kind: 'differs', path: entry.key });
5163:  }
5164:
5165:  for (const key of trackedTreeKeys(ctx.root)) {
5166:    if (upstream.has(key) || !isJudged(key)) continue;
5167:    // A tracked path deleted from the working tree is the other side's drift.
5168:    if (!pathExists(path.join(ctx.root, key))) continue;
5169:    findings.push({ kind: 'extra', path: key });
5170:  }
5171:  findings.sort((left, right) => (left.path < right.path ? -1 : left.path > right.path ? 1 : 0));
5172:  return { isAvailable: true, findings };
5173:}
5174:
5175:/**
5176: * Refuse a commit that stages a vendored change no overlay owns. Never writes.
5177: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
5178: * @returns {number} 0 to allow, 1 to refuse.
5179: */
5180:function commandPreCommit(ctx) {
5181:  const manifest = loadManifest(ctx);
5182:  if (!isStrictWrites(manifest)) {
5183:    console.log('allow: strictWrites is off in the overlay manifest, so nothing was checked');
5184:    return EXIT_OK;
5185:  }
5186:  const unowned = stagedUnownedChanges(ctx, manifest);
5187:  if (unowned.length === 0) {
5188:    console.log('allow');
5189:    return EXIT_OK;
5190:  }
5191:  for (const change of unowned) {
5192:    console.log(`refuse: staged ${change.status === 'D' ? 'deletion of' : 'change to'} ${change.key} is under a vendored root and no overlay owns it`);
5193:  }
5194:  console.log(`  bring each path under an overlay with ${ENGINE_COMMAND} apply --adopt <path>, add a generator's output to ` +
5195:    'the manifest\'s generated list, or unstage it');
5196:  return EXIT_CONFLICT;
5197:}

5199:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
5200:// 18. RELAYOUT
5201:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
5202:
5203:/**
5204: * Resolve one path the manifest names inside the overlay folder, refusing any
5205: * that leaves it.
5206: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
5207: * @param {string} value Path as the manifest writes it.
5208: * @returns {{absolute: string, relative: string}|{problem: string}} The resolved
5209: *   path, or why it cannot be used.
5210: */
5211:function containedOverlayPath(ctx, value) {
5212:  const absolute = path.resolve(ctx.overlayRoot, value);
5213:  try {
5214:    return { absolute, relative: sourceRelative(ctx, absolute) };
5215:  } catch (error) {
5216:    return { problem: error.message };
5217:  }
5218:}
5219:
5220:/**
5221: * Group moved fields by the copy each one names, so a copy two fields name
5222: * moves once.
5223: * @param {object[]} moves Records from layoutMoves.
5224: * @returns {object[]} One group per source path, carrying the fields naming it.
5225: */
5226:function groupLayoutMoves(moves) {
5227:  const groups = new Map();
5228:  for (const move of moves) {
5229:    const group = groups.get(move.from);
5230:    if (!group) {
5231:      groups.set(move.from, { from: move.from, to: move.to, records: [move] });
5232:      continue;
5233:    }
5234:    group.records.push(move);
5235:    // Two fields naming the same copy belong at the same layout path, so a
5236:    // second destination for it means the manifest disagrees with itself.
5237:    if (group.to !== move.to) group.clashes = [...(group.clashes || []), move.to];
5238:  }
5239:  return [...groups.values()];
5240:}
5241:
5242:/**
5243: * Resolve every move into filesystem paths and collect what stops the run.
5244: * Nothing is written here, so a refusal leaves the tree as it stood.
5245: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
5246: * @param {object[]} groups Groups from groupLayoutMoves.
5247: * @returns {{problems: string[], moves: object[], skipped: string[]}}
5248: */
5249:function planRelayout(ctx, groups) {
5250:  const problems = [];
5251:  const moves = [];
5252:  const skipped = [];
5253:  const claimed = new Map();
5254:  for (const group of groups) {
5255:    if (group.clashes) {
5256:      const destinations = [group.to, ...group.clashes].join(' and ');
5257:      problems.push(`refuse: ${group.from} is claimed by ${destinations}; one copy cannot move to both`);
5258:      continue;
5259:    }
5260:    const from = containedOverlayPath(ctx, group.from);
5261:    const to = containedOverlayPath(ctx, group.to);
5262:    if (from.problem || to.problem) {
5263:      problems.push('refuse: ' + (from.problem || to.problem));
5264:      continue;
5265:    }
5266:    if (pathExists(to.absolute)) {
5267:      problems.push(`refuse: ${to.relative} already exists in ${ctx.overlayLabel}`);
5268:      continue;
5269:    }
5270:    if (!pathExists(from.absolute)) {
5271:      problems.push(`refuse: ${from.relative} does not exist in ${ctx.overlayLabel}`);
5272:      continue;
5273:    }
5274:    // Moving a folder carries the copies inside it, so a second move out of that
5275:    // folder would find its own path gone half-way through the run.
5276:    const nested = moves.find((move) => isWithin(move.from.absolute, from.absolute) ||
5277:      isWithin(from.absolute, move.from.absolute));
5278:    if (nested) {
5279:      problems.push(`refuse: ${nested.from.relative} and ${from.relative} sit one inside the other; ` +
5280:        'they have to move in separate runs');
5281:      continue;
5282:    }
5283:    if (claimed.has(to.relative)) {
5284:      problems.push(`refuse: ${claimed.get(to.relative)} and ${from.relative} both belong at ${to.relative}`);
5285:      continue;
5286:    }
5287:    claimed.set(to.relative, from.relative);
5288:    // A credential file is never opened, so it cannot be backed up, and a copy
5289:    // that cannot be backed up is left where it is.
5290:    if (isEnvName(from.absolute)) {
5291:      skipped.push(from.relative);
5292:      continue;
5293:    }
5294:    moves.push({ from, to, records: group.records });
5295:  }
5296:  return { problems, moves, skipped };
5297:}
5298:
5299:/**
5300: * Copy one path into a backup folder, keeping its shape and never opening a
5301: * credential file. A backup is a second copy of the tree, so it holds only what
5302: * apply itself may read: a token-named path no manifest entry names exactly is
5303: * skipped rather than duplicated beside the source.
5304: * @param {string} sourcePath Path to copy.
5305: * @param {string} targetPath Where the copy goes.
5306: * @param {string} relativePath Position of the copy, for the `skipped` line.
5307: * @param {Set<string>} exact Absolute paths a manifest entry names exactly.
5308: */
5309:function copyForBackup(sourcePath, targetPath, relativePath, exact) {
5310:  let stats;
5311:  try {
5312:    stats = fs.lstatSync(sourcePath);
5313:  } catch (error) {
5314:    if (error && error.code === 'ENOENT') return;
5315:    throw error;
5316:  }
5317:  if (stats.isSymbolicLink()) {
5318:    // A link is restored as a link: following it would copy bytes belonging to
5319:    // whatever it points at instead of the shape the folder had.
5320:    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(targetPath), { recursive: true });
5321:    fs.symlinkSync(fs.readlinkSync(sourcePath), targetPath);
5322:    return;
5323:  }
5324:  if (stats.isDirectory()) {
5325:    fs.mkdirSync(targetPath, { recursive: true });
5326:    for (const name of fs.readdirSync(sourcePath).sort()) {
5327:      copyForBackup(path.join(sourcePath, name), path.join(targetPath, name), relativePath + '/' + name, exact);
5328:    }
5329:    return;
5330:  }
5331:  if (isProtectedReadPath(sourcePath, exact)) {
5332:    console.log(`skipped ${relativePath}`);
5333:    return;
5334:  }
5335:  writeFile(targetPath, fs.readFileSync(sourcePath), stats.mode & 0o777);
5336:}
5337:
5338:/**
5339: * Every layout link whose target reached through a copy about to move.
5340: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
5341: * @param {object[]} moves Groups the run is about to move.
5342: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
5343: * @returns {{path: string, from: string, target: string}[]} The link path, the
5344: *   target it names now and the target holding the same bytes afterwards.
5345: */
5346:function relayoutLinkTargets(ctx, moves, manifest) {
5347:  const changes = [];
5348:  for (const link of manifest.layoutLinks || []) {
5349:    if (!link || typeof link.path !== 'string' || typeof link.target !== 'string') continue;
5350:    const linkDir = path.dirname(path.join(ctx.root, link.path));
5351:    const reached = path.resolve(linkDir, link.target);
5352:    const move = moves.find((candidate) => isWithin(candidate.from.absolute, reached));
5353:    if (!move) continue;
5354:    const below = path.relative(move.from.absolute, reached);
5355:    const landed = below === '' ? move.to.absolute : path.join(move.to.absolute, below);
5356:    const target = toPosix(path.relative(linkDir, landed));
5357:    if (target === '' || target === link.target) continue;
5358:    changes.push({ path: link.path, from: link.target, target });
5359:  }
5360:  return changes;
5361:}
5362:
5363:/**
5364: * Build the whole manifest text a relayout writes, without writing it. Every
5365: * check that can refuse the run runs here, so a manifest this run cannot
5366: * rewrite stops it while every copy is still at the path that manifest names.
5367: * Only the changed string values are replaced, so the rest keeps its bytes.
5368: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
5369: * @param {object[]} fieldEdits The fields of the copies about to move.
5370: * @param {object[]} linkEdits Changes from relayoutLinkTargets.
5371: * @returns {string|null} The new manifest text, or null when no field changed.
5372: */
5373:function planRelayoutManifest(ctx, fieldEdits, linkEdits) {
5374:  const text = fs.readFileSync(ctx.manifestPath, 'utf8');
5375:  const document = parseJsonSource(text);
5376:  const edits = [];
5377:  if (fieldEdits.length > 0) {
5378:    const entries = new Map();
5379:    for (const node of nodeAtPath(document, ['overlays']).items) {
5380:      const id = node.type === 'object' ? findMember(node, 'id') : undefined;
5381:      if (id && id.value.type === 'string') entries.set(id.value.value, node);
5382:    }
5383:    for (const change of fieldEdits) {
5384:      const entry = entries.get(change.overlay);
5385:      const member = entry && findMember(entry, change.field);
5386:      // The manifest was read before the moves, so a field that no longer holds
5387:      // the path this run moved is a different file from the one planned against.
5388:      if (!member || member.value.value !== change.from) {
5389:        throw new Error(`overlay ${change.overlay} no longer keeps ${change.from} in ${change.field}`);
5390:      }
5391:      edits.push({ start: member.value.start, end: member.value.end, text: JSON.stringify(change.to) });
5392:    }
5393:  }
5394:  if (linkEdits.length > 0) {
5395:    const links = new Map();
5396:    for (const node of nodeAtPath(document, ['layoutLinks']).items) {
5397:      const linkPath = node.type === 'object' ? findMember(node, 'path') : undefined;
5398:      if (linkPath && linkPath.value.type === 'string') links.set(linkPath.value.value, node);
5399:    }
5400:    for (const change of linkEdits) {
5401:      const node = links.get(change.path);
5402:      const member = node && findMember(node, 'target');
5403:      if (!member || member.value.value !== change.from) {
5404:        throw new Error(`layout link ${change.path} no longer points at ${change.from}`);
5405:      }
5406:      edits.push({ start: member.value.start, end: member.value.end, text: JSON.stringify(change.target) });
5407:    }
5408:  }
5409:  if (edits.length === 0) return null;
5410:  return applyEdits(text, edits);
5411:}
5412:
5413:/**
5414: * Write the manifest text a relayout planned, once every copy has moved.
5415: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
5416: * @param {string|null} text Text from planRelayoutManifest.
5417: */
5418:function writeRelayoutManifest(ctx, text) {
5419:  if (text === null) return;
5420:  fs.writeFileSync(ctx.manifestPath, text);
5421:}
5422:
5423:/**
5424: * Point every live link whose target a relayout moved at the new path.
5425: * A state file written before links were recorded holds nothing to authorise a
5426: * retarget with, so the run that moves the bytes repairs the link itself. Any
5427: * other path standing at a link entry is left alone, for the apply that follows
5428: * to report, because this run never takes a path it did not put there.
5429: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
5430: * @param {{path: string, from: string, target: string}[]} changes Changes from
5431: *   relayoutLinkTargets.
5432: */
5433:function retargetRelayoutLinks(ctx, changes) {
5434:  for (const change of changes) {
5435:    const linkPath = path.join(ctx.root, change.path);
5436:    let stats = null;
5437:    try {
5438:      stats = fs.lstatSync(linkPath);
5439:    } catch (error) {
5440:      if (!error || error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
5441:    }
5442:    if (!stats || !stats.isSymbolicLink() || fs.readlinkSync(linkPath) !== change.from) continue;
5443:    fs.unlinkSync(linkPath);
5444:    ensureSymlink(linkPath, change.target);
5445:    console.log(`relinked ${change.path} -> ${change.target}`);
5446:  }
5447:}
5448:
5449:/**
5450: * Move every copy the manifest keeps off the layout onto its layout path.
5451: * The backup stays inside the overlay folder, so the old copy is recoverable
5452: * beside the manifest that named it.
5453: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
5454: * @param {object} manifest Loaded overlay manifest.
5455: * @returns {number} 0 when done or there was nothing to move, 2 on a refusal.
5456: */
5457:function relayoutUnderLock(ctx, manifest) {
5458:  const groups = groupLayoutMoves(layoutMoves(manifest));
5459:  if (groups.length === 0) {
5460:    console.log('0 moved');
5461:    return EXIT_OK;
5462:  }
5463:  const plan = planRelayout(ctx, groups);
5464:  if (plan.problems.length > 0) {
5465:    for (const problem of plan.problems) console.log(problem);
5466:    return EXIT_ERROR;
5467:  }
5468:  for (const relativePath of plan.skipped) console.log(`skipped ${relativePath}`);
5469:  if (plan.moves.length === 0) {
5470:    console.log('0 moved');
5471:    return EXIT_OK;
5472:  }
5473:
5474:  const fieldEdits = [];
5475:  for (const move of plan.moves) {
5476:    for (const record of move.records) {
5477:      fieldEdits.push({ overlay: record.overlay, field: record.field, from: record.from, to: record.to });
5478:    }
5479:  }
5480:  const linkEdits = relayoutLinkTargets(ctx, plan.moves, manifest);
5481:  // A manifest this run cannot rewrite has to refuse before the first rename:
5482:  // copies already at their layout paths behind a manifest that still names the
5483:  // old ones is a folder nobody can read, and no backup restores it on its own.
5484:  let manifestText;
5485:  try {
5486:    manifestText = planRelayoutManifest(ctx, fieldEdits, linkEdits);
5487:  } catch (error) {
5488:    console.log('refuse: ' + error.message);
5489:    return EXIT_ERROR;
5490:  }
5491:
5492:  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/:/g, '-');
5493:  const runDir = path.join(ctx.overlayRoot, RELAYOUT_RUNS_FOLDER, stamp);
5494:  const exact = exactManifestPaths(ctx, manifest);
5495:  for (const move of plan.moves) {
5496:    copyForBackup(move.from.absolute, path.join(runDir, relativeKey(ctx.root, move.from.absolute)), move.from.relative, exact);
5497:  }
5498:  copyForBackup(ctx.manifestPath, path.join(runDir, relativeKey(ctx.root, ctx.manifestPath)), relativeKey(ctx.root, ctx.manifestPath), exact);
5499:  console.log('backup ' + relativeKey(ctx.root, runDir));
5500:
5501:  for (const move of plan.moves) {
5502:    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(move.to.absolute), { recursive: true });
5503:    fs.renameSync(move.from.absolute, move.to.absolute);
5504:    pruneEmptyParents(ctx.overlayRoot, move.from.absolute);
5505:    console.log(`moved ${move.from.relative} -> ${move.to.relative}`);
5506:  }
5507:
5508:  writeRelayoutManifest(ctx, manifestText);
5509:  retargetRelayoutLinks(ctx, linkEdits);
5510:
5511:  console.log(`${plan.moves.length} moved`);
5512:  console.log(`run ${ENGINE_COMMAND} apply`);
5513:}
5514:
5515:/**
5516: * Move the off-layout copies a manifest keeps onto their layout paths.
5517: * @param {object} ctx Engine context.
5518: * @returns {number} 0 when done or there was nothing to move, 2 on a refusal.
5519: */
5520:function commandRelayout(ctx) {
5521:  return withLock(ctx, 'overlay-engine relayout', () => relayoutUnderLock(ctx, loadManifest(ctx)));
5522:}
5523:
5524:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
5525:// 19. COMMAND ENTRY POINT
5526:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
5527:
5528:/**
5529: * Parse the command line into one command and its options.
5530: * @param {string[]} argv Arguments after the script path.
5531: * @returns {object|null} Parsed options, or null for a misused command line.
5532: */
5533:function parseArguments(argv) {
5534:  const options = {
5535:    command: null, root: null, isJson: false, isAudit: false, isRelayout: false, capture: null, guard: null, adopt: null,
5536:    upstream: null, to: null, stage: null, rollback: null,
5537:  };
5538:  for (let index = 0; index < argv.length; index += 1) {
5539:    const value = argv[index];
5540:    if (value === '--help' || value === '-h') return { command: 'help' };
5541:    if (COMMAND_WORDS.includes(value)) {
5542:      if (options.command) return null;
5543:      options.command = value;
5544:      continue;
5545:    }
5546:    if (value === '--root' || value === '--capture' || value === '--adopt' || value === '--guard' ||
5547:        REPIN_VALUE_FLAGS.includes(value.slice(2))) {
5548:      const next = argv[index + 1];
5549:      const field = value.slice(2);
5550:      if (options[field] !== null || !next || next.startsWith('-')) return null;
5551:      options[field] = next;
5552:      index += 1;
5553:      continue;
5554:    }
5555:    if (value === '--json') {
5556:      options.isJson = true;
5557:      continue;
5558:    }
5559:    if (value === '--audit') {
5560:      options.isAudit = true;
5561:      continue;
5562:    }
5563:    if (value === '--relayout') {
5564:      options.isRelayout = true;
5565:      continue;
5566:    }
5567:    return null;
5568:  }
5569:  if (options.guard !== null) {
5570:    if (options.command || options.capture !== null || options.adopt !== null || options.isJson) return null;
5571:    options.command = 'guard';
5572:  }
5573:  if (!options.command) return null;
5574:  if (options.isAudit && options.command !== 'status') return null;
5575:  if (options.capture !== null && options.command !== 'apply') return null;
5576:  if (options.adopt !== null && (options.command !== 'apply' || options.capture !== null)) return null;
5577:  if (options.isRelayout && (options.command !== 'apply' || options.capture !== null || options.adopt !== null)) return null;
5578:  if (options.isJson && options.command !== 'status' && options.command !== 'repin') return null;
5579:  const repinFlags = REPIN_VALUE_FLAGS.filter((field) => options[field] !== null);
5580:  if (options.command !== 'repin') return repinFlags.length === 0 ? options : null;
5581:  if (options.rollback !== null) return repinFlags.length === 1 ? options : null;
5582:  return options.upstream !== null && options.to !== null ? options : null;
5583:}
5584:
5585:/**
5586: * Run the engine from command-line arguments.
5587: * @param {string[]} argv Arguments after the script path.
5588: * @returns {number} Exit code.
5589: */
5590:function main(argv) {
5591:  const options = parseArguments(argv);
5592:  if (!options) {
5593:    console.log(USAGE);
5594:    return EXIT_ERROR;
5595:  }
5596:  if (options.command === 'help') {
5597:    console.log(USAGE);
5598:    return EXIT_OK;
5599:  }
5600:  const root = path.resolve(options.root || DEFAULT_ROOT);
5601:  if (!isDirectory(root)) {
5602:    console.log('root is not a directory: ' + root);
5603:    return EXIT_ERROR;
5604:  }
5605:  try {
5606:    const ctx = createContext(root);
5607:    if (options.command === 'guard') return commandGuard(ctx, options.guard);
5608:    if (options.command === 'status') return commandStatus(ctx, { isJson: options.isJson, isAudit: options.isAudit });
5609:    if (options.command === 'repin') return commandRepin(ctx, options);
5610:    if (options.command === 'pre-commit') return commandPreCommit(ctx);
5611:    if (options.command === 'apply' && options.isRelayout) return commandRelayout(ctx);
5612:    if (options.capture !== null) return commandCapture(ctx, options.capture);
5613:    if (options.adopt !== null) return commandAdopt(ctx, options.adopt);
5614:    return commandApply(ctx);
5615:  } catch (error) {
5616:    // A JSON caller parses stdout, so the error has to arrive there as JSON too.
5617:    if (options.isJson) console.log(JSON.stringify({ result: 'ERROR', error: error.message }, null, 2));
5618:    else console.error('[overlay-engine] ' + error.message);
5619:    return EXIT_ERROR;
5620:  }
5621:}
5622:
5623:if (require.main === module) {
5624:  process.exitCode = main(process.argv.slice(2));
5625:}
